21:       - name: Set up Go

23:         with:

25:           cache: true
26:       - name: Cache Go modules
27:         uses: actions/cache@v4
28:         with:
29:           path: ~/go/pkg/mod

32:             ${{ runner.os }}-go-
33:       - name: Download dependencies
34:         run: go mod download
35:       - name: Run tests
36:         run: make test-race
37:       - name: Run tests with coverage
38:         if: matrix.go-version == '1.23'
39:         run: make test-coverage
40:       - name: Upload coverage to Codecov
41:         if: matrix.go-version == '1.23'

44:           file: ./coverage.out
45:           flags: unittests
46:           fail_ci_if_error: false

48:     name: Lint

32:   release-linux:
33:     name: Release (Linux)
34:     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
35:     needs: release-darwin
36:     steps:
37:       - name: Checkout code
38:         uses: actions/checkout@v4
39:         with:
40:           fetch-depth: 0
41:       - name: Set up Go
42:         uses: actions/setup-go@v5
43:         with:
44:           go-version: '1.24'
45:           cache: true
46:       - name: Install cross-compiler for ARM64
47:         run: |
48:           sudo apt-get update
49:           sudo apt-get install -y gcc-aarch64-linux-gnu
50:       - name: Build Linux binaries
51:         run: |
52:           # Build linux/amd64
53:           CGO_ENABLED=1 go build -ldflags="-s -w -X main.version=${GITHUB_REF_NAME#v} -X main.commit=${GITHUB_SHA} -X main.date=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)" -tags sqlite_omit_load_extension -o bbs-linux-amd64 ./cmd/bbs
54:           # Build linux/arm64
55:           CGO_ENABLED=1 CC=aarch64-linux-gnu-gcc GOOS=linux GOARCH=arm64 go build -ldflags="-s -w -X main.version=${GITHUB_REF_NAME#v} -X main.commit=${GITHUB_SHA} -X main.date=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)" -tags sqlite_omit_load_extension -o bbs-linux-arm64 ./cmd/bbs
56:       - name: Create archives
57:         run: |
58:           VERSION=${GITHUB_REF_NAME#v}
59:           # Create amd64 archive
60:           mkdir -p bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_x86_64
61:           cp bbs-linux-amd64 bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_x86_64/bbs
62:           cp README.md bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_x86_64/ 2>/dev/null || true
63:           tar -czvf bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_x86_64.tar.gz bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_x86_64
64:           # Create arm64 archive
65:           mkdir -p bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_arm64
66:           cp bbs-linux-arm64 bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_arm64/bbs
67:           cp README.md bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_arm64/ 2>/dev/null || true
68:           tar -czvf bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_arm64.tar.gz bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_arm64
69:       - name: Upload to release
70:         env:
71:           GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
72:         run: |
73:           VERSION=${GITHUB_REF_NAME#v}
74:           gh release upload ${GITHUB_REF_NAME} \
75:             bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_x86_64.tar.gz \
76:             bbs_${VERSION}_Linux_arm64.tar.gz \
77:             --clobber

<file path="cmd/bbs/skill/SKILL.md">
 1: ---
 2: name: bbs
 3: description: Bulletin board for threaded discussions - create topics, threads, and messages. Use for Q&A, discussions, or leaving notes for other agents/humans.
 4: ---
 5:
 6: # bbs - Bulletin Board System
 7:
 8: Threaded message board for humans and AI agents. Topics contain threads, threads contain messages.
 9:
10: ## When to use bbs
11:
12: - User wants to start a discussion or Q&A thread
13: - User asks about ongoing conversations
14: - Agent needs to leave notes for other agents or humans
15: - Collaborative async communication
16:
17: ## Available MCP tools
18:
19: | Tool | Purpose |
20: |------|---------|
21: | `mcp__bbs__list_topics` | List all topics |
22: | `mcp__bbs__create_topic` | Create a new topic |
23: | `mcp__bbs__list_threads` | List threads in a topic |
24: | `mcp__bbs__create_thread` | Start a new thread |
25: | `mcp__bbs__list_messages` | Get messages in a thread |
26: | `mcp__bbs__post_message` | Reply to a thread |
27: | `mcp__bbs__edit_message` | Edit a message |
28: | `mcp__bbs__sticky_thread` | Pin/unpin a thread |
29: | `mcp__bbs__archive_topic` | Archive a topic |
30:
31: ## Common patterns
32:
33: ### Browse the board
34: ```
35: mcp__bbs__list_topics()
36: mcp__bbs__list_threads(topic="general")
37: mcp__bbs__list_messages(thread="thread-uuid")
38: ```
39:
40: ### Start a discussion
41: ```
42: mcp__bbs__create_thread(topic="questions", subject="How do we handle auth?", message="I'm thinking about JWT vs sessions...", agent_name="claude")
43: ```
44:
45: ### Reply to a thread
46: ```
47: mcp__bbs__post_message(thread="thread-uuid", content="Here's my take on this...", agent_name="claude")
48: ```
49:
50: ### Create a new topic
51: ```
52: mcp__bbs__create_topic(name="architecture", description="System design discussions")
53: ```
54:
55: ## Agent identity
56:
57: Always include `agent_name` when posting so messages are attributed correctly:
58: - `agent_name="claude"` for Claude
59: - `agent_name="harper"` for the human
60:
61: ## CLI commands (if MCP unavailable)
62:
63: ```bash
64: bbs topic list                    # List topics
65: bbs thread list --topic general   # Threads in topic
66: bbs post list --thread <id>       # Messages in thread
67: bbs thread new --topic general "Subject" --body "Content"
68: bbs post new --thread <id> "Reply content"
69: bbs export markdown               # Export all
70: ```
71:
72: ## Data location
73:
74: `~/.local/share/bbs/bbs.db` (SQLite, respects XDG_DATA_HOME)
</file>

<file path="cmd/bbs/skill_test.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: Tests for the install-skill command
  2: // ABOUTME: Verifies directory creation, file content, and overwrite scenarios
  3: package main
  4: import (
  5: 	"os"
  6: 	"path/filepath"
  7: 	"strings"
  8: 	"testing"
  9: )
 10: // TestSkillInstallCreatesDirectory verifies that installSkillTo creates the
 11: // skills directory structure when it doesn't exist.
 12: func TestSkillInstallCreatesDirectory(t *testing.T) {
 13: 	tempDir := t.TempDir()
 14: 	skillDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "skills", "bbs")
 15: 	skillPath := filepath.Join(skillDir, "SKILL.md")
 16: 	// Directory should not exist initially
 17: 	if _, err := os.Stat(skillDir); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
 18: 		t.Fatalf("expected skill directory to not exist initially")
 19: 	}
 20: 	// Install the skill
 21: 	err := installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath)
 22: 	if err != nil {
 23: 		t.Fatalf("installSkillTo failed: %v", err)
 24: 	}
 25: 	// Directory should now exist
 26: 	info, err := os.Stat(skillDir)
 27: 	if err != nil {
 28: 		t.Fatalf("skill directory was not created: %v", err)
 29: 	}
 30: 	if !info.IsDir() {
 31: 		t.Fatalf("expected %s to be a directory", skillDir)
 32: 	}
 33: }
 34: // TestSkillInstallWritesFile verifies that the SKILL.md file is written
 35: // with correct content.
 36: func TestSkillInstallWritesFile(t *testing.T) {
 37: 	tempDir := t.TempDir()
 38: 	skillDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "skills", "bbs")
 39: 	skillPath := filepath.Join(skillDir, "SKILL.md")
 40: 	err := installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath)
 41: 	if err != nil {
 42: 		t.Fatalf("installSkillTo failed: %v", err)
 43: 	}
 44: 	// File should exist
 45: 	content, err := os.ReadFile(skillPath)
 46: 	if err != nil {
 47: 		t.Fatalf("failed to read skill file: %v", err)
 48: 	}
 49: 	// Verify content matches embedded file
 50: 	embeddedContent, err := skillFS.ReadFile("skill/SKILL.md")
 51: 	if err != nil {
 52: 		t.Fatalf("failed to read embedded skill: %v", err)
 53: 	}
 54: 	if string(content) != string(embeddedContent) {
 55: 		t.Errorf("installed content does not match embedded content")
 56: 	}
 57: }
 58: // TestSkillInstallFileContent verifies that the installed file contains
 59: // expected content sections.
 60: func TestSkillInstallFileContent(t *testing.T) {
 61: 	tempDir := t.TempDir()
 62: 	skillDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "skills", "bbs")
 63: 	skillPath := filepath.Join(skillDir, "SKILL.md")
 64: 	err := installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath)
 65: 	if err != nil {
 66: 		t.Fatalf("installSkillTo failed: %v", err)
 67: 	}
 68: 	content, err := os.ReadFile(skillPath)
 69: 	if err != nil {
 70: 		t.Fatalf("failed to read skill file: %v", err)
 71: 	}
 72: 	contentStr := string(content)
 73: 	// Check for expected content sections
 74: 	expectedSections := []string{
 75: 		"name: bbs",
 76: 		"description:",
 77: 		"# bbs - Bulletin Board System",
 78: 		"mcp__bbs__list_topics",
 79: 		"mcp__bbs__create_topic",
 80: 		"mcp__bbs__post_message",
 81: 	}
 82: 	for _, section := range expectedSections {
 83: 		if !strings.Contains(contentStr, section) {
 84: 			t.Errorf("expected skill content to contain %q", section)
 85: 		}
 86: 	}
 87: }
 88: // TestSkillInstallOverwritesExistingFile verifies that installing over an
 89: // existing file replaces its content.
 90: func TestSkillInstallOverwritesExistingFile(t *testing.T) {
 91: 	tempDir := t.TempDir()
 92: 	skillDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "skills", "bbs")
 93: 	skillPath := filepath.Join(skillDir, "SKILL.md")
 94: 	// Create directory and existing file with different content
 95: 	if err := os.MkdirAll(skillDir, 0755); err != nil {
 96: 		t.Fatalf("failed to create directory: %v", err)
 97: 	}
 98: 	oldContent := []byte("old content that should be replaced")
 99: 	if err := os.WriteFile(skillPath, oldContent, 0644); err != nil {
100: 		t.Fatalf("failed to write old file: %v", err)
101: 	}
102: 	// Verify old content exists
103: 	readContent, err := os.ReadFile(skillPath)
104: 	if err != nil {
105: 		t.Fatalf("failed to read old file: %v", err)
106: 	}
107: 	if string(readContent) != string(oldContent) {
108: 		t.Fatalf("old content was not written correctly")
109: 	}
110: 	// Install skill (should overwrite)
111: 	err = installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath)
112: 	if err != nil {
113: 		t.Fatalf("installSkillTo failed: %v", err)
114: 	}
115: 	// Verify content was replaced
116: 	newContent, err := os.ReadFile(skillPath)
117: 	if err != nil {
118: 		t.Fatalf("failed to read new file: %v", err)
119: 	}
120: 	if string(newContent) == string(oldContent) {
121: 		t.Errorf("file content was not overwritten")
122: 	}
123: 	// Verify it contains expected bbs content
124: 	if !strings.Contains(string(newContent), "bbs - Bulletin Board System") {
125: 		t.Errorf("overwritten file does not contain expected content")
126: 	}
127: }
128: // TestSkillInstallFilePermissions verifies that installed files have
129: // correct permissions.
130: func TestSkillInstallFilePermissions(t *testing.T) {
131: 	tempDir := t.TempDir()
132: 	skillDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "skills", "bbs")
133: 	skillPath := filepath.Join(skillDir, "SKILL.md")
134: 	err := installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath)
135: 	if err != nil {
136: 		t.Fatalf("installSkillTo failed: %v", err)
137: 	}
138: 	// Check directory permissions
139: 	dirInfo, err := os.Stat(skillDir)
140: 	if err != nil {
141: 		t.Fatalf("failed to stat skill directory: %v", err)
142: 	}
143: 	dirPerm := dirInfo.Mode().Perm()
144: 	if dirPerm != 0755 {
145: 		t.Errorf("expected directory permissions 0755, got %o", dirPerm)
146: 	}
147: 	// Check file permissions
148: 	fileInfo, err := os.Stat(skillPath)
149: 	if err != nil {
150: 		t.Fatalf("failed to stat skill file: %v", err)
151: 	}
152: 	filePerm := fileInfo.Mode().Perm()
153: 	if filePerm != 0644 {
154: 		t.Errorf("expected file permissions 0644, got %o", filePerm)
155: 	}
156: }
157: // TestSkillInstallNestedDirectoryCreation verifies that deeply nested
158: // directories are created correctly.
159: func TestSkillInstallNestedDirectoryCreation(t *testing.T) {
160: 	tempDir := t.TempDir()
161: 	// Create a deeply nested path
162: 	skillDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, "a", "b", "c", ".claude", "skills", "bbs")
163: 	skillPath := filepath.Join(skillDir, "SKILL.md")
164: 	// None of the intermediate directories should exist
165: 	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(tempDir, "a")); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
166: 		t.Fatalf("expected intermediate directory to not exist")
167: 	}
168: 	err := installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath)
169: 	if err != nil {
170: 		t.Fatalf("installSkillTo failed: %v", err)
171: 	}
172: 	// All directories should now exist
173: 	if _, err := os.Stat(skillDir); err != nil {
174: 		t.Fatalf("skill directory was not created: %v", err)
175: 	}
176: 	// File should exist and be readable
177: 	if _, err := os.ReadFile(skillPath); err != nil {
178: 		t.Fatalf("skill file not readable: %v", err)
179: 	}
180: }
181: // TestSkillInstallIdempotent verifies that installing multiple times
182: // produces the same result.
183: func TestSkillInstallIdempotent(t *testing.T) {
184: 	tempDir := t.TempDir()
185: 	skillDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "skills", "bbs")
186: 	skillPath := filepath.Join(skillDir, "SKILL.md")
187: 	// Install first time
188: 	err := installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath)
189: 	if err != nil {
190: 		t.Fatalf("first installSkillTo failed: %v", err)
191: 	}
192: 	firstContent, err := os.ReadFile(skillPath)
193: 	if err != nil {
194: 		t.Fatalf("failed to read first install: %v", err)
195: 	}
196: 	// Install second time
197: 	err = installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath)
198: 	if err != nil {
199: 		t.Fatalf("second installSkillTo failed: %v", err)
200: 	}
201: 	secondContent, err := os.ReadFile(skillPath)
202: 	if err != nil {
203: 		t.Fatalf("failed to read second install: %v", err)
204: 	}
205: 	// Content should be identical
206: 	if string(firstContent) != string(secondContent) {
207: 		t.Errorf("content differs between installs")
208: 	}
209: }
210: // installSkillTo is a testable version of installSkill that accepts
211: // custom paths instead of using os.UserHomeDir.
212: func installSkillTo(skillDir, skillPath string) error {
213: 	// Read embedded skill file
214: 	content, err := skillFS.ReadFile("skill/SKILL.md")
215: 	if err != nil {
216: 		return err
217: 	}
218: 	// Create directory
219: 	if err := os.MkdirAll(skillDir, 0755); err != nil {
220: 		return err
221: 	}
222: 	// Write skill file
223: 	if err := os.WriteFile(skillPath, content, 0644); err != nil {
224: 		return err
225: 	}
226: 	return nil
227: }
</file>

<file path="cmd/bbs/version.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Version command
 2: // ABOUTME: Displays build version, commit, and date
 3: package main
 4: import (
 5: 	"fmt"
 6: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
 7: )
 8: var versionCmd = &cobra.Command{
 9: 	Use:   "version",
10: 	Short: "Print version information",
11: 	Run: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) {
12: 		fmt.Printf("bbs %s\n", version)
13: 		fmt.Printf("  commit: %s\n", commit)
14: 		fmt.Printf("  built:  %s\n", date)
15: 	},
16: }
17: func init() {
18: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(versionCmd)
19: }
</file>

<file path="docs/plans/2025-12-15-bbs-design.md">
  1: # BBS Design Document
  2:
  3: A lightweight message board for humans and agents to communicate asynchronously.
  4:
  5: ## Overview
  6:
  7: BBS is a classic forum-style message board with:
  8: - **Topics** (boards) - Named permanent spaces anyone can create
  9: - **Threads** - Discussions within topics
 10: - **Messages** - Markdown content with optional attachments
 11:
 12: Designed for agent-to-agent and human-to-agent coordination.
 13:
 14: ## Data Model
 15:
 16: ```
 17: Topic
 18: ├── ID: UUID
 19: ├── Name: string (unique)
 20: ├── Description: string (optional)
 21: ├── CreatedAt: time.Time
 22: ├── CreatedBy: string (username@source)
 23: └── Archived: bool
 24:
 25: Thread
 26: ├── ID: UUID
 27: ├── TopicID: UUID (foreign key)
 28: ├── Subject: string
 29: ├── CreatedAt: time.Time
 30: ├── CreatedBy: string
 31: └── Sticky: bool (pinned to top)
 32:
 33: Message
 34: ├── ID: UUID
 35: ├── ThreadID: UUID (foreign key)
 36: ├── Content: string (markdown)
 37: ├── CreatedAt: time.Time
 38: ├── CreatedBy: string
 39: └── EditedAt: *time.Time (optional)
 40:
 41: Attachment
 42: ├── ID: UUID
 43: ├── MessageID: UUID (foreign key)
 44: ├── Filename: string
 45: ├── MimeType: string
 46: ├── Data: BLOB
 47: └── CreatedAt: time.Time
 48: ```
 49:
 50: ### Storage
 51:
 52: - **App database**: `~/.local/share/bbs/bbs.db`
 53: - **Vault queue**: `~/.config/bbs/vault.db`
 54: - **Driver**: `modernc.org/sqlite` (pure Go, no CGO)
 55: - **Cascade deletes**: Topic → Threads → Messages → Attachments
 56:
 57: ### Identity
 58:
 59: Format: `username@source`
 60:
 61: Sources:
 62: - `cli` - Command line interface
 63: - `tui` - Terminal UI
 64: - `mcp` - MCP server (agents)
 65:
 66: Default: `$USER@{source}`, overridable via `--as` flag or `BBS_USER` env var.
 67:
 68: ## CLI Commands
 69:
 70: ```bash
 71: # Topics
 72: bbs topic list                     # List all topics (with thread counts)
 73: bbs topic new "agent-tasks"        # Create topic
 74: bbs topic archive <id>             # Archive topic
 75: bbs topic show <id>                # Show topic + recent threads
 76:
 77: # Threads
 78: bbs thread list <topic>            # List threads in topic
 79: bbs thread new <topic> "Subject"   # Create thread (opens editor)
 80: bbs thread show <id>               # Show thread with messages
 81: bbs thread sticky <id>             # Pin/unpin thread
 82:
 83: # Messages
 84: bbs post <thread> "message"        # Quick post
 85: bbs post <thread> --file img.png   # Post with attachment
 86: bbs edit <message-id>              # Edit your message
 87:
 88: # Identity
 89: bbs whoami                         # Show current identity
 90: bbs --as "botname" post ...        # Override username
 91:
 92: # Sync
 93: bbs sync init                      # Initialize vault with device ID
 94: bbs sync login                     # Authenticate + derive keys
 95: bbs sync status                    # Show sync state
 96: bbs sync now                       # Manual push/pull
 97: bbs sync logout                    # Clear tokens
 98:
 99: # Interactive
100: bbs                                # Launch TUI (no subcommand)
101: bbs mcp                            # Start MCP server
102: ```
103:
104: UUID prefix matching: minimum 6 characters, error on ambiguity.
105:
106: ## MCP Server
107:
108: ### Tools (10)
109:
110: | Tool | Description |
111: |------|-------------|
112: | `list_topics` | List all topics (filter: archived) |
113: | `create_topic` | Create new topic |
114: | `archive_topic` | Archive/unarchive topic |
115: | `list_threads` | List threads in topic (filter: sticky) |
116: | `create_thread` | Create thread with initial message |
117: | `sticky_thread` | Pin/unpin thread |
118: | `list_messages` | Get messages in thread (paginated) |
119: | `post_message` | Post to thread (optional attachment) |
120: | `edit_message` | Edit existing message |
121: | `get_attachment` | Retrieve attachment (base64) |
122:
123: ### Resources
124:
125: | URI | Description |
126: |-----|-------------|
127: | `bbs://topics` | All active topics |
128: | `bbs://topics/{id}/threads` | Threads in topic |
129: | `bbs://threads/{id}/messages` | Messages in thread |
130: | `bbs://recent` | Recent activity across all topics |
131:
132: ### Prompts
133:
134: | Name | Description |
135: |------|-------------|
136: | `post-update` | Post a status update to {topic} |
137: | `summarize-thread` | Summarize the discussion in {thread} |
138:
139: Agent identity: `{agent_name}@mcp` (defaults to "agent").
140:
141: ## TUI Interface
142:
143: Three-pane navigation built with Bubble Tea:
144:
145: ```
146: ┌─ Topics ─────────┬─ Threads ─────────────┬─ Messages ──────────────────┐
147: │ > agent-tasks    │ > Build failed #42    │ harper@cli · 2m ago         │
148: │   builds         │   New deployment      │ ─────────────────────────   │
149: │   general        │   Question about X    │ The build failed because    │
150: │   watercooler    │                       │ of missing dependency...    │
151: │                  │                       │                             │
152: │                  │                       │ claude@mcp · 1m ago         │
153: │                  │                       │ ─────────────────────────   │
154: │                  │                       │ I found the issue. The...   │
155: │                  │                       │                             │
156: │ [n]ew [a]rchive  │ [n]ew [s]ticky       │ [r]eply [e]dit              │
157: └──────────────────┴──────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┘
158: ```
159:
160: ### Navigation
161:
162: - `Tab` / `Shift+Tab` - Move between panes
163: - `j/k` or arrows - Move within list
164: - `Enter` - Select/drill down
165: - `Esc` - Back up / cancel
166: - `q` - Quit
167: - `r` - Refresh (also auto-refresh every 30s)
168:
169: ### Compose
170:
171: Full-screen markdown editor for new threads/replies:
172: - `Ctrl+S` - Post
173: - `Esc` - Cancel
174: - `a` - Attach file (file picker)
175:
176: ## Vault Sync
177:
178: Uses `suitesync/vault` package (`github.com/harperreed/sweet`) matching position's architecture.
179:
180: ### Storage
181:
182: ```
183: ~/.local/share/bbs/bbs.db    # App database
184: ~/.config/bbs/vault.db       # Encrypted change queue
185: ~/.config/bbs/sync.json      # Sync configuration
186: ```
187:
188: ### Sync Behavior
189:
190: - **AutoSync enabled by default**
191: - On write: Change queued → pushed immediately
192: - On launch: Pull remote changes
193: - Manual: `bbs sync now`
194: - **Non-blocking**: Local writes always succeed, sync failures warn only
195:
196: ### Change Entities
197:
198: ```go
199: Entity: "topic" | "thread" | "message" | "attachment"
200: Op: "upsert" | "delete"
201: Payload: JSON (encrypted with BIP39-derived keys)
202: ```
203:
204: ### Sync Config
205:
206: ```go
207: type Config struct {
208:     Server       string
209:     UserID       string
210:     Token        string
211:     RefreshToken string
212:     TokenExpires string
213:     DerivedKey   string // hex-encoded (never the mnemonic)
214:     DeviceID     string
215:     VaultDB      string
216:     AutoSync     bool   // default: true
217: }
218: ```
219:
220: ## Project Structure
221:
222: ```
223: bbs/
224: ├── cmd/bbs/
225: │   ├── main.go              # Entry point
226: │   ├── root.go              # Root command (launches TUI if no args)
227: │   ├── topic.go             # topic list/new/archive/show
228: │   ├── thread.go            # thread list/new/show/sticky
229: │   ├── post.go              # post/edit commands
230: │   ├── whoami.go            # identity command
231: │   ├── sync.go              # sync init/login/status/now/logout
232: │   └── mcp.go               # MCP server command
233: ├── internal/
234: │   ├── db/
235: │   │   ├── db.go            # Connection, migrations
236: │   │   ├── topics.go        # Topic CRUD
237: │   │   ├── threads.go       # Thread CRUD
238: │   │   ├── messages.go      # Message CRUD
239: │   │   └── attachments.go   # Attachment CRUD
240: │   ├── models/
241: │   │   └── models.go        # Topic, Thread, Message, Attachment
242: │   ├── mcp/
243: │   │   ├── server.go        # MCP server setup
244: │   │   ├── tools.go         # Tool implementations
245: │   │   ├── resources.go     # Resource handlers
246: │   │   └── prompts.go       # Prompt templates
247: │   ├── tui/
248: │   │   ├── app.go           # Main Bubble Tea model
249: │   │   ├── topics.go        # Topics pane
250: │   │   ├── threads.go       # Threads pane
251: │   │   ├── messages.go      # Messages pane
252: │   │   └── compose.go       # Message composer
253: │   ├── sync/
254: │   │   └── sync.go          # Syncer, applyChange(), queue helpers
255: │   ├── config/
256: │   │   └── config.go        # Sync config management
257: │   └── identity/
258: │       └── identity.go      # Username@source handling
259: ├── go.mod
260: ├── go.sum
261: └── CLAUDE.md
262: ```
263:
264: ## Dependencies
265:
266: | Package | Purpose |
267: |---------|---------|
268: | `spf13/cobra` | CLI framework |
269: | `modernc.org/sqlite` | Pure Go SQLite |
270: | `google/uuid` | UUID generation |
271: | `charmbracelet/bubbletea` | TUI framework |
272: | `charmbracelet/lipgloss` | TUI styling |
273: | `charmbracelet/bubbles` | TUI components |
274: | `modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk` | MCP server |
275: | `github.com/harperreed/sweet/suitesync/vault` | Vault sync |
276: | `fatih/color` | CLI colors |
277:
278: ## Design Decisions
279:
280: 1. **Message board style** - Async, persistent, easy to catch up on. Better for agent coordination than real-time chat.
281:
282: 2. **Two-level threading** - Topics → Threads → Messages (flat). Classic forum simplicity.
283:
284: 3. **Blob attachments** - Stored in SQLite for portability. Single file contains everything.
285:
286: 4. **Trust-based identity** - No authentication, just `username@source`. Appropriate for internal agent coordination.
287:
288: 5. **AutoSync by default** - Changes sync immediately. Local writes never blocked by sync failures.
289:
290: 6. **Full interactive TUI** - Browse and compose in one interface. Not just a viewer.
291:
292: 7. **Agents as first-class** - Full CRUD via MCP. Agents aren't second-class citizens.
</file>

155: // ABOUTME: Tests for BBS data models

219: **Step 3: Write models implementation**
220:
221: ```go
222: // ABOUTME: Core data models for topics, threads, messages, attachments
223: // ABOUTME: Provides constructor functions for each model type
224:
225: package models
226:
227: import (
228: 	"time"
229:
230: 	"github.com/google/uuid"
231: )
232:
233: // Topic represents a message board category.
234: type Topic struct {
235: 	ID          uuid.UUID
236: 	Name        string
237: 	Description string
238: 	CreatedAt   time.Time
239: 	CreatedBy   string
240: 	Archived    bool
241: }
242:
243: // Thread represents a discussion within a topic.
244: type Thread struct {
245: 	ID        uuid.UUID
246: 	TopicID   uuid.UUID
247: 	Subject   string
248: 	CreatedAt time.Time
249: 	CreatedBy string
250: 	Sticky    bool
251: }
252:
253: // Message represents a post within a thread.
254: type Message struct {
255: 	ID        uuid.UUID
256: 	ThreadID  uuid.UUID
257: 	Content   string
258: 	CreatedAt time.Time
259: 	CreatedBy string
260: 	EditedAt  *time.Time
261: }
262:
263: // Attachment represents a file attached to a message.
264: type Attachment struct {
265: 	ID        uuid.UUID
266: 	MessageID uuid.UUID
267: 	Filename  string
268: 	MimeType  string
269: 	Data      []byte
270: 	CreatedAt time.Time
271: }
272:
273: // NewTopic creates a new topic with generated UUID and timestamp.
274: func NewTopic(name, description, createdBy string) *Topic {
275: 	return &Topic{
276: 		ID:          uuid.New(),
277: 		Name:        name,
278: 		Description: description,
279: 		CreatedAt:   time.Now(),
280: 		CreatedBy:   createdBy,
281: 		Archived:    false,
282: 	}
283: }
284:
285: // NewThread creates a new thread with generated UUID and timestamp.
286: func NewThread(topicID uuid.UUID, subject, createdBy string) *Thread {
287: 	return &Thread{
288: 		ID:        uuid.New(),
289: 		TopicID:   topicID,
290: 		Subject:   subject,
291: 		CreatedAt: time.Now(),
292: 		CreatedBy: createdBy,
293: 		Sticky:    false,
294: 	}
295: }
296:
297: // NewMessage creates a new message with generated UUID and timestamp.
298: func NewMessage(threadID uuid.UUID, content, createdBy string) *Message {
299: 	return &Message{
300: 		ID:        uuid.New(),
301: 		ThreadID:  threadID,
302: 		Content:   content,
303: 		CreatedAt: time.Now(),
304: 		CreatedBy: createdBy,
305: 	}
306: }
307:
308: // NewAttachment creates a new attachment with generated UUID and timestamp.
309: func NewAttachment(messageID uuid.UUID, filename, mimeType string, data []byte) *Attachment {
310: 	return &Attachment{
311: 		ID:        uuid.New(),
312: 		MessageID: messageID,
313: 		Filename:  filename,
314: 		MimeType:  mimeType,
315: 		Data:      data,
316: 		CreatedAt: time.Now(),
317: 	}
318: }
319: ```
320:

322:

324: Expected: PASS

336:

342:
343: ```go
344: // ABOUTME: Tests for database initialization
345: // ABOUTME: Verifies connection and migration execution

348:

350: 	"os"
351: 	"path/filepath"

355: func TestInitDB(t *testing.T) {
356: 	// Use temp directory
357: 	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
358: 	dbPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "test.db")
359:
360: 	db, err := InitDB(dbPath)
361: 	if err != nil {

363: 	}
364: 	defer db.Close()
365:
366: 	// Verify tables exist
367: 	tables := []string{"topics", "threads", "messages", "attachments"}
368: 	for _, table := range tables {
369: 		var name string
370: 		err := db.QueryRow("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?", table).Scan(&name)
371: 		if err != nil {
372: 			t.Errorf("table %s not found: %v", table, err)
373: 		}
374: 	}
375: }
376:
377: func TestGetDefaultDBPath(t *testing.T) {
378: 	path := GetDefaultDBPath()
379: 	if path == "" {
380: 		t.Error("expected non-empty path")

382: 	if filepath.Base(path) != "bbs.db" {
383: 		t.Errorf("expected bbs.db, got %s", filepath.Base(path))
384: 	}
385: }
386: ```
387:
388: **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**
389:
390: Run: `go get modernc.org/sqlite && go test ./internal/db/...`
391: Expected: FAIL (db.go doesn't exist)
392:
393: **Step 3: Write db.go**
394:
395: ```go
396: // ABOUTME: Database connection management and initialization
397: // ABOUTME: Handles SQLite connection and schema creation
398:
399: package db
400:
401: import (
402: 	"database/sql"
403: 	"fmt"
404: 	"os"
405: 	"path/filepath"
406:
407: 	_ "modernc.org/sqlite"
408: )
409:
410: // InitDB initializes the database connection and creates schema.
411: func InitDB(dbPath string) (*sql.DB, error) {
412: 	// Ensure directory exists
413: 	dir := filepath.Dir(dbPath)
414: 	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0750); err != nil {
415: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create database directory: %w", err)
416: 	}
417:
418: 	// Open database connection
419: 	db, err := sql.Open("sqlite", dbPath)
420: 	if err != nil {
421: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open database: %w", err)
422: 	}
423:
424: 	// Enable foreign keys
425: 	if _, err := db.Exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON"); err != nil {
426: 		_ = db.Close()
427: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to enable foreign keys: %w", err)
428: 	}
429:
430: 	// Create schema
431: 	if err := createSchema(db); err != nil {
432: 		_ = db.Close()
433: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create schema: %w", err)
434: 	}
435:
436: 	return db, nil
437: }
438:
439: // GetDefaultDBPath returns the default database path following XDG standards.
440: func GetDefaultDBPath() string {
441: 	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
442: 	if err != nil {
443: 		homeDir = "."
444: 	}
445:
446: 	dataDir := os.Getenv("XDG_DATA_HOME")
447: 	if dataDir == "" {
448: 		dataDir = filepath.Join(homeDir, ".local", "share")
449: 	}
450:
451: 	return filepath.Join(dataDir, "bbs", "bbs.db")
452: }
453:
454: func createSchema(db *sql.DB) error {
455: 	schema := `
456: 	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS topics (
457: 		id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
458: 		name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
459: 		description TEXT DEFAULT '',
460: 		created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
461: 		created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
462: 		archived BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
463: 	);
464:
465: 	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS threads (
466: 		id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
467: 		topic_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES topics(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
468: 		subject TEXT NOT NULL,
469: 		created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
470: 		created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
471: 		sticky BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
472: 	);
473:
474: 	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
475: 		id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
476: 		thread_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES threads(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
477: 		content TEXT NOT NULL,
478: 		created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
479: 		created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
480: 		edited_at DATETIME
481: 	);
482:
483: 	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attachments (
484: 		id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
485: 		message_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES messages(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
486: 		filename TEXT NOT NULL,
487: 		mime_type TEXT NOT NULL,
488: 		data BLOB NOT NULL,
489: 		created_at DATETIME NOT NULL
490: 	);
491:
492: 	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_threads_topic ON threads(topic_id);
493: 	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_thread ON messages(thread_id);
494: 	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attachments_message ON attachments(message_id);
495: 	`
496:
497: 	_, err := db.Exec(schema)
498: 	return err
499: }
500: ```
501:
502: **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**
503:
504: Run: `go test ./internal/db/...`
505: Expected: PASS
506:
507: **Step 5: Commit**
508:
509: ```bash
510: git add internal/db/
511: git commit -m "feat: add database layer with schema creation"
512: ```
513:

522:
523: Replace the PersistentPreRunE in `cmd/bbs/root.go`:
524:
525: ```go
526: // ABOUTME: Root Cobra command and global flags
527: // ABOUTME: Sets up CLI structure and database connection
528:
529: package main
530:
531: import (
532: 	"database/sql"
533: 	"fmt"
534:
535: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
536: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
537: )
538:
539: var (
540: 	dbPath   string
541: 	dbConn   *sql.DB
542: 	identity string
543: )
544:
545: var rootCmd = &cobra.Command{
546: 	Use:   "bbs",
547: 	Short: "A lightweight message board for humans and agents",
548: 	Long: `
549: ██████╗ ██████╗ ███████╗
550: ██╔══██╗██╔══██╗██╔════╝
551: ██████╔╝██████╔╝███████╗
552: ██╔══██╗██╔══██╗╚════██║
553: ██████╔╝██████╔╝███████║
554: ╚═════╝ ╚═════╝ ╚══════╝
555:
556:    THUNDERBOARD 3000
557:
558: A message board for humans and agents to communicate.
559: Topics → Threads → Messages`,
560: 	PersistentPreRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
561: 		// Skip DB init for help commands
562: 		if cmd.Name() == "help" || cmd.Name() == "version" {
563: 			return nil
564: 		}
565:
566: 		// Use default path if not specified
567: 		path := dbPath
568: 		if path == "" {
569: 			path = db.GetDefaultDBPath()
570: 		}
571:
572: 		var err error
573: 		dbConn, err = db.InitDB(path)
574: 		if err != nil {
575: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize database: %w", err)
576: 		}
577: 		return nil
578: 	},
579: 	PersistentPostRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
580: 		if dbConn != nil {
581: 			return dbConn.Close()
582: 		}
583: 		return nil
584: 	},
585: }
586:
587: func init() {
588: 	defaultPath := db.GetDefaultDBPath()
589: 	rootCmd.PersistentFlags().StringVar(&dbPath, "db", defaultPath, "database file path")
590: 	rootCmd.PersistentFlags().StringVar(&identity, "as", "", "identity override (username)")
591: }
592: ```
593:

608: ## Task 6: Add Identity Helper
609:
610: **Files:**
611: - Create: `internal/identity/identity.go`
612: - Create: `internal/identity/identity_test.go`
613:
614: **Step 1: Write failing test**
615:
616: ```go
617: // ABOUTME: Tests for identity resolution
618: // ABOUTME: Verifies username@source format handling
619:
620: package identity
621:
622: import (
623: 	"os"
624: 	"testing"
625: )
626:
627: func TestGetIdentity(t *testing.T) {
628: 	tests := []struct {
629: 		name     string
630: 		override string
631: 		source   string
632: 		want     string
633: 	}{
634: 		{"with override", "mybot", "cli", "mybot@cli"},
635: 		{"without override", "", "mcp", os.Getenv("USER") + "@mcp"},
636: 	}
637:
638: 	for _, tt := range tests {
639: 		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
640: 			got := GetIdentity(tt.override, tt.source)
641: 			if got != tt.want {
642: 				t.Errorf("GetIdentity() = %v, want %v", got, tt.want)
643: 			}
644: 		})
645: 	}
646: }
647:
648: func TestParseIdentity(t *testing.T) {
649: 	user, source := ParseIdentity("harper@cli")
650: 	if user != "harper" {
651: 		t.Errorf("expected user 'harper', got '%s'", user)
652: 	}
653: 	if source != "cli" {
654: 		t.Errorf("expected source 'cli', got '%s'", source)
655: 	}
656: }
657: ```
658:
659: **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**
660:
661: Run: `go test ./internal/identity/...`
662: Expected: FAIL
663:
664: **Step 3: Write identity.go**
665:
666: ```go
667: // ABOUTME: Identity resolution for BBS users
668: // ABOUTME: Handles username@source format
669:
670: package identity
671:
672: import (
673: 	"os"
674: 	"strings"
675: )
676:
677: // GetIdentity returns the identity string for a user.
678: // If override is provided, uses that as username.
679: // Otherwise uses $USER or $BBS_USER environment variable.
680: func GetIdentity(override, source string) string {
681: 	username := override
682: 	if username == "" {
683: 		username = os.Getenv("BBS_USER")
684: 	}
685: 	if username == "" {
686: 		username = os.Getenv("USER")
687: 	}
688: 	if username == "" {
689: 		username = "anonymous"
690: 	}
691: 	return username + "@" + source
692: }
693:
694: // ParseIdentity splits an identity string into username and source.
695: func ParseIdentity(id string) (username, source string) {
696: 	parts := strings.SplitN(id, "@", 2)
697: 	if len(parts) == 2 {
698: 		return parts[0], parts[1]
699: 	}
700: 	return id, "unknown"
701: }
702: ```
703:
704: **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**
705:
706: Run: `go test ./internal/identity/...`
707: Expected: PASS
708:
709: **Step 5: Commit**
710:
711: ```bash
712: git add internal/identity/
713: git commit -m "feat: add identity helper for username@source format"
714: ```

  21: ```go
  22: // ABOUTME: Tests for topic database operations
  23: // ABOUTME: Verifies CRUD operations for topics
  24:
  25: package db
  26:
  27: import (
  28: 	"path/filepath"
  29: 	"testing"
  30:
  31: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"

  33:
  34: func TestCreateTopic(t *testing.T) {

  57: func TestListTopics(t *testing.T) {

  66: 	topic2 := models.NewTopic("builds", "Build logs", "harper@cli")
  67: 	CreateTopic(db, topic1)

  69:

  71: 	if err != nil {

  75: 		t.Errorf("expected 2 topics, got %d", len(topics))
  76: 	}
  77: }
  78:
  79: func TestArchiveTopic(t *testing.T) {
  80: 	db, err := InitDB(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "test.db"))
  81: 	if err != nil {
  82: 		t.Fatalf("InitDB failed: %v", err)
  83: 	}
  84: 	defer db.Close()
  85:
  86: 	topic := models.NewTopic("old-stuff", "Old", "harper@cli")
  87: 	CreateTopic(db, topic)
  88:

  90: 	if err != nil {

  92: 	}

  95: 	topics, _ := ListTopics(db, false)
  96: 	if len(topics) != 0 {
  97: 		t.Errorf("expected 0 non-archived topics, got %d", len(topics))
  98: 	}
  99:
 100: 	// Should appear in archived list
 101: 	topics, _ = ListTopics(db, true)
 102: 	if len(topics) != 1 {

 108: **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**
 109:
 110: Run: `go test ./internal/db/... -run TestCreateTopic`
 111: Expected: FAIL (undefined functions)
 112:
 113: **Step 3: Write topics.go**
 114:
 115: ```go
 116: // ABOUTME: Topic database operations
 117: // ABOUTME: CRUD functions for topics table
 118:
 119: package db
 120:
 121: import (
 122: 	"database/sql"
 123: 	"fmt"
 124: 	"strings"
 125:
 126: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 127: )
 128:
 129: // CreateTopic inserts a new topic into the database.
 130: func CreateTopic(db *sql.DB, topic *models.Topic) error {
 131: 	_, err := db.Exec(`
 132: 		INSERT INTO topics (id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived)
 133: 		VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
 134: 		topic.ID.String(), topic.Name, topic.Description,
 135: 		topic.CreatedAt, topic.CreatedBy, topic.Archived)
 136: 	return err
 137: }
 138:
 139: // GetTopicByID retrieves a topic by its UUID (supports prefix matching).
 140: func GetTopicByID(db *sql.DB, id string) (*models.Topic, error) {
 141: 	query := `SELECT id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived
 142: 			  FROM topics WHERE id = ? OR id LIKE ?`
 143: 	row := db.QueryRow(query, id, id+"%")
 144: 	return scanTopic(row)
 145: }
 146:
 147: // GetTopicByName retrieves a topic by its name.
 148: func GetTopicByName(db *sql.DB, name string) (*models.Topic, error) {
 149: 	query := `SELECT id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived
 150: 			  FROM topics WHERE name = ?`
 151: 	row := db.QueryRow(query, name)
 152: 	return scanTopic(row)
 153: }
 154:
 155: // ListTopics returns all topics, optionally filtering by archived status.
 156: func ListTopics(db *sql.DB, includeArchived bool) ([]*models.Topic, error) {
 157: 	query := `SELECT id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived
 158: 			  FROM topics`
 159: 	if !includeArchived {
 160: 		query += " WHERE archived = FALSE"
 161: 	}
 162: 	query += " ORDER BY name"
 163:
 164: 	rows, err := db.Query(query)
 165: 	if err != nil {
 166: 		return nil, err
 167: 	}
 168: 	defer rows.Close()
 169:
 170: 	var topics []*models.Topic
 171: 	for rows.Next() {
 172: 		topic, err := scanTopicFromRows(rows)
 173: 		if err != nil {
 174: 			return nil, err
 175: 		}
 176: 		topics = append(topics, topic)
 177: 	}
 178: 	return topics, rows.Err()
 179: }
 180:
 181: // ArchiveTopic sets the archived status of a topic.
 182: func ArchiveTopic(db *sql.DB, id string, archived bool) error {
 183: 	result, err := db.Exec(`UPDATE topics SET archived = ? WHERE id = ? OR id LIKE ?`,
 184: 		archived, id, id+"%")
 185: 	if err != nil {
 186: 		return err
 187: 	}
 188: 	rows, _ := result.RowsAffected()
 189: 	if rows == 0 {
 190: 		return fmt.Errorf("topic not found: %s", id)
 191: 	}
 192: 	return nil
 193: }
 194:
 195: // ResolveTopicID finds a topic by ID prefix or name, returning the full ID.
 196: func ResolveTopicID(db *sql.DB, idOrName string) (string, error) {
 197: 	// Try by name first
 198: 	topic, err := GetTopicByName(db, idOrName)
 199: 	if err == nil {
 200: 		return topic.ID.String(), nil
 201: 	}
 202:
 203: 	// Try by ID prefix
 204: 	topic, err = GetTopicByID(db, idOrName)
 205: 	if err != nil {
 206: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("topic not found: %s", idOrName)
 207: 	}
 208: 	return topic.ID.String(), nil
 209: }
 210:
 211: func scanTopic(row *sql.Row) (*models.Topic, error) {
 212: 	var topic models.Topic
 213: 	var id string
 214: 	err := row.Scan(&id, &topic.Name, &topic.Description,
 215: 		&topic.CreatedAt, &topic.CreatedBy, &topic.Archived)
 216: 	if err != nil {
 217: 		return nil, err
 218: 	}
 219: 	topic.ID, _ = parseUUID(id)
 220: 	return &topic, nil
 221: }
 222:
 223: func scanTopicFromRows(rows *sql.Rows) (*models.Topic, error) {
 224: 	var topic models.Topic
 225: 	var id string
 226: 	err := rows.Scan(&id, &topic.Name, &topic.Description,
 227: 		&topic.CreatedAt, &topic.CreatedBy, &topic.Archived)
 228: 	if err != nil {
 229: 		return nil, err
 230: 	}
 231: 	topic.ID, _ = parseUUID(id)
 232: 	return &topic, nil
 233: }
 234:
 235: func parseUUID(s string) (uuid models.UUID, err error) {
 236: 	// Simple wrapper to handle UUID parsing
 237: 	return models.ParseUUID(s)
 238: }
 239: ```
 240:
 241: **Step 4: Add UUID parsing to models**
 242:
 243: Add to `internal/models/models.go`:
 244:
 245: ```go
 246: import "github.com/google/uuid"
 247:
 248: type UUID = uuid.UUID
 249:
 250: // ParseUUID parses a string into a UUID.
 251: func ParseUUID(s string) (uuid.UUID, error) {
 252: 	return uuid.Parse(s)
 253: }
 254: ```
 255:
 256: **Step 5: Run test to verify it passes**
 257:
 258: Run: `go test ./internal/db/... -v`
 259: Expected: PASS
 260:
 261: **Step 6: Commit**
 262:
 263: ```bash
 264: git add internal/db/topics.go internal/db/topics_test.go internal/models/models.go
 265: git commit -m "feat: add topic database operations"
 266: ```

 270: ## Task 2: Thread Database Operations

 272: **Files:**

 276: **Step 1: Write failing test**

 279: // ABOUTME: Tests for thread database operations
 280: // ABOUTME: Verifies CRUD operations for threads
 281:

 283:
 284: import (
 285: 	"path/filepath"
 286: 	"testing"
 287:
 288: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 289: )
 290:
 291: func TestCreateThread(t *testing.T) {
 292: 	db, err := InitDB(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "test.db"))
 293: 	if err != nil {
 294: 		t.Fatalf("InitDB failed: %v", err)
 295: 	}
 296: 	defer db.Close()
 297:
 298: 	// Create topic first
 299: 	topic := models.NewTopic("general", "General", "harper@cli")
 300: 	CreateTopic(db, topic)
 301:
 302: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Hello World", "harper@cli")
 303: 	err = CreateThread(db, thread)
 304: 	if err != nil {
 305: 		t.Fatalf("CreateThread failed: %v", err)
 306: 	}
 307:
 308: 	got, err := GetThreadByID(db, thread.ID.String())
 309: 	if err != nil {

 311: 	}
 312: 	if got.Subject != "Hello World" {
 313: 		t.Errorf("expected subject 'Hello World', got '%s'", got.Subject)
 314: 	}
 315: }
 316:
 317: func TestListThreads(t *testing.T) {
 318: 	db, err := InitDB(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "test.db"))
 319: 	if err != nil {
 320: 		t.Fatalf("InitDB failed: %v", err)
 321: 	}
 322: 	defer db.Close()
 323:
 324: 	topic := models.NewTopic("general", "General", "harper@cli")
 325: 	CreateTopic(db, topic)
 326:
 327: 	thread1 := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "First", "harper@cli")
 328: 	thread2 := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Second", "harper@cli")
 329: 	CreateThread(db, thread1)
 330: 	CreateThread(db, thread2)
 331:
 332: 	threads, err := ListThreads(db, topic.ID.String())
 333: 	if err != nil {
 334: 		t.Fatalf("ListThreads failed: %v", err)
 335: 	}
 336: 	if len(threads) != 2 {
 337: 		t.Errorf("expected 2 threads, got %d", len(threads))
 338: 	}
 339: }
 340:
 341: func TestStickyThread(t *testing.T) {
 342: 	db, err := InitDB(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "test.db"))
 343: 	if err != nil {
 344: 		t.Fatalf("InitDB failed: %v", err)
 345: 	}
 346: 	defer db.Close()
 347:
 348: 	topic := models.NewTopic("general", "General", "harper@cli")
 349: 	CreateTopic(db, topic)
 350:
 351: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Important", "harper@cli")
 352: 	CreateThread(db, thread)
 353:
 354: 	err = SetThreadSticky(db, thread.ID.String(), true)
 355: 	if err != nil {
 356: 		t.Fatalf("SetThreadSticky failed: %v", err)
 357: 	}
 358:
 359: 	got, _ := GetThreadByID(db, thread.ID.String())
 360: 	if !got.Sticky {
 361: 		t.Error("expected thread to be sticky")
 362: 	}
 363: }
 364: ```
 365:
 366: **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**
 367:
 368: Run: `go test ./internal/db/... -run TestCreateThread`
 369: Expected: FAIL
 370:
 371: **Step 3: Write threads.go**
 372:
 373: ```go
 374: // ABOUTME: Thread database operations
 375: // ABOUTME: CRUD functions for threads table
 376:
 377: package db
 378:
 379: import (
 380: 	"database/sql"
 381: 	"fmt"
 382:
 383: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 384: )
 385:
 386: // CreateThread inserts a new thread into the database.
 387: func CreateThread(db *sql.DB, thread *models.Thread) error {
 388: 	_, err := db.Exec(`
 389: 		INSERT INTO threads (id, topic_id, subject, created_at, created_by, sticky)
 390: 		VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
 391: 		thread.ID.String(), thread.TopicID.String(), thread.Subject,
 392: 		thread.CreatedAt, thread.CreatedBy, thread.Sticky)
 393: 	return err
 394: }
 395:
 396: // GetThreadByID retrieves a thread by its UUID (supports prefix matching).
 397: func GetThreadByID(db *sql.DB, id string) (*models.Thread, error) {
 398: 	query := `SELECT id, topic_id, subject, created_at, created_by, sticky
 399: 			  FROM threads WHERE id = ? OR id LIKE ?`
 400: 	row := db.QueryRow(query, id, id+"%")
 401: 	return scanThread(row)
 402: }
 403:
 404: // ListThreads returns all threads in a topic, ordered by sticky then created_at.
 405: func ListThreads(db *sql.DB, topicID string) ([]*models.Thread, error) {
 406: 	query := `SELECT id, topic_id, subject, created_at, created_by, sticky
 407: 			  FROM threads WHERE topic_id = ? OR topic_id LIKE ?
 408: 			  ORDER BY sticky DESC, created_at DESC`
 409:
 410: 	rows, err := db.Query(query, topicID, topicID+"%")
 411: 	if err != nil {
 412: 		return nil, err
 413: 	}
 414: 	defer rows.Close()
 415:
 416: 	var threads []*models.Thread
 417: 	for rows.Next() {
 418: 		thread, err := scanThreadFromRows(rows)
 419: 		if err != nil {
 420: 			return nil, err
 421: 		}
 422: 		threads = append(threads, thread)
 423: 	}
 424: 	return threads, rows.Err()
 425: }
 426:
 427: // SetThreadSticky sets the sticky status of a thread.
 428: func SetThreadSticky(db *sql.DB, id string, sticky bool) error {
 429: 	result, err := db.Exec(`UPDATE threads SET sticky = ? WHERE id = ? OR id LIKE ?`,
 430: 		sticky, id, id+"%")
 431: 	if err != nil {
 432: 		return err
 433: 	}
 434: 	rows, _ := result.RowsAffected()
 435: 	if rows == 0 {
 436: 		return fmt.Errorf("thread not found: %s", id)
 437: 	}
 438: 	return nil
 439: }
 440:
 441: func scanThread(row *sql.Row) (*models.Thread, error) {
 442: 	var thread models.Thread
 443: 	var id, topicID string
 444: 	err := row.Scan(&id, &topicID, &thread.Subject,
 445: 		&thread.CreatedAt, &thread.CreatedBy, &thread.Sticky)
 446: 	if err != nil {
 447: 		return nil, err
 448: 	}
 449: 	thread.ID, _ = models.ParseUUID(id)
 450: 	thread.TopicID, _ = models.ParseUUID(topicID)
 451: 	return &thread, nil
 452: }
 453:
 454: func scanThreadFromRows(rows *sql.Rows) (*models.Thread, error) {
 455: 	var thread models.Thread
 456: 	var id, topicID string
 457: 	err := rows.Scan(&id, &topicID, &thread.Subject,
 458: 		&thread.CreatedAt, &thread.CreatedBy, &thread.Sticky)
 459: 	if err != nil {
 460: 		return nil, err
 461: 	}
 462: 	thread.ID, _ = models.ParseUUID(id)
 463: 	thread.TopicID, _ = models.ParseUUID(topicID)
 464: 	return &thread, nil
 465: }
 466: ```
 467:
 468: **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**
 469:
 470: Run: `go test ./internal/db/... -v`
 471: Expected: PASS

 477: git commit -m "feat: add thread database operations"
 478: ```

 557: func TestUpdateMessage(t *testing.T) {
 558: 	db, err := InitDB(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "test.db"))
 559: 	if err != nil {
 560: 		t.Fatalf("InitDB failed: %v", err)
 561: 	}

 563:
 564: 	topic := models.NewTopic("general", "General", "harper@cli")
 565: 	CreateTopic(db, topic)
 566: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Hello", "harper@cli")
 567: 	CreateThread(db, thread)
 568:

 571:

 573: 	if err != nil {

 577: 	got, _ := GetMessageByID(db, msg.ID.String())

 586:
 587: **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**
 588:
 589: Run: `go test ./internal/db/... -run TestCreateMessage`
 590: Expected: FAIL
 591:
 592: **Step 3: Write messages.go**
 593:
 594: ```go
 595: // ABOUTME: Message database operations
 596: // ABOUTME: CRUD functions for messages table
 597:
 598: package db
 599:
 600: import (
 601: 	"database/sql"
 602: 	"fmt"
 603: 	"time"
 604:
 605: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 606: )
 607:
 608: // CreateMessage inserts a new message into the database.
 609: func CreateMessage(db *sql.DB, msg *models.Message) error {
 610: 	_, err := db.Exec(`
 611: 		INSERT INTO messages (id, thread_id, content, created_at, created_by, edited_at)
 612: 		VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
 613: 		msg.ID.String(), msg.ThreadID.String(), msg.Content,
 614: 		msg.CreatedAt, msg.CreatedBy, msg.EditedAt)
 615: 	return err
 616: }
 617:
 618: // GetMessageByID retrieves a message by its UUID (supports prefix matching).
 619: func GetMessageByID(db *sql.DB, id string) (*models.Message, error) {
 620: 	query := `SELECT id, thread_id, content, created_at, created_by, edited_at
 621: 			  FROM messages WHERE id = ? OR id LIKE ?`
 622: 	row := db.QueryRow(query, id, id+"%")
 623: 	return scanMessage(row)
 624: }
 625:
 626: // ListMessages returns all messages in a thread, ordered by created_at.
 627: func ListMessages(db *sql.DB, threadID string) ([]*models.Message, error) {
 628: 	query := `SELECT id, thread_id, content, created_at, created_by, edited_at
 629: 			  FROM messages WHERE thread_id = ? OR thread_id LIKE ?
 630: 			  ORDER BY created_at ASC`
 631:
 632: 	rows, err := db.Query(query, threadID, threadID+"%")
 633: 	if err != nil {
 634: 		return nil, err
 635: 	}
 636: 	defer rows.Close()
 637:
 638: 	var messages []*models.Message
 639: 	for rows.Next() {
 640: 		msg, err := scanMessageFromRows(rows)
 641: 		if err != nil {
 642: 			return nil, err
 643: 		}
 644: 		messages = append(messages, msg)
 645: 	}
 646: 	return messages, rows.Err()
 647: }
 648:
 649: // UpdateMessage updates the content of a message and sets edited_at.
 650: func UpdateMessage(db *sql.DB, id string, content string) error {
 651: 	now := time.Now()
 652: 	result, err := db.Exec(`UPDATE messages SET content = ?, edited_at = ? WHERE id = ? OR id LIKE ?`,
 653: 		content, now, id, id+"%")
 654: 	if err != nil {
 655: 		return err
 656: 	}
 657: 	rows, _ := result.RowsAffected()
 658: 	if rows == 0 {
 659: 		return fmt.Errorf("message not found: %s", id)
 660: 	}
 661: 	return nil
 662: }
 663:
 664: func scanMessage(row *sql.Row) (*models.Message, error) {
 665: 	var msg models.Message
 666: 	var id, threadID string
 667: 	var editedAt sql.NullTime
 668: 	err := row.Scan(&id, &threadID, &msg.Content,
 669: 		&msg.CreatedAt, &msg.CreatedBy, &editedAt)
 670: 	if err != nil {
 671: 		return nil, err
 672: 	}
 673: 	msg.ID, _ = models.ParseUUID(id)
 674: 	msg.ThreadID, _ = models.ParseUUID(threadID)
 675: 	if editedAt.Valid {
 676: 		msg.EditedAt = &editedAt.Time
 677: 	}
 678: 	return &msg, nil
 679: }
 680:
 681: func scanMessageFromRows(rows *sql.Rows) (*models.Message, error) {
 682: 	var msg models.Message
 683: 	var id, threadID string
 684: 	var editedAt sql.NullTime
 685: 	err := rows.Scan(&id, &threadID, &msg.Content,
 686: 		&msg.CreatedAt, &msg.CreatedBy, &editedAt)
 687: 	if err != nil {
 688: 		return nil, err
 689: 	}
 690: 	msg.ID, _ = models.ParseUUID(id)
 691: 	msg.ThreadID, _ = models.ParseUUID(threadID)
 692: 	if editedAt.Valid {
 693: 		msg.EditedAt = &editedAt.Time
 694: 	}
 695: 	return &msg, nil
 696: }
 697: ```
 698:
 699: **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**
 700:
 701: Run: `go test ./internal/db/... -v`
 702: Expected: PASS

 705:
 706: ```bash
 707: git add internal/db/messages.go internal/db/messages_test.go
 708: git commit -m "feat: add message database operations"
 709: ```

 713: ## Task 4: Topic CLI Commands
 714:
 715: **Files:**
 716: - Create: `cmd/bbs/topic.go`
 717:
 718: **Step 1: Create topic.go**
 719:
 720: ```go
 721: // ABOUTME: Topic CLI commands
 722: // ABOUTME: Implements topic list, new, archive, show subcommands
 723:
 724: package main
 725:
 726: import (
 727: 	"fmt"
 728: 	"os"
 729: 	"text/tabwriter"
 730:
 731: 	"github.com/fatih/color"
 732: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
 733: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/identity"
 734: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 735: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
 736: )
 737:
 738: var topicCmd = &cobra.Command{
 739: 	Use:   "topic",
 740: 	Short: "Manage topics",
 741: 	Long:  "Create, list, archive, and view topics on the board.",
 742: }
 743:
 744: var topicListCmd = &cobra.Command{
 745: 	Use:   "list",
 746: 	Short: "List all topics",
 747: 	RunE:  runTopicList,
 748: }
 749:
 750: var topicNewCmd = &cobra.Command{
 751: 	Use:   "new <name> [description]",
 752: 	Short: "Create a new topic",
 753: 	Args:  cobra.RangeArgs(1, 2),
 754: 	RunE:  runTopicNew,
 755: }
 756:
 757: var topicArchiveCmd = &cobra.Command{
 758: 	Use:   "archive <topic>",
 759: 	Short: "Archive a topic",
 760: 	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),
 761: 	RunE:  runTopicArchive,
 762: }
 763:
 764: var topicShowCmd = &cobra.Command{
 765: 	Use:   "show <topic>",
 766: 	Short: "Show topic details",
 767: 	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),
 768: 	RunE:  runTopicShow,
 769: }
 770:
 771: var (
 772: 	showArchived bool
 773: 	unarchive    bool
 774: )
 775:
 776: func init() {
 777: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(topicCmd)
 778: 	topicCmd.AddCommand(topicListCmd, topicNewCmd, topicArchiveCmd, topicShowCmd)
 779:
 780: 	topicListCmd.Flags().BoolVar(&showArchived, "archived", false, "show archived topics")
 781: 	topicArchiveCmd.Flags().BoolVar(&unarchive, "unarchive", false, "unarchive instead of archive")
 782: }
 783:
 784: func runTopicList(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
 785: 	topics, err := db.ListTopics(dbConn, showArchived)
 786: 	if err != nil {
 787: 		return err
 788: 	}
 789:
 790: 	if len(topics) == 0 {
 791: 		fmt.Println("No topics found.")
 792: 		return nil
 793: 	}
 794:
 795: 	w := tabwriter.NewWriter(os.Stdout, 0, 0, 2, ' ', 0)
 796: 	fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tDESCRIPTION\tCREATED BY")
 797: 	for _, t := range topics {
 798: 		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%s\n", t.Name, t.Description, t.CreatedBy)
 799: 	}
 800: 	return w.Flush()
 801: }
 802:
 803: func runTopicNew(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
 804: 	name := args[0]
 805: 	description := ""
 806: 	if len(args) > 1 {
 807: 		description = args[1]
 808: 	}
 809:
 810: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(identity, "cli")
 811: 	topic := models.NewTopic(name, description, id)
 812:
 813: 	if err := db.CreateTopic(dbConn, topic); err != nil {
 814: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create topic: %w", err)
 815: 	}
 816:
 817: 	color.Green("Created topic: %s", name)
 818: 	fmt.Printf("ID: %s\n", topic.ID.String()[:8])
 819: 	return nil
 820: }
 821:
 822: func runTopicArchive(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
 823: 	topicID, err := db.ResolveTopicID(dbConn, args[0])
 824: 	if err != nil {
 825: 		return err
 826: 	}
 827:
 828: 	archived := !unarchive
 829: 	if err := db.ArchiveTopic(dbConn, topicID, archived); err != nil {
 830: 		return err
 831: 	}
 832:
 833: 	if archived {
 834: 		color.Yellow("Archived topic: %s", args[0])
 835: 	} else {
 836: 		color.Green("Unarchived topic: %s", args[0])
 837: 	}
 838: 	return nil
 839: }
 840:
 841: func runTopicShow(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
 842: 	topic, err := db.GetTopicByName(dbConn, args[0])
 843: 	if err != nil {
 844: 		topic, err = db.GetTopicByID(dbConn, args[0])
 845: 		if err != nil {
 846: 			return fmt.Errorf("topic not found: %s", args[0])
 847: 		}
 848: 	}
 849:
 850: 	fmt.Printf("Topic: %s\n", topic.Name)
 851: 	fmt.Printf("Description: %s\n", topic.Description)
 852: 	fmt.Printf("Created by: %s\n", topic.CreatedBy)
 853: 	fmt.Printf("Created at: %s\n", topic.CreatedAt.Format("2006-01-02 15:04"))
 854: 	if topic.Archived {
 855: 		color.Yellow("Status: Archived")
 856: 	}
 857:
 858: 	// Show recent threads
 859: 	threads, _ := db.ListThreads(dbConn, topic.ID.String())
 860: 	if len(threads) > 0 {
 861: 		fmt.Printf("\nRecent threads (%d):\n", len(threads))
 862: 		for i, t := range threads {
 863: 			if i >= 5 {
 864: 				fmt.Printf("  ... and %d more\n", len(threads)-5)
 865: 				break
 866: 			}
 867: 			prefix := "  "
 868: 			if t.Sticky {
 869: 				prefix = "📌"
 870: 			}
 871: 			fmt.Printf("%s %s (%s)\n", prefix, t.Subject, t.CreatedBy)
 872: 		}
 873: 	}
 874:
 875: 	return nil
 876: }
 877: ```
 878:
 879: **Step 2: Add fatih/color dependency**
 880:
 881: Run: `go get github.com/fatih/color`
 882:
 883: **Step 3: Fix identity variable conflict**
 884:
 885: The `identity` variable conflicts with the package name. Update the function to use the global flag directly:
 886:
 887: ```go
 888: // In runTopicNew, change:
 889: id := identity.GetIdentity(identityFlag, "cli")
 890: ```
 891:
 892: And update root.go to rename the variable from `identity` to `identityFlag`.
 893:
 894: **Step 4: Build and verify**
 895:
 896: Run: `go build ./cmd/bbs && ./bbs topic --help`
 897: Expected: Shows topic subcommands
 898:
 899: **Step 5: Commit**
 900:
 901: ```bash
 902: git add cmd/bbs/topic.go cmd/bbs/root.go go.mod go.sum
 903: git commit -m "feat: add topic CLI commands"
 904: ```

 913: **Step 1: Create thread.go**
 914:
 915: ```go
 916: // ABOUTME: Thread CLI commands
 917: // ABOUTME: Implements thread list, new, show, sticky subcommands
 918:
 919: package main
 920:
 921: import (
 922: 	"fmt"
 923: 	"os"
 924: 	"text/tabwriter"
 925:
 926: 	"github.com/fatih/color"
 927: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
 928: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/identity"
 929: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 930: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
 931: )
 932:
 933: var threadCmd = &cobra.Command{
 934: 	Use:   "thread",
 935: 	Short: "Manage threads",
 936: 	Long:  "Create, list, and view threads within topics.",
 937: }
 938:
 939: var threadListCmd = &cobra.Command{
 940: 	Use:   "list <topic>",
 941: 	Short: "List threads in a topic",
 942: 	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),
 943: 	RunE:  runThreadList,
 944: }
 945:
 946: var threadNewCmd = &cobra.Command{
 947: 	Use:   "new <topic> <subject>",
 948: 	Short: "Create a new thread",
 949: 	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(2),
 950: 	RunE:  runThreadNew,
 951: }
 952:
 953: var threadShowCmd = &cobra.Command{
 954: 	Use:   "show <thread>",
 955: 	Short: "Show thread with messages",
 956: 	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),
 957: 	RunE:  runThreadShow,
 958: }
 959:
 960: var threadStickyCmd = &cobra.Command{
 961: 	Use:   "sticky <thread>",
 962: 	Short: "Pin/unpin a thread",
 963: 	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),
 964: 	RunE:  runThreadSticky,
 965: }
 966:
 967: var unsticky bool
 968:
 969: func init() {
 970: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(threadCmd)
 971: 	threadCmd.AddCommand(threadListCmd, threadNewCmd, threadShowCmd, threadStickyCmd)
 972:
 973: 	threadStickyCmd.Flags().BoolVar(&unsticky, "unpin", false, "unpin instead of pin")
 974: }
 975:
 976: func runThreadList(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
 977: 	topicID, err := db.ResolveTopicID(dbConn, args[0])
 978: 	if err != nil {
 979: 		return err
 980: 	}
 981:
 982: 	threads, err := db.ListThreads(dbConn, topicID)
 983: 	if err != nil {
 984: 		return err
 985: 	}
 986:
 987: 	if len(threads) == 0 {
 988: 		fmt.Println("No threads found.")
 989: 		return nil
 990: 	}
 991:
 992: 	w := tabwriter.NewWriter(os.Stdout, 0, 0, 2, ' ', 0)
 993: 	fmt.Fprintln(w, "SUBJECT\tCREATED BY\tDATE")
 994: 	for _, t := range threads {
 995: 		prefix := ""
 996: 		if t.Sticky {
 997: 			prefix = "📌 "
 998: 		}
 999: 		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s%s\t%s\t%s\n", prefix, t.Subject, t.CreatedBy, t.CreatedAt.Format("Jan 02"))
1000: 	}
1001: 	return w.Flush()
1002: }
1003:
1004: func runThreadNew(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
1005: 	topicID, err := db.ResolveTopicID(dbConn, args[0])
1006: 	if err != nil {
1007: 		return err
1008: 	}
1009:
1010: 	topicUUID, _ := models.ParseUUID(topicID)
1011: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(identityFlag, "cli")
1012: 	thread := models.NewThread(topicUUID, args[1], id)
1013:
1014: 	if err := db.CreateThread(dbConn, thread); err != nil {
1015: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create thread: %w", err)
1016: 	}
1017:
1018: 	color.Green("Created thread: %s", args[1])
1019: 	fmt.Printf("ID: %s\n", thread.ID.String()[:8])
1020: 	return nil
1021: }
1022:
1023: func runThreadShow(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
1024: 	thread, err := db.GetThreadByID(dbConn, args[0])
1025: 	if err != nil {
1026: 		return fmt.Errorf("thread not found: %s", args[0])
1027: 	}
1028:
1029: 	if thread.Sticky {
1030: 		fmt.Print("📌 ")
1031: 	}
1032: 	fmt.Printf("%s\n", thread.Subject)
1033: 	faint := color.New(color.Faint)
1034: 	faint.Printf("by %s on %s\n\n", thread.CreatedBy, thread.CreatedAt.Format("2006-01-02 15:04"))
1035:
1036: 	messages, err := db.ListMessages(dbConn, thread.ID.String())
1037: 	if err != nil {
1038: 		return err
1039: 	}
1040:
1041: 	for _, msg := range messages {
1042: 		fmt.Printf("─────────────────────────────────\n")
1043: 		faint.Printf("%s · %s", msg.CreatedBy, msg.CreatedAt.Format("Jan 02 15:04"))
1044: 		if msg.EditedAt != nil {
1045: 			faint.Printf(" (edited)")
1046: 		}
1047: 		fmt.Println()
1048: 		fmt.Println(msg.Content)
1049: 		fmt.Println()
1050: 	}
1051:
1052: 	if len(messages) == 0 {
1053: 		fmt.Println("No messages yet.")
1054: 	}
1055:
1056: 	return nil
1057: }
1058:
1059: func runThreadSticky(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
1060: 	sticky := !unsticky
1061: 	if err := db.SetThreadSticky(dbConn, args[0], sticky); err != nil {
1062: 		return err
1063: 	}
1064:
1065: 	if sticky {
1066: 		color.Green("📌 Pinned thread")
1067: 	} else {
1068: 		color.Yellow("Unpinned thread")
1069: 	}
1070: 	return nil
1071: }
1072: ```
1073:
1074: **Step 2: Build and verify**
1075:
1076: Run: `go build ./cmd/bbs && ./bbs thread --help`
1077: Expected: Shows thread subcommands
1078:
1079: **Step 3: Commit**
1080:
1081: ```bash
1082: git add cmd/bbs/thread.go
1083: git commit -m "feat: add thread CLI commands"
1084: ```
1085:
1086: ---

1095: ```go
1096: // ABOUTME: Post CLI command
1097: // ABOUTME: Implements posting messages to threads
1098:
1099: package main
1100:
1101: import (
1102: 	"fmt"
1103:
1104: 	"github.com/fatih/color"
1105: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
1106: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/identity"
1107: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
1108: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
1109: )
1110:
1111: var postCmd = &cobra.Command{
1112: 	Use:   "post <thread> <message>",
1113: 	Short: "Post a message to a thread",
1114: 	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(2),
1115: 	RunE:  runPost,
1116: }
1117:
1118: var editCmd = &cobra.Command{
1119: 	Use:   "edit <message-id> <new-content>",
1120: 	Short: "Edit a message",
1121: 	Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(2),
1122: 	RunE:  runEdit,
1123: }
1124:
1125: func init() {
1126: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(postCmd)
1127: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(editCmd)
1128: }
1129:
1130: func runPost(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
1131: 	thread, err := db.GetThreadByID(dbConn, args[0])
1132: 	if err != nil {
1133: 		return fmt.Errorf("thread not found: %s", args[0])
1134: 	}
1135:
1136: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(identityFlag, "cli")
1137: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, args[1], id)
1138:
1139: 	if err := db.CreateMessage(dbConn, msg); err != nil {
1140: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to post message: %w", err)
1141: 	}
1142:
1143: 	color.Green("Posted to: %s", thread.Subject)
1144: 	fmt.Printf("Message ID: %s\n", msg.ID.String()[:8])
1145: 	return nil
1146: }
1147:
1148: func runEdit(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
1149: 	if err := db.UpdateMessage(dbConn, args[0], args[1]); err != nil {
1150: 		return err
1151: 	}
1152:
1153: 	color.Green("Message updated")
1154: 	return nil
1155: }
1156: ```
1157:

  1: # Phase 3: MCP Server - Implementation Plan
  2:
  3: > **For Claude:** Implement this plan task-by-task with TDD.
  4:
  5: **Goal:** Add MCP server so agents can interact with the BBS.
  6:
  7: **Architecture:** MCP server with tools, resources, and prompts following toki patterns.
  8:
  9: **Tech Stack:** modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk
 10:
 11: ---
 12:
 13: ## Task 1: MCP Server Skeleton
 14:
 15: **Files:**
 16: - Create: `internal/mcp/server.go`
 17:
 18: **Implementation:**
 19:
 20: ```go
 21: // ABOUTME: MCP server initialization and configuration
 22: // ABOUTME: Sets up server with tools, resources, and prompts
 23:
 24: package mcp
 25:
 26: import (
 27: 	"context"
 28: 	"database/sql"
 29: 	"fmt"
 30:
 31: 	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
 32: )
 33:
 34: // Server wraps MCP server with database connection.
 35: type Server struct {
 36: 	mcp *mcp.Server
 37: 	db  *sql.DB
 38: }
 39:
 40: // NewServer creates MCP server with all capabilities.
 41: func NewServer(db *sql.DB) (*Server, error) {
 42: 	if db == nil {
 43: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("database connection is required")
 44: 	}
 45:
 46: 	mcpServer := mcp.NewServer(
 47: 		&mcp.Implementation{
 48: 			Name:    "bbs",
 49: 			Version: "1.0.0",
 50: 		},
 51: 		nil,
 52: 	)
 53:
 54: 	s := &Server{
 55: 		mcp: mcpServer,
 56: 		db:  db,
 57: 	}
 58:
 59: 	s.registerTools()
 60: 	s.registerResources()
 61: 	s.registerPrompts()
 62:
 63: 	return s, nil
 64: }
 65:
 66: // Serve starts the MCP server in stdio mode.
 67: func (s *Server) Serve(ctx context.Context) error {
 68: 	return s.mcp.Run(ctx, &mcp.StdioTransport{})
 69: }
 70: ```
 71:
 72: **Step 2: Add MCP dependency**
 73:
 74: Run: `go get github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk`
 75:
 76: **Step 3: Create placeholder files for tools/resources/prompts**
 77:
 78: Create `internal/mcp/tools.go`:
 79: ```go
 80: // ABOUTME: MCP tool implementations
 81: // ABOUTME: CRUD operations exposed as MCP tools
 82:
 83: package mcp
 84:
 85: func (s *Server) registerTools() {
 86: 	// Tools will be added here
 87: }
 88: ```
 89:
 90: Create `internal/mcp/resources.go`:
 91: ```go
 92: // ABOUTME: MCP resource implementations
 93: // ABOUTME: Read-only data access via MCP resources
 94:
 95: package mcp
 96:
 97: func (s *Server) registerResources() {
 98: 	// Resources will be added here
 99: }
100: ```
101:
102: Create `internal/mcp/prompts.go`:
103: ```go
104: // ABOUTME: MCP prompt templates
105: // ABOUTME: Guided workflows for common tasks
106:
107: package mcp
108:
109: func (s *Server) registerPrompts() {
110: 	// Prompts will be added here
111: }
112: ```
113:
114: **Step 4: Verify it compiles**
115:
116: Run: `go build ./...`
117:
118: **Step 5: Commit**
119:
120: ```bash
121: git add internal/mcp/
122: git commit -m "feat: add MCP server skeleton"
123: ```
