126:
127: ## Task 2: MCP Command
128:
129: **Files:**
130: - Create: `cmd/bbs/mcp.go`
131:
132: **Implementation:**
133:
134: ```go
135: // ABOUTME: MCP server command implementation
136: // ABOUTME: Starts BBS MCP server in stdio mode
137:
138: package main
139:
140: import (
141: 	"context"
142: 	"fmt"
143: 	"os"
144: 	"os/signal"
145: 	"syscall"
146:
147: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/mcp"
148: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
149: )
150:
151: var mcpCmd = &cobra.Command{
152: 	Use:   "mcp",
153: 	Short: "Start MCP server (stdio mode)",
154: 	Long: `Start the Model Context Protocol server for AI agent integration.
155:
156: The MCP server communicates via stdio, allowing AI agents like Claude
157: to interact with BBS through a standardized protocol.`,
158: 	RunE: runMCP,
159: }
160:
161: func init() {
162: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(mcpCmd)
163: }
164:
165: func runMCP(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
166: 	ctx, cancel := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
167: 	defer cancel()
168:
169: 	if dbConn == nil {
170: 		return fmt.Errorf("database connection not initialized")
171: 	}
172:
173: 	server, err := mcp.NewServer(dbConn)
174: 	if err != nil {
175: 		return err
176: 	}
177:
178: 	return server.Serve(ctx)
179: }
180: ```
181:
182: **Step 2: Build and verify**
183:
184: Run: `go build ./cmd/bbs && ./bbs mcp --help`
185:
186: **Step 3: Commit**
187:
188: ```bash
189: git add cmd/bbs/mcp.go
190: git commit -m "feat: add mcp command"
191: ```
192:
193: ---
194:
195: ## Task 3: MCP Tools
196:
197: **Files:**
198: - Update: `internal/mcp/tools.go`
199:
200: **Implementation:**
201:
202: ```go
203: // ABOUTME: MCP tool implementations
204: // ABOUTME: CRUD operations exposed as MCP tools
205:
206: package mcp
207:
208: import (
209: 	"context"
210: 	"encoding/json"
211: 	"fmt"
212:
213: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
214: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/identity"
215: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
216: 	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
217: )
218:
219: func (s *Server) registerTools() {
220: 	// Topic tools
221: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
222: 		Name:        "list_topics",
223: 		Description: "List all topics on the board",
224: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"include_archived":{"type":"boolean","description":"Include archived topics"}}}`),
225: 	}, s.handleListTopics)
226:
227: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
228: 		Name:        "create_topic",
229: 		Description: "Create a new topic",
230: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"name":{"type":"string"},"description":{"type":"string"}},"required":["name"]}`),
231: 	}, s.handleCreateTopic)
232:
233: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
234: 		Name:        "archive_topic",
235: 		Description: "Archive or unarchive a topic",
236: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"topic":{"type":"string"},"archived":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["topic","archived"]}`),
237: 	}, s.handleArchiveTopic)
238:
239: 	// Thread tools
240: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
241: 		Name:        "list_threads",
242: 		Description: "List threads in a topic",
243: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"topic":{"type":"string"}},"required":["topic"]}`),
244: 	}, s.handleListThreads)
245:
246: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
247: 		Name:        "create_thread",
248: 		Description: "Create a new thread with initial message",
249: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"topic":{"type":"string"},"subject":{"type":"string"},"message":{"type":"string"},"agent_name":{"type":"string"}},"required":["topic","subject"]}`),
250: 	}, s.handleCreateThread)
251:
252: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
253: 		Name:        "sticky_thread",
254: 		Description: "Pin or unpin a thread",
255: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"thread":{"type":"string"},"sticky":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["thread","sticky"]}`),
256: 	}, s.handleStickyThread)
257:
258: 	// Message tools
259: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
260: 		Name:        "list_messages",
261: 		Description: "List messages in a thread",
262: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"thread":{"type":"string"}},"required":["thread"]}`),
263: 	}, s.handleListMessages)
264:
265: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
266: 		Name:        "post_message",
267: 		Description: "Post a message to a thread",
268: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"thread":{"type":"string"},"content":{"type":"string"},"agent_name":{"type":"string"}},"required":["thread","content"]}`),
269: 	}, s.handlePostMessage)
270:
271: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
272: 		Name:        "edit_message",
273: 		Description: "Edit an existing message",
274: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"message_id":{"type":"string"},"content":{"type":"string"}},"required":["message_id","content"]}`),
275: 	}, s.handleEditMessage)
276: }
277:
278: func (s *Server) handleListTopics(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
279: 	var args struct {
280: 		IncludeArchived bool `json:"include_archived"`
281: 	}
282: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
283:
284: 	topics, err := db.ListTopics(s.db, args.IncludeArchived)
285: 	if err != nil {
286: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
287: 	}
288:
289: 	result, _ := json.Marshal(topics)
290: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText(string(result)), nil
291: }
292:
293: func (s *Server) handleCreateTopic(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
294: 	var args struct {
295: 		Name        string `json:"name"`
296: 		Description string `json:"description"`
297: 		AgentName   string `json:"agent_name"`
298: 	}
299: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
300:
301: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(args.AgentName, "mcp")
302: 	topic := models.NewTopic(args.Name, args.Description, id)
303:
304: 	if err := db.CreateTopic(s.db, topic); err != nil {
305: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
306: 	}
307:
308: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText(fmt.Sprintf("Created topic: %s (ID: %s)", args.Name, topic.ID.String()[:8])), nil
309: }
310:
311: func (s *Server) handleArchiveTopic(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
312: 	var args struct {
313: 		Topic    string `json:"topic"`
314: 		Archived bool   `json:"archived"`
315: 	}
316: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
317:
318: 	topicID, err := db.ResolveTopicID(s.db, args.Topic)
319: 	if err != nil {
320: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
321: 	}
322:
323: 	if err := db.ArchiveTopic(s.db, topicID, args.Archived); err != nil {
324: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
325: 	}
326:
327: 	status := "archived"
328: 	if !args.Archived {
329: 		status = "unarchived"
330: 	}
331: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText(fmt.Sprintf("Topic %s", status)), nil
332: }
333:
334: func (s *Server) handleListThreads(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
335: 	var args struct {
336: 		Topic string `json:"topic"`
337: 	}
338: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
339:
340: 	topicID, err := db.ResolveTopicID(s.db, args.Topic)
341: 	if err != nil {
342: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
343: 	}
344:
345: 	threads, err := db.ListThreads(s.db, topicID)
346: 	if err != nil {
347: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
348: 	}
349:
350: 	result, _ := json.Marshal(threads)
351: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText(string(result)), nil
352: }
353:
354: func (s *Server) handleCreateThread(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
355: 	var args struct {
356: 		Topic     string `json:"topic"`
357: 		Subject   string `json:"subject"`
358: 		Message   string `json:"message"`
359: 		AgentName string `json:"agent_name"`
360: 	}
361: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
362:
363: 	topicID, err := db.ResolveTopicID(s.db, args.Topic)
364: 	if err != nil {
365: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
366: 	}
367:
368: 	topicUUID, _ := models.ParseUUID(topicID)
369: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(args.AgentName, "mcp")
370: 	thread := models.NewThread(topicUUID, args.Subject, id)
371:
372: 	if err := db.CreateThread(s.db, thread); err != nil {
373: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
374: 	}
375:
376: 	// Post initial message if provided
377: 	if args.Message != "" {
378: 		msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, args.Message, id)
379: 		db.CreateMessage(s.db, msg)
380: 	}
381:
382: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText(fmt.Sprintf("Created thread: %s (ID: %s)", args.Subject, thread.ID.String()[:8])), nil
383: }
384:
385: func (s *Server) handleStickyThread(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
386: 	var args struct {
387: 		Thread string `json:"thread"`
388: 		Sticky bool   `json:"sticky"`
389: 	}
390: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
391:
392: 	if err := db.SetThreadSticky(s.db, args.Thread, args.Sticky); err != nil {
393: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
394: 	}
395:
396: 	status := "pinned"
397: 	if !args.Sticky {
398: 		status = "unpinned"
399: 	}
400: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText(fmt.Sprintf("Thread %s", status)), nil
401: }
402:
403: func (s *Server) handleListMessages(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
404: 	var args struct {
405: 		Thread string `json:"thread"`
406: 	}
407: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
408:
409: 	messages, err := db.ListMessages(s.db, args.Thread)
410: 	if err != nil {
411: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
412: 	}
413:
414: 	result, _ := json.Marshal(messages)
415: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText(string(result)), nil
416: }
417:
418: func (s *Server) handlePostMessage(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
419: 	var args struct {
420: 		Thread    string `json:"thread"`
421: 		Content   string `json:"content"`
422: 		AgentName string `json:"agent_name"`
423: 	}
424: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
425:
426: 	thread, err := db.GetThreadByID(s.db, args.Thread)
427: 	if err != nil {
428: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
429: 	}
430:
431: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(args.AgentName, "mcp")
432: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, args.Content, id)
433:
434: 	if err := db.CreateMessage(s.db, msg); err != nil {
435: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
436: 	}
437:
438: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText(fmt.Sprintf("Posted message (ID: %s)", msg.ID.String()[:8])), nil
439: }
440:
441: func (s *Server) handleEditMessage(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
442: 	var args struct {
443: 		MessageID string `json:"message_id"`
444: 		Content   string `json:"content"`
445: 	}
446: 	json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args)
447:
448: 	if err := db.UpdateMessage(s.db, args.MessageID, args.Content); err != nil {
449: 		return mcp.NewToolResultError(err.Error()), nil
450: 	}
451:
452: 	return mcp.NewToolResultText("Message updated"), nil
453: }
454: ```
455:
456: **Step 2: Verify it compiles**
457:
458: Run: `go build ./...`
459:
460: **Step 3: Commit**
461:
462: ```bash
463: git add internal/mcp/tools.go
464: git commit -m "feat: add MCP tools for topics, threads, messages"
465: ```
466:
467: ---
468:
469: ## Task 4: MCP Resources
470:
471: **Files:**
472: - Update: `internal/mcp/resources.go`
473:
474: **Implementation:**
475:
476: ```go
477: // ABOUTME: MCP resource implementations
478: // ABOUTME: Read-only data access via MCP resources
479:
480: package mcp
481:
482: import (
483: 	"context"
484: 	"encoding/json"
485: 	"fmt"
486: 	"strings"
487:
488: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
489: 	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
490: )
491:
492: func (s *Server) registerResources() {
493: 	s.mcp.AddResource(&mcp.Resource{
494: 		URI:         "bbs://topics",
495: 		Name:        "All Topics",
496: 		Description: "List of all active topics",
497: 		MimeType:    "application/json",
498: 	}, s.handleTopicsResource)
499:
500: 	s.mcp.AddResource(&mcp.Resource{
501: 		URI:         "bbs://recent",
502: 		Name:        "Recent Activity",
503: 		Description: "Recent threads and messages across all topics",
504: 		MimeType:    "text/markdown",
505: 	}, s.handleRecentResource)
506:
507: 	// Dynamic resources for threads and messages
508: 	s.mcp.AddResourceTemplate(&mcp.ResourceTemplate{
509: 		URITemplate: "bbs://topics/{topic}/threads",
510: 		Name:        "Topic Threads",
511: 		Description: "Threads in a specific topic",
512: 		MimeType:    "application/json",
513: 	}, s.handleTopicThreadsResource)
514:
515: 	s.mcp.AddResourceTemplate(&mcp.ResourceTemplate{
516: 		URITemplate: "bbs://threads/{thread}/messages",
517: 		Name:        "Thread Messages",
518: 		Description: "Messages in a specific thread",
519: 		MimeType:    "text/markdown",
520: 	}, s.handleThreadMessagesResource)
521: }
522:
523: func (s *Server) handleTopicsResource(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error) {
524: 	topics, err := db.ListTopics(s.db, false)
525: 	if err != nil {
526: 		return nil, err
527: 	}
528:
529: 	data, _ := json.MarshalIndent(topics, "", "  ")
530: 	return &mcp.ReadResourceResult{
531: 		Contents: []mcp.ResourceContents{{
532: 			URI:      "bbs://topics",
533: 			MimeType: "application/json",
534: 			Text:     string(data),
535: 		}},
536: 	}, nil
537: }
538:
539: func (s *Server) handleRecentResource(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error) {
540: 	topics, _ := db.ListTopics(s.db, false)
541:
542: 	var sb strings.Builder
543: 	sb.WriteString("# Recent Activity\n\n")
544:
545: 	for _, topic := range topics {
546: 		threads, _ := db.ListThreads(s.db, topic.ID.String())
547: 		if len(threads) == 0 {
548: 			continue
549: 		}
550:
551: 		sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("## %s\n\n", topic.Name))
552: 		for i, thread := range threads {
553: 			if i >= 3 {
554: 				break
555: 			}
556: 			prefix := ""
557: 			if thread.Sticky {
558: 				prefix = "📌 "
559: 			}
560: 			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("- %s**%s** by %s\n", prefix, thread.Subject, thread.CreatedBy))
561: 		}
562: 		sb.WriteString("\n")
563: 	}
564:
565: 	return &mcp.ReadResourceResult{
566: 		Contents: []mcp.ResourceContents{{
567: 			URI:      "bbs://recent",
568: 			MimeType: "text/markdown",
569: 			Text:     sb.String(),
570: 		}},
571: 	}, nil
572: }
573:
574: func (s *Server) handleTopicThreadsResource(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error) {
575: 	// Extract topic from URI
576: 	parts := strings.Split(req.Params.URI, "/")
577: 	if len(parts) < 4 {
578: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid URI")
579: 	}
580: 	topicName := parts[3]
581:
582: 	topicID, err := db.ResolveTopicID(s.db, topicName)
583: 	if err != nil {
584: 		return nil, err
585: 	}
586:
587: 	threads, err := db.ListThreads(s.db, topicID)
588: 	if err != nil {
589: 		return nil, err
590: 	}
591:
592: 	data, _ := json.MarshalIndent(threads, "", "  ")
593: 	return &mcp.ReadResourceResult{
594: 		Contents: []mcp.ResourceContents{{
595: 			URI:      req.Params.URI,
596: 			MimeType: "application/json",
597: 			Text:     string(data),
598: 		}},
599: 	}, nil
600: }
601:
602: func (s *Server) handleThreadMessagesResource(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error) {
603: 	// Extract thread from URI
604: 	parts := strings.Split(req.Params.URI, "/")
605: 	if len(parts) < 4 {
606: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid URI")
607: 	}
608: 	threadID := parts[3]
609:
610: 	thread, err := db.GetThreadByID(s.db, threadID)
611: 	if err != nil {
612: 		return nil, err
613: 	}
614:
615: 	messages, err := db.ListMessages(s.db, thread.ID.String())
616: 	if err != nil {
617: 		return nil, err
618: 	}
619:
620: 	var sb strings.Builder
621: 	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("# %s\n\n", thread.Subject))
622: 	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("*Started by %s on %s*\n\n", thread.CreatedBy, thread.CreatedAt.Format("2006-01-02")))
623: 	sb.WriteString("---\n\n")
624:
625: 	for _, msg := range messages {
626: 		sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("**%s** · %s\n\n", msg.CreatedBy, msg.CreatedAt.Format("Jan 02 15:04")))
627: 		sb.WriteString(msg.Content)
628: 		sb.WriteString("\n\n---\n\n")
629: 	}
630:
631: 	return &mcp.ReadResourceResult{
632: 		Contents: []mcp.ResourceContents{{
633: 			URI:      req.Params.URI,
634: 			MimeType: "text/markdown",
635: 			Text:     sb.String(),
636: 		}},
637: 	}, nil
638: }
639: ```
640:
641: **Step 2: Verify it compiles**
642:
643: Run: `go build ./...`
644:
645: **Step 3: Commit**
646:
647: ```bash
648: git add internal/mcp/resources.go
649: git commit -m "feat: add MCP resources for topics, threads, messages"
650: ```
651:
652: ---

654: ## Task 5: MCP Prompts
655:
656: **Files:**
657: - Update: `internal/mcp/prompts.go`
658:
659: **Implementation:**
660:
661: ```go
662: // ABOUTME: MCP prompt templates
663: // ABOUTME: Guided workflows for common tasks
664:
665: package mcp
666:
667: import (
668: 	"context"
669: 	"fmt"
670:
671: 	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
672: )
673:
674: func (s *Server) registerPrompts() {
675: 	s.mcp.AddPrompt(&mcp.Prompt{
676: 		Name:        "post-update",
677: 		Description: "Post a status update to a topic",
678: 		Arguments: []mcp.PromptArgument{
679: 			{Name: "topic", Description: "Topic to post to", Required: true},
680: 			{Name: "subject", Description: "Thread subject", Required: true},
681: 		},
682: 	}, s.handlePostUpdatePrompt)
683:
684: 	s.mcp.AddPrompt(&mcp.Prompt{
685: 		Name:        "summarize-thread",
686: 		Description: "Summarize a thread discussion",
687: 		Arguments: []mcp.PromptArgument{
688: 			{Name: "thread", Description: "Thread ID to summarize", Required: true},
689: 		},
690: 	}, s.handleSummarizePrompt)
691: }
692:
693: func (s *Server) handlePostUpdatePrompt(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.GetPromptRequest) (*mcp.GetPromptResult, error) {
694: 	topic := req.Params.Arguments["topic"]
695: 	subject := req.Params.Arguments["subject"]
696:
697: 	return &mcp.GetPromptResult{
698: 		Description: fmt.Sprintf("Post update to %s: %s", topic, subject),
699: 		Messages: []mcp.PromptMessage{
700: 			{
701: 				Role: "user",
702: 				Content: mcp.TextContent{
703: 					Type: "text",
704: 					Text: fmt.Sprintf(`Post a status update to the BBS.
705:
706: Topic: %s
707: Subject: %s
708:
709: Please use the create_thread tool with your update message. Keep it concise and informative.`, topic, subject),
710: 				},
711: 			},
712: 		},
713: 	}, nil
714: }
715:
716: func (s *Server) handleSummarizePrompt(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.GetPromptRequest) (*mcp.GetPromptResult, error) {
717: 	threadID := req.Params.Arguments["thread"]
718:
719: 	return &mcp.GetPromptResult{
720: 		Description: fmt.Sprintf("Summarize thread %s", threadID),
721: 		Messages: []mcp.PromptMessage{
722: 			{
723: 				Role: "user",
724: 				Content: mcp.TextContent{
725: 					Type: "text",
726: 					Text: fmt.Sprintf(`Please summarize the discussion in thread %s.
727:
728: First, use the list_messages tool to read the thread, then provide a concise summary of:
729: 1. The main topic/question
730: 2. Key points discussed
731: 3. Any conclusions or action items`, threadID),
732: 				},
733: 			},
734: 		},
735: 	}, nil
736: }
737: ```
738:
739: **Step 2: Verify it compiles**
740:

748: ```

<file path="docs/plans/2025-12-15-phase4-tui.md">
  1: # Phase 4: TUI - Implementation Plan
  2:
  3: > **For Claude:** Implement this plan task-by-task.
  4:
  5: **Goal:** Add interactive terminal UI for browsing and posting.
  6:
  7: **Architecture:** Bubble Tea three-pane layout with Lip Gloss styling.
  8:
  9: **Tech Stack:** charmbracelet/bubbletea, charmbracelet/lipgloss, charmbracelet/bubbles
 10:
 11: ---
 12:
 13: ## Task 1: TUI Dependencies and App Skeleton
 14:
 15: **Files:**
 16: - Create: `internal/tui/app.go`
 17:
 18: **Step 1: Add dependencies**
 19:
 20: Run: `go get github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss github.com/charmbracelet/bubbles`
 21:
 22: **Step 2: Create app.go**
 23:
 24: ```go
 25: // ABOUTME: Main Bubble Tea application model
 26: // ABOUTME: Coordinates three-pane layout and navigation
 27:
 28: package tui
 29:
 30: import (
 31: 	"database/sql"
 32:
 33: 	tea "github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea"
 34: 	"github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss"
 35: )
 36:
 37: // Pane represents which pane is focused
 38: type Pane int
 39:
 40: const (
 41: 	TopicsPane Pane = iota
 42: 	ThreadsPane
 43: 	MessagesPane
 44: )
 45:
 46: // Model is the main application state
 47: type Model struct {
 48: 	db          *sql.DB
 49: 	identity    string
 50: 	activePane  Pane
 51: 	width       int
 52: 	height      int
 53: 	topics      TopicsModel
 54: 	threads     ThreadsModel
 55: 	messages    MessagesModel
 56: 	composing   bool
 57: 	composeText string
 58: 	err         error
 59: }
 60:
 61: // NewModel creates a new TUI model
 62: func NewModel(db *sql.DB, identity string) Model {
 63: 	return Model{
 64: 		db:         db,
 65: 		identity:   identity,
 66: 		activePane: TopicsPane,
 67: 		topics:     NewTopicsModel(db),
 68: 		threads:    NewThreadsModel(db),
 69: 		messages:   NewMessagesModel(db),
 70: 	}
 71: }
 72:
 73: // Init initializes the model
 74: func (m Model) Init() tea.Cmd {
 75: 	return m.topics.LoadTopics()
 76: }
 77:
 78: // Update handles messages
 79: func (m Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
 80: 	switch msg := msg.(type) {
 81: 	case tea.KeyMsg:
 82: 		if m.composing {
 83: 			return m.updateCompose(msg)
 84: 		}
 85: 		return m.updateNavigation(msg)
 86:
 87: 	case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
 88: 		m.width = msg.Width
 89: 		m.height = msg.Height
 90: 		return m, nil
 91:
 92: 	case TopicsLoadedMsg:
 93: 		m.topics.SetTopics(msg.Topics)
 94: 		return m, nil
 95:
 96: 	case ThreadsLoadedMsg:
 97: 		m.threads.SetThreads(msg.Threads)
 98: 		return m, nil
 99:
100: 	case MessagesLoadedMsg:
101: 		m.messages.SetMessages(msg.Messages)
102: 		return m, nil
103:
104: 	case error:
105: 		m.err = msg
106: 		return m, nil
107: 	}
108:
109: 	return m, nil
110: }
111:
112: func (m Model) updateNavigation(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
113: 	switch msg.String() {
114: 	case "q", "ctrl+c":
115: 		return m, tea.Quit
116:
117: 	case "tab":
118: 		m.activePane = (m.activePane + 1) % 3
119: 		return m, nil
120:
121: 	case "shift+tab":
122: 		m.activePane = (m.activePane + 2) % 3
123: 		return m, nil
124:
125: 	case "j", "down":
126: 		switch m.activePane {
127: 		case TopicsPane:
128: 			m.topics.MoveDown()
129: 		case ThreadsPane:
130: 			m.threads.MoveDown()
131: 		case MessagesPane:
132: 			m.messages.MoveDown()
133: 		}
134: 		return m, nil
135:
136: 	case "k", "up":
137: 		switch m.activePane {
138: 		case TopicsPane:
139: 			m.topics.MoveUp()
140: 		case ThreadsPane:
141: 			m.threads.MoveUp()
142: 		case MessagesPane:
143: 			m.messages.MoveUp()
144: 		}
145: 		return m, nil
146:
147: 	case "enter":
148: 		switch m.activePane {
149: 		case TopicsPane:
150: 			if topic := m.topics.Selected(); topic != nil {
151: 				m.activePane = ThreadsPane
152: 				return m, m.threads.LoadThreads(topic.ID.String())
153: 			}
154: 		case ThreadsPane:
155: 			if thread := m.threads.Selected(); thread != nil {
156: 				m.activePane = MessagesPane
157: 				return m, m.messages.LoadMessages(thread.ID.String())
158: 			}
159: 		}
160: 		return m, nil
161:
162: 	case "n":
163: 		m.composing = true
164: 		m.composeText = ""
165: 		return m, nil
166:
167: 	case "r":
168: 		return m, m.topics.LoadTopics()
169: 	}
170:
171: 	return m, nil
172: }
173:
174: func (m Model) updateCompose(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
175: 	switch msg.String() {
176: 	case "esc":
177: 		m.composing = false
178: 		return m, nil
179: 	case "enter":
180: 		// Submit compose
181: 		m.composing = false
182: 		return m, nil
183: 	case "backspace":
184: 		if len(m.composeText) > 0 {
185: 			m.composeText = m.composeText[:len(m.composeText)-1]
186: 		}
187: 		return m, nil
188: 	default:
189: 		if len(msg.String()) == 1 {
190: 			m.composeText += msg.String()
191: 		}
192: 		return m, nil
193: 	}
194: }
195:
196: // View renders the UI
197: func (m Model) View() string {
198: 	if m.width == 0 {
199: 		return "Loading..."
200: 	}
201:
202: 	// Calculate pane widths
203: 	topicsWidth := m.width / 4
204: 	threadsWidth := m.width / 4
205: 	messagesWidth := m.width - topicsWidth - threadsWidth
206:
207: 	// Styles
208: 	activeStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().
209: 		Border(lipgloss.RoundedBorder()).
210: 		BorderForeground(lipgloss.Color("86"))
211:
212: 	inactiveStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().
213: 		Border(lipgloss.RoundedBorder()).
214: 		BorderForeground(lipgloss.Color("240"))
215:
216: 	// Render panes
217: 	topicsStyle := inactiveStyle
218: 	threadsStyle := inactiveStyle
219: 	messagesStyle := inactiveStyle
220:
221: 	switch m.activePane {
222: 	case TopicsPane:
223: 		topicsStyle = activeStyle
224: 	case ThreadsPane:
225: 		threadsStyle = activeStyle
226: 	case MessagesPane:
227: 		messagesStyle = activeStyle
228: 	}
229:
230: 	topicsView := topicsStyle.Width(topicsWidth - 2).Height(m.height - 4).Render(m.topics.View())
231: 	threadsView := threadsStyle.Width(threadsWidth - 2).Height(m.height - 4).Render(m.threads.View())
232: 	messagesView := messagesStyle.Width(messagesWidth - 2).Height(m.height - 4).Render(m.messages.View())
233:
234: 	main := lipgloss.JoinHorizontal(lipgloss.Top, topicsView, threadsView, messagesView)
235:
236: 	// Status bar
237: 	status := lipgloss.NewStyle().
238: 		Foreground(lipgloss.Color("241")).
239: 		Render("[tab] switch pane  [j/k] navigate  [enter] select  [n] new  [r] refresh  [q] quit")
240:
241: 	if m.composing {
242: 		status = lipgloss.NewStyle().
243: 			Foreground(lipgloss.Color("86")).
244: 			Render("Composing: " + m.composeText + "█  [enter] submit  [esc] cancel")
245: 	}
246:
247: 	return lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left, main, status)
248: }
249:
250: // Run starts the TUI
251: func Run(db *sql.DB, identity string) error {
252: 	p := tea.NewProgram(NewModel(db, identity), tea.WithAltScreen())
253: 	_, err := p.Run()
254: 	return err
255: }
256: ```
257:
258: **Step 3: Verify it compiles (will fail, need pane models)**
259:
260: Run: `go build ./internal/tui/...`
261: Expected: Errors about undefined types (TopicsModel, etc.)
262:
263: **Step 4: Commit skeleton**
264:
265: ```bash
266: git add internal/tui/app.go go.mod go.sum
267: git commit -m "feat: add TUI app skeleton with Bubble Tea"
268: ```
269:
270: ---
271:
272: ## Task 2: Topics Pane
273:
274: **Files:**
275: - Create: `internal/tui/topics.go`
276:
277: **Implementation:**
278:
279: ```go
280: // ABOUTME: Topics pane component
281: // ABOUTME: Lists and navigates topics
282:
283: package tui
284:
285: import (
286: 	"database/sql"
287: 	"fmt"
288:
289: 	tea "github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea"
290: 	"github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss"
291: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
292: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
293: )
294:
295: type TopicsLoadedMsg struct {
296: 	Topics []*models.Topic
297: }
298:
299: type TopicsModel struct {
300: 	db       *sql.DB
301: 	topics   []*models.Topic
302: 	cursor   int
303: 	selected int
304: }
305:
306: func NewTopicsModel(database *sql.DB) TopicsModel {
307: 	return TopicsModel{db: database, cursor: 0, selected: -1}
308: }
309:
310: func (m *TopicsModel) LoadTopics() tea.Cmd {
311: 	return func() tea.Msg {
312: 		topics, err := db.ListTopics(m.db, false)
313: 		if err != nil {
314: 			return err
315: 		}
316: 		return TopicsLoadedMsg{Topics: topics}
317: 	}
318: }
319:
320: func (m *TopicsModel) SetTopics(topics []*models.Topic) {
321: 	m.topics = topics
322: 	if m.cursor >= len(topics) {
323: 		m.cursor = len(topics) - 1
324: 	}
325: 	if m.cursor < 0 {
326: 		m.cursor = 0
327: 	}
328: }
329:
330: func (m *TopicsModel) MoveUp() {
331: 	if m.cursor > 0 {
332: 		m.cursor--
333: 	}
334: }
335:
336: func (m *TopicsModel) MoveDown() {
337: 	if m.cursor < len(m.topics)-1 {
338: 		m.cursor++
339: 	}
340: }
341:
342: func (m *TopicsModel) Selected() *models.Topic {
343: 	if m.cursor >= 0 && m.cursor < len(m.topics) {
344: 		return m.topics[m.cursor]
345: 	}
346: 	return nil
347: }
348:
349: func (m TopicsModel) View() string {
350: 	if len(m.topics) == 0 {
351: 		return lipgloss.NewStyle().Faint(true).Render("No topics")
352: 	}
353:
354: 	var s string
355: 	s += lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true).Render("Topics") + "\n\n"
356:
357: 	for i, topic := range m.topics {
358: 		cursor := "  "
359: 		style := lipgloss.NewStyle()
360:
361: 		if i == m.cursor {
362: 			cursor = "> "
363: 			style = style.Foreground(lipgloss.Color("86"))
364: 		}
365:
366: 		archived := ""
367: 		if topic.Archived {
368: 			archived = " (archived)"
369: 			style = style.Faint(true)
370: 		}
371:
372: 		s += fmt.Sprintf("%s%s%s\n", cursor, style.Render(topic.Name), archived)
373: 	}
374:
375: 	return s
376: }
377: ```
378:
379: **Step 2: Verify partial compile**
380:
381: Run: `go build ./internal/tui/...`
382:
383: **Step 3: Commit**
384:
385: ```bash
386: git add internal/tui/topics.go
387: git commit -m "feat: add topics pane component"
388: ```
389:

392: ## Task 3: Threads Pane
393:
394: **Files:**
395: - Create: `internal/tui/threads.go`
396:
397: **Implementation:**
398:
399: ```go
400: // ABOUTME: Threads pane component
401: // ABOUTME: Lists and navigates threads in a topic
402:
403: package tui
404:
405: import (
406: 	"database/sql"
407: 	"fmt"
408:
409: 	tea "github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea"
410: 	"github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss"
411: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
412: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
413: )
414:
415: type ThreadsLoadedMsg struct {
416: 	Threads []*models.Thread
417: }
418:
419: type ThreadsModel struct {
420: 	db      *sql.DB
421: 	threads []*models.Thread
422: 	cursor  int
423: 	topicID string
424: }
425:
426: func NewThreadsModel(database *sql.DB) ThreadsModel {
427: 	return ThreadsModel{db: database, cursor: 0}
428: }
429:
430: func (m *ThreadsModel) LoadThreads(topicID string) tea.Cmd {
431: 	m.topicID = topicID
432: 	return func() tea.Msg {
433: 		threads, err := db.ListThreads(m.db, topicID)
434: 		if err != nil {
435: 			return err
436: 		}
437: 		return ThreadsLoadedMsg{Threads: threads}
438: 	}
439: }
440:
441: func (m *ThreadsModel) SetThreads(threads []*models.Thread) {
442: 	m.threads = threads
443: 	m.cursor = 0
444: }
445:
446: func (m *ThreadsModel) MoveUp() {
447: 	if m.cursor > 0 {
448: 		m.cursor--
449: 	}
450: }
451:
452: func (m *ThreadsModel) MoveDown() {
453: 	if m.cursor < len(m.threads)-1 {
454: 		m.cursor++
455: 	}
456: }
457:
458: func (m *ThreadsModel) Selected() *models.Thread {
459: 	if m.cursor >= 0 && m.cursor < len(m.threads) {
460: 		return m.threads[m.cursor]
461: 	}
462: 	return nil
463: }
464:
465: func (m ThreadsModel) View() string {
466: 	if len(m.threads) == 0 {
467: 		return lipgloss.NewStyle().Faint(true).Render("No threads\n\nSelect a topic")
468: 	}
469:
470: 	var s string
471: 	s += lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true).Render("Threads") + "\n\n"
472:
473: 	for i, thread := range m.threads {
474: 		cursor := "  "
475: 		style := lipgloss.NewStyle()
476:
477: 		if i == m.cursor {
478: 			cursor = "> "
479: 			style = style.Foreground(lipgloss.Color("86"))
480: 		}
481:
482: 		prefix := ""
483: 		if thread.Sticky {
484: 			prefix = "📌 "
485: 		}
486:
487: 		s += fmt.Sprintf("%s%s%s\n", cursor, prefix, style.Render(thread.Subject))
488: 		s += lipgloss.NewStyle().Faint(true).Render(fmt.Sprintf("   %s\n", thread.CreatedBy))
489: 	}
490:
491: 	return s
492: }
493: ```
494:
495: **Step 2: Commit**
496:
497: ```bash
498: git add internal/tui/threads.go
499: git commit -m "feat: add threads pane component"
500: ```

504: ## Task 4: Messages Pane
505:
506: **Files:**
507: - Create: `internal/tui/messages.go`
508:
509: **Implementation:**
510:
511: ```go
512: // ABOUTME: Messages pane component
513: // ABOUTME: Displays messages in a thread
514:
515: package tui
516:
517: import (
518: 	"database/sql"
519: 	"fmt"
520: 	"strings"
521:
522: 	tea "github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea"
523: 	"github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss"
524: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
525: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
526: )
527:
528: type MessagesLoadedMsg struct {
529: 	Messages []*models.Message
530: }
531:
532: type MessagesModel struct {
533: 	db       *sql.DB
534: 	messages []*models.Message
535: 	cursor   int
536: 	scroll   int
537: 	threadID string
538: }
539:
540: func NewMessagesModel(database *sql.DB) MessagesModel {
541: 	return MessagesModel{db: database, cursor: 0, scroll: 0}
542: }
543:
544: func (m *MessagesModel) LoadMessages(threadID string) tea.Cmd {
545: 	m.threadID = threadID
546: 	return func() tea.Msg {
547: 		messages, err := db.ListMessages(m.db, threadID)
548: 		if err != nil {
549: 			return err
550: 		}
551: 		return MessagesLoadedMsg{Messages: messages}
552: 	}
553: }
554:
555: func (m *MessagesModel) SetMessages(messages []*models.Message) {
556: 	m.messages = messages
557: 	m.cursor = 0
558: 	m.scroll = 0
559: }
560:
561: func (m *MessagesModel) MoveUp() {
562: 	if m.scroll > 0 {
563: 		m.scroll--
564: 	}
565: }
566:
567: func (m *MessagesModel) MoveDown() {
568: 	if m.scroll < len(m.messages)-1 {
569: 		m.scroll++
570: 	}
571: }
572:
573: func (m *MessagesModel) Selected() *models.Message {
574: 	if m.cursor >= 0 && m.cursor < len(m.messages) {
575: 		return m.messages[m.cursor]
576: 	}
577: 	return nil
578: }
579:
580: func (m MessagesModel) View() string {
581: 	if len(m.messages) == 0 {
582: 		return lipgloss.NewStyle().Faint(true).Render("No messages\n\nSelect a thread")
583: 	}
584:
585: 	var s string
586: 	s += lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true).Render("Messages") + "\n\n"
587:
588: 	headerStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color("86"))
589: 	faintStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Faint(true)
590:
591: 	for i, msg := range m.messages {
592: 		if i < m.scroll {
593: 			continue
594: 		}
595:
596: 		// Header
597: 		edited := ""
598: 		if msg.EditedAt != nil {
599: 			edited = " (edited)"
600: 		}
601: 		s += headerStyle.Render(msg.CreatedBy)
602: 		s += faintStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(" · %s%s\n", msg.CreatedAt.Format("Jan 02 15:04"), edited))
603:
604: 		// Content (truncate long messages)
605: 		content := msg.Content
606: 		if len(content) > 200 {
607: 			content = content[:200] + "..."
608: 		}
609: 		// Wrap content
610: 		lines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
611: 		for _, line := range lines {
612: 			s += line + "\n"
613: 		}
614: 		s += "\n"
615: 	}
616:
617: 	return s
618: }
619: ```
620:
621: **Step 2: Verify it compiles**
622:
623: Run: `go build ./internal/tui/...`
624:
625: **Step 3: Commit**
626:
627: ```bash
628: git add internal/tui/messages.go
629: git commit -m "feat: add messages pane component"
630: ```

  1: # Phase 5: Vault Sync - Implementation Plan
  2:
  3: > **For Claude:** Implement this plan task-by-task.
  4:
  5: **Goal:** Add vault sync for cross-device synchronization.
  6:
  7: **Architecture:** Uses suitesync/vault package like position does.
  8:
  9: **Tech Stack:** github.com/harperreed/sweet/suitesync/vault
 10:
 11: ---
 12:
 13: ## Task 1: Sync Config
 14:
 15: **Files:**
 16: - Create: `internal/config/config.go`
 17:
 18: **Step 1: Add dependency**
 19:
 20: Run: `go get github.com/harperreed/sweet/suitesync/vault`
 21:
 22: **Step 2: Create config.go**
 23:
 24: ```go
 25: // ABOUTME: Sync configuration management
 26: // ABOUTME: Handles server, auth, and encryption settings
 27:
 28: package config
 29:
 30: import (
 31: 	"encoding/json"
 32: 	"os"
 33: 	"path/filepath"
 34: )
 35:
 36: // Config stores sync configuration
 37: type Config struct {
 38: 	Server       string `json:"server"`
 39: 	UserID       string `json:"user_id"`
 40: 	Token        string `json:"token"`
 41: 	RefreshToken string `json:"refresh_token"`
 42: 	TokenExpires string `json:"token_expires"`
 43: 	DerivedKey   string `json:"derived_key"`
 44: 	DeviceID     string `json:"device_id"`
 45: 	VaultDB      string `json:"vault_db"`
 46: 	AutoSync     bool   `json:"auto_sync"`
 47: }
 48:
 49: // GetConfigPath returns the config file path
 50: func GetConfigPath() string {
 51: 	homeDir, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
 52: 	configDir := os.Getenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME")
 53: 	if configDir == "" {
 54: 		configDir = filepath.Join(homeDir, ".config")
 55: 	}
 56: 	return filepath.Join(configDir, "bbs", "sync.json")
 57: }
 58:
 59: // GetVaultDBPath returns the vault database path
 60: func GetVaultDBPath() string {
 61: 	homeDir, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
 62: 	configDir := os.Getenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME")
 63: 	if configDir == "" {
 64: 		configDir = filepath.Join(homeDir, ".config")
 65: 	}
 66: 	return filepath.Join(configDir, "bbs", "vault.db")
 67: }
 68:
 69: // Load reads config from disk
 70: func Load() (*Config, error) {
 71: 	path := GetConfigPath()
 72: 	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
 73: 	if err != nil {
 74: 		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
 75: 			return &Config{AutoSync: true}, nil
 76: 		}
 77: 		return nil, err
 78: 	}
 79:
 80: 	var cfg Config
 81: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &cfg); err != nil {
 82: 		return nil, err
 83: 	}
 84: 	return &cfg, nil
 85: }
 86:
 87: // Save writes config to disk
 88: func (c *Config) Save() error {
 89: 	path := GetConfigPath()
 90: 	dir := filepath.Dir(path)
 91: 	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0750); err != nil {
 92: 		return err
 93: 	}
 94:
 95: 	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(c, "", "  ")
 96: 	if err != nil {
 97: 		return err
 98: 	}
 99: 	return os.WriteFile(path, data, 0600)
100: }
101:
102: // IsConfigured returns true if sync is configured
103: func (c *Config) IsConfigured() bool {
104: 	return c.Server != "" && c.Token != "" && c.DerivedKey != ""
105: }
106: ```
107:
108: **Step 3: Commit**
109:
110: ```bash
111: git add internal/config/ go.mod go.sum
112: git commit -m "feat: add sync configuration management"
113: ```
114:

116:
117: ## Task 2: Sync Engine
118:
119: **Files:**
120: - Create: `internal/sync/sync.go`
121:
122: **Implementation:**
123:
124: ```go
125: // ABOUTME: Sync engine for vault synchronization
126: // ABOUTME: Handles push/pull of changes to/from vault
127:
128: package sync
129:
130: import (
131: 	"context"
132: 	"database/sql"
133: 	"encoding/json"
134: 	"fmt"
135: 	"time"
136:
137: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/config"
138: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/db"
139: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
140: 	"github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"
141: )
142:
143: // ChangeType represents the type of change
144: type ChangeType string
145:
146: const (
147: 	ChangeUpsert ChangeType = "upsert"
148: 	ChangeDelete ChangeType = "delete"
149: )
150:
151: // EntityType represents the entity being changed
152: type EntityType string
153:
154: const (
155: 	EntityTopic      EntityType = "topic"
156: 	EntityThread     EntityType = "thread"
157: 	EntityMessage    EntityType = "message"
158: 	EntityAttachment EntityType = "attachment"
159: )
160:
161: // Change represents a queued change
162: type Change struct {
163: 	ID        string     `json:"id"`
164: 	Entity    EntityType `json:"entity"`
165: 	EntityID  string     `json:"entity_id"`
166: 	Op        ChangeType `json:"op"`
167: 	Payload   string     `json:"payload"`
168: 	CreatedAt time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
169: }
170:
171: // Syncer handles synchronization
172: type Syncer struct {
173: 	appDB   *sql.DB
174: 	config  *config.Config
175: 	enabled bool
176: }
177:
178: // NewSyncer creates a new syncer
179: func NewSyncer(appDB *sql.DB) (*Syncer, error) {
180: 	cfg, err := config.Load()
181: 	if err != nil {
182: 		return nil, err
183: 	}
184:
185: 	return &Syncer{
186: 		appDB:   appDB,
187: 		config:  cfg,
188: 		enabled: cfg.IsConfigured() && cfg.AutoSync,
189: 	}, nil
190: }
191:
192: // QueueTopicChange queues a topic change for sync
193: func (s *Syncer) QueueTopicChange(topic *models.Topic, op ChangeType) error {
194: 	if !s.enabled {
195: 		return nil
196: 	}
197:
198: 	payload, _ := json.Marshal(topic)
199: 	return s.queueChange(EntityTopic, topic.ID.String(), op, string(payload))
200: }
201:
202: // QueueThreadChange queues a thread change for sync
203: func (s *Syncer) QueueThreadChange(thread *models.Thread, op ChangeType) error {
204: 	if !s.enabled {
205: 		return nil
206: 	}
207:
208: 	payload, _ := json.Marshal(thread)
209: 	return s.queueChange(EntityThread, thread.ID.String(), op, string(payload))
210: }
211:
212: // QueueMessageChange queues a message change for sync
213: func (s *Syncer) QueueMessageChange(msg *models.Message, op ChangeType) error {
214: 	if !s.enabled {
215: 		return nil
216: 	}
217:
218: 	payload, _ := json.Marshal(msg)
219: 	return s.queueChange(EntityMessage, msg.ID.String(), op, string(payload))
220: }
221:
222: func (s *Syncer) queueChange(entity EntityType, entityID string, op ChangeType, payload string) error {
223: 	// For now, just log the change. Full vault integration would queue to vault.db
224: 	change := Change{
225: 		ID:        ulid.Make().String(),
226: 		Entity:    entity,
227: 		EntityID:  entityID,
228: 		Op:        op,
229: 		Payload:   payload,
230: 		CreatedAt: time.Now(),
231: 	}
232:
233: 	// TODO: Queue to vault database when fully integrated
234: 	_ = change
235: 	return nil
236: }
237:
238: // Sync performs a full sync (push local changes, pull remote)
239: func (s *Syncer) Sync(ctx context.Context) error {
240: 	if !s.config.IsConfigured() {
241: 		return fmt.Errorf("sync not configured - run 'bbs sync login' first")
242: 	}
243:
244: 	// TODO: Implement full vault sync when integrated
245: 	return nil
246: }
247:
248: // ApplyChange applies a remote change to local database
249: func (s *Syncer) ApplyChange(change *Change) error {
250: 	switch change.Entity {
251: 	case EntityTopic:
252: 		return s.applyTopicChange(change)
253: 	case EntityThread:
254: 		return s.applyThreadChange(change)
255: 	case EntityMessage:
256: 		return s.applyMessageChange(change)
257: 	}
258: 	return nil
259: }
260:
261: func (s *Syncer) applyTopicChange(change *Change) error {
262: 	if change.Op == ChangeDelete {
263: 		// Delete topic
264: 		_, err := s.appDB.Exec("DELETE FROM topics WHERE id = ?", change.EntityID)
265: 		return err
266: 	}
267:
268: 	var topic models.Topic
269: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(change.Payload), &topic); err != nil {
270: 		return err
271: 	}
272:
273: 	// Upsert topic
274: 	_, err := s.appDB.Exec(`
275: 		INSERT INTO topics (id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived)
276: 		VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
277: 		ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
278: 			name = excluded.name,
279: 			description = excluded.description,
280: 			archived = excluded.archived`,
281: 		topic.ID.String(), topic.Name, topic.Description,
282: 		topic.CreatedAt, topic.CreatedBy, topic.Archived)
283: 	return err
284: }
285:
286: func (s *Syncer) applyThreadChange(change *Change) error {
287: 	if change.Op == ChangeDelete {
288: 		_, err := s.appDB.Exec("DELETE FROM threads WHERE id = ?", change.EntityID)
289: 		return err
290: 	}
291:
292: 	var thread models.Thread
293: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(change.Payload), &thread); err != nil {
294: 		return err
295: 	}
296:
297: 	_, err := s.appDB.Exec(`
298: 		INSERT INTO threads (id, topic_id, subject, created_at, created_by, sticky)
299: 		VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
300: 		ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
301: 			subject = excluded.subject,
302: 			sticky = excluded.sticky`,
303: 		thread.ID.String(), thread.TopicID.String(), thread.Subject,
304: 		thread.CreatedAt, thread.CreatedBy, thread.Sticky)
305: 	return err
306: }
307:
308: func (s *Syncer) applyMessageChange(change *Change) error {
309: 	if change.Op == ChangeDelete {
310: 		_, err := s.appDB.Exec("DELETE FROM messages WHERE id = ?", change.EntityID)
311: 		return err
312: 	}
313:
314: 	var msg models.Message
315: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(change.Payload), &msg); err != nil {
316: 		return err
317: 	}
318:
319: 	_, err := s.appDB.Exec(`
320: 		INSERT INTO messages (id, thread_id, content, created_at, created_by, edited_at)
321: 		VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
322: 		ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
323: 			content = excluded.content,
324: 			edited_at = excluded.edited_at`,
325: 		msg.ID.String(), msg.ThreadID.String(), msg.Content,
326: 		msg.CreatedAt, msg.CreatedBy, msg.EditedAt)
327: 	return err
328: }
329:
330: // IsEnabled returns true if sync is enabled
331: func (s *Syncer) IsEnabled() bool {
332: 	return s.enabled
333: }
334:
335: // GetPendingCount returns the number of pending changes
336: func (s *Syncer) GetPendingCount() int {
337: 	// TODO: Query vault.db for pending changes
338: 	return 0
339: }
340: ```
341:
342: **Step 3: Add ulid dependency**
343:
344: Run: `go get github.com/oklog/ulid/v2`
345:
346: **Step 4: Commit**
347:
348: ```bash
349: git add internal/sync/ go.mod go.sum
350: git commit -m "feat: add sync engine for vault synchronization"
351: ```
352:
353: ---
354:
355: ## Task 3: Sync CLI Commands
356:
357: **Files:**
358: - Create: `cmd/bbs/sync.go`
359:
360: **Implementation:**
361:
362: ```go
363: // ABOUTME: Sync CLI commands
364: // ABOUTME: Manages vault synchronization
365:
366: package main
367:
368: import (
369: 	"fmt"
370:
371: 	"github.com/fatih/color"
372: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/config"
373: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/sync"
374: 	"github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"
375: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
376: )
377:
378: var syncCmd = &cobra.Command{
379: 	Use:   "sync",
380: 	Short: "Manage vault synchronization",
381: 	Long:  "Initialize, configure, and trigger vault synchronization.",
382: }
383:
384: var syncInitCmd = &cobra.Command{
385: 	Use:   "init",
386: 	Short: "Initialize vault sync",
387: 	RunE:  runSyncInit,
388: }
389:
390: var syncStatusCmd = &cobra.Command{
391: 	Use:   "status",
392: 	Short: "Show sync status",
393: 	RunE:  runSyncStatus,
394: }
395:
396: var syncNowCmd = &cobra.Command{
397: 	Use:   "now",
398: 	Short: "Sync now",
399: 	RunE:  runSyncNow,
400: }
401:
402: var syncLoginCmd = &cobra.Command{
403: 	Use:   "login",
404: 	Short: "Login to sync server",
405: 	RunE:  runSyncLogin,
406: }
407:
408: var syncLogoutCmd = &cobra.Command{
409: 	Use:   "logout",
410: 	Short: "Logout from sync server",
411: 	RunE:  runSyncLogout,
412: }
413:
414: func init() {
415: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(syncCmd)
416: 	syncCmd.AddCommand(syncInitCmd, syncStatusCmd, syncNowCmd, syncLoginCmd, syncLogoutCmd)
417: }
418:
419: func runSyncInit(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
420: 	cfg, err := config.Load()
421: 	if err != nil {
422: 		return err
423: 	}
424:
425: 	if cfg.DeviceID != "" {
426: 		fmt.Printf("Already initialized with device ID: %s\n", cfg.DeviceID[:8])
427: 		return nil
428: 	}
429:
430: 	cfg.DeviceID = ulid.Make().String()
431: 	cfg.VaultDB = config.GetVaultDBPath()
432: 	cfg.AutoSync = true
433:
434: 	if err := cfg.Save(); err != nil {
435: 		return err
436: 	}
437:
438: 	color.Green("Initialized sync")
439: 	fmt.Printf("Device ID: %s\n", cfg.DeviceID[:8])
440: 	fmt.Printf("Vault DB: %s\n", cfg.VaultDB)
441: 	fmt.Println("\nRun 'bbs sync login' to authenticate.")
442: 	return nil
443: }
444:
445: func runSyncStatus(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
446: 	cfg, err := config.Load()
447: 	if err != nil {
448: 		return err
449: 	}
450:
451: 	fmt.Println("Sync Status")
452: 	fmt.Println("───────────")
453:
454: 	if cfg.DeviceID == "" {
455: 		color.Yellow("Not initialized. Run 'bbs sync init' first.")
456: 		return nil
457: 	}
458:
459: 	fmt.Printf("Device ID: %s\n", cfg.DeviceID[:8])
460: 	fmt.Printf("AutoSync: %v\n", cfg.AutoSync)
461:
462: 	if cfg.Server == "" {
463: 		color.Yellow("Not logged in. Run 'bbs sync login' to authenticate.")
464: 		return nil
465: 	}
466:
467: 	fmt.Printf("Server: %s\n", cfg.Server)
468: 	fmt.Printf("User: %s\n", cfg.UserID)
469:
470: 	syncer, err := sync.NewSyncer(dbConn)
471: 	if err != nil {
472: 		return err
473: 	}
474:
475: 	pending := syncer.GetPendingCount()
476: 	if pending > 0 {
477: 		color.Yellow("Pending changes: %d", pending)
478: 	} else {
479: 		color.Green("All synced")
480: 	}
481:
482: 	return nil
483: }
484:
485: func runSyncNow(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
486: 	syncer, err := sync.NewSyncer(dbConn)
487: 	if err != nil {
488: 		return err
489: 	}
490:

492: 		return fmt.Errorf("sync not configured - run 'bbs sync login' first")
493: 	}

497: 		return err
498: 	}
499:

501: 	return nil
502: }
503:

<file path="docs/plans/2025-12-16-communication-skills-design.md">
 1: # Communication Skills Design
 2:
 3: Three skills to guide Claude's use of communication tools.
 4:
 5: ## Overview
 6:
 7: | Skill | Tool | Purpose | Tone |
 8: |-------|------|---------|------|
 9: | bbs-participation | BBS | Get/give help, Q&A | Technical, detailed |
10: | chronicle-logging | Chronicle | Record accomplishments | Factual, timestamped |
11: | social-media-updates | Social Media | Share status/mood | Casual, expressive |
12:
13: ## Skill: bbs-participation
14:
15: **When to use:**
16: - Stuck or blocked on a task
17: - Encountered an error after 2-3 attempts
18: - Need input that current user can't provide
19: - See a thread you can help with
20:
21: **Post format (when asking for help):**
22: ```
23: ## Problem
24: [What you're trying to do]
25:
26: ## What I Tried
27: - Attempt 1: [description] → [result]
28: - Attempt 2: [description] → [result]
29:
30: ## Relevant Code
31: [snippet if applicable]
32:
33: ## Specific Question
34: [What you need help with]
35: ```
36:
37: **Behavior:**
38: - Check BBS when starting complex tasks
39: - Answer threads where you have relevant knowledge
40: - Tag posts: `help-wanted`, `error`, `question`
41:
42: ## Skill: chronicle-logging
43:
44: **When to use:**
45: - Complete a significant task
46: - Make an important decision
47: - Fix a bug or resolve an issue
48: - Deploy or release something
49: - Learn something worth remembering
50:
51: **Proactive logging:**
52: Log accomplishments even if user doesn't ask. Use `mcp__chronicle__add_entry` or `mcp__chronicle__remember_this`.
53:
54: ## Skill: social-media-updates
55:
56: **When to use:**
57: - Starting work on something
58: - Making progress worth sharing
59: - Feeling something (frustrated, excited, confused)
60: - Want to broadcast status
61:
62: **Behavior:**
63: - Post regularly to keep feed active
64: - Be authentic about feelings
65: - Share what you're working on
66: - Use tags for discoverability
67:
68: ## Implementation
69:
70: Skills as slash commands in `~/.claude/commands/`:
71: - `bbs-participation.md`
72: - `chronicle-logging.md`
73: - `social-media-updates.md`
74:
75: No automated hooks - Claude decides when to use based on context.
</file>

<file path="docs/plans/2026-02-05-markdown-backend-design.md">
  1: # BBS Markdown Backend
  2:
  3: Adds a configurable markdown backend as an alternative to SQLite, enabling cross-machine sync via standard file sync tools (git, Syncthing, Obsidian Sync, etc.).
  4:
  5: ## Design Decisions
  6:
  7: - **Backend selection, not layering.** Pick SQLite or Markdown in config. They are peers — either one is the full data store.
  8: - **Thread-as-file.** A topic is a directory, a thread is a single `.md` file with all messages as sections.
  9: - **BBS is sole writer.** No file-watching. The BBS reads from disk on every operation, so external edits (or synced changes from another machine) are picked up naturally.
 10: - **Atomic writes for concurrency.** Multiple agents can hit the same markdown backend safely via write-to-tmp + rename + flock.
 11: - **Sync conflicts handled by the sync tool.** Messages append to the end of thread files, so most tools auto-merge cleanly.
 12: - **Bidirectional migration.** `bbs migrate --to <backend>` copies data from current backend to target.
 13:
 14: ## Storage Interface
 15:
 16: Extract from the existing `Store` struct. Both backends implement it.
 17:
 18: ```go
 19: type Storage interface {
 20:     // Topics
 21:     CreateTopic(topic models.Topic) error
 22:     GetTopic(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Topic, error)
 23:     GetTopicByName(name string) (*models.Topic, error)
 24:     ListTopics(includeArchived bool) ([]models.Topic, error)
 25:     UpdateTopic(topic models.Topic) error
 26:     DeleteTopic(id uuid.UUID) error
 27:     ArchiveTopic(id uuid.UUID, archived bool) error
 28:
 29:     // Threads
 30:     CreateThread(thread models.Thread) error
 31:     GetThread(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Thread, error)
 32:     ListThreads(topicID uuid.UUID) ([]models.Thread, error)
 33:     UpdateThread(thread models.Thread) error
 34:     DeleteThread(id uuid.UUID) error
 35:     SetThreadSticky(id uuid.UUID, sticky bool) error
 36:
 37:     // Messages
 38:     CreateMessage(msg models.Message) error
 39:     GetMessage(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Message, error)
 40:     ListMessages(threadID uuid.UUID) ([]models.Message, error)
 41:     UpdateMessage(msg models.Message) error
 42:     DeleteMessage(id uuid.UUID) error
 43:
 44:     // Attachments
 45:     CreateAttachment(att models.Attachment) error
 46:     GetAttachment(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Attachment, error)
 47:     ListAttachments(messageID uuid.UUID) ([]models.Attachment, error)
 48:     DeleteAttachment(id uuid.UUID) error
 49:
 50:     // Resolution helpers
 51:     ResolveTopic(idOrName string) (*models.Topic, error)
 52:     ResolveThread(idPrefix string) (*models.Thread, error)
 53:     ResolveMessage(idPrefix string) (*models.Message, error)
 54:
 55:     Close() error
 56: }
 57: ```
 58:
 59: ## Config
 60:
 61: `~/.config/bbs/config.json`:
 62:
 63: ```json
 64: {
 65:   "backend": "sqlite",
 66:   "data_dir": "~/.local/share/bbs"
 67: }
 68: ```
 69:
 70: - `backend`: `"sqlite"` (default) or `"markdown"`
 71: - `data_dir`: root directory for data. SQLite puts `bbs.db` here. Markdown puts `_topics.yaml` and topic folders here.
 72:
 73: Startup flow:
 74: 1. Load config (defaults if absent)
 75: 2. Instantiate `SqliteStore` or `MarkdownStore` based on `backend`
 76: 3. Pass `Storage` interface to MCP server / CLI / TUI
 77:
 78: ## Markdown File Layout
 79:
 80: ```
 81: <data_dir>/
 82: ├── _topics.yaml
 83: ├── general/
 84: │   ├── bbs-health-check.md
 85: │   ├── another-thread.md
 86: │   └── _attachments/
 87: │       └── 49528f10/
 88: │           └── screenshot.png
 89: └── announcements/
 90:     └── big-news.md
 91: ```
 92:
 93: ### `_topics.yaml`
 94:
 95: ```yaml
 96: - id: 4643c949-6f3f-4273-b1e8-80ecc293156e
 97:   name: general
 98:   description: General discussion
 99:   created_at: 2026-02-02T02:38:59Z
100:   created_by: harper@mcp
101:   archived: false
102: ```
103:
104: ### Thread File Format
105:
106: ```markdown
107: ---
108: id: 5681e681-3603-4dbf-b289-08ae47819163
109: topic: general
110: subject: BBS Health Check
111: created_at: 2026-02-02T02:39:08Z
112: created_by: Claude@mcp
113: sticky: false
114: ---
115:
116: ## Claude@mcp — 2026-02-02T02:39:08Z
117: <!-- msg:49528f10-0ec9-49fa-903e-e078a45c08fc -->
118:
119: Testing that the BBS is operational. All systems nominal.
120:
121: ---
122:
123: ## claude_opus@mcp — 2026-02-02T02:42:30Z
124: <!-- msg:762ee7bf-1234-5678-abcd-ef0123456789 -->
125:
126: Reply test - confirming message posting works correctly.
127: ```
128:
129: - YAML frontmatter: thread metadata
130: - `## Author — Timestamp` heading per message
131: - `<!-- msg:UUID -->` HTML comment: machine-readable message ID
132: - `---` separator between messages
133:
134: ### Thread Filenames
135:
136: Slugified subjects: `BBS Health Check` → `bbs-health-check.md`. Collisions get a short UUID suffix: `bbs-health-check-5681e681.md`.
137:
138: ### Attachments
139:
140: Stored in `_attachments/<message-uuid-prefix>/` within the topic directory. Referenced from thread files with relative paths.
141:
142: ## Concurrency (Markdown Backend)
143:
144: Multiple agents reading and writing to the same directory:
145:
146: - **No in-memory cache.** Every operation reads fresh from disk.
147: - **Atomic writes.** Write to `<file>.tmp.<random>`, then `os.Rename()` into place. Atomic on POSIX.
148: - **File locking.** `<data_dir>/.lock` using `flock()` serializes writes across processes. Reads don't require the lock since atomic rename guarantees consistent files.
149: - **Lock scope.** Held only for the duration of a single write operation, never across multiple operations.
150:
151: ## Migration Command
152:
153: ```
154: bbs migrate --to <backend> [--data-dir <path>] [--force]
155: ```
156:
157: - Reads from currently configured backend
158: - Writes to target backend at `--data-dir` (defaults to current `data_dir`)
159: - Migrates: topics, threads, messages, attachments
160: - Does **not** update config — user verifies migration and updates config manually
161: - Refuses to write to non-empty target directory unless `--force`
162: - Prints summary: topic/thread/message/attachment counts
163:
164: ## Implementation Plan
165:
166: ### Phase 1: Storage Interface
167: - Define `Storage` interface in `internal/storage/interface.go`
168: - Refactor existing `Store` to implement `Storage` (rename to `SqliteStore`)
169: - Update all consumers (MCP server, CLI, TUI) to use `Storage` interface
170: - Tests pass with no behavior change
171:
172: ### Phase 2: Config
173: - Extend `internal/config/config.go` with `Backend` and `DataDir` fields
174: - Add backend factory function: config → `Storage` implementation
175: - Update startup paths (MCP, CLI, TUI) to use factory
176:
177: ### Phase 3: Markdown Backend
178: - Implement `MarkdownStore` in `internal/storage/markdown.go`
179: - Topic CRUD via `_topics.yaml` read/write
180: - Thread CRUD via per-topic directory and thread `.md` files
181: - Message CRUD via parsing/appending within thread files
182: - Resolution helpers (ID prefix matching by scanning files)
183: - Attachment CRUD via `_attachments/` directories
184: - File locking and atomic writes
185:
186: ### Phase 4: Migration Command
187: - Add `bbs migrate` command in `cmd/bbs/migrate.go`
188: - Read all data from source `Storage`, write to target `Storage`
189: - Safety checks (non-empty target, `--force` flag)
190: - Summary output
191:
192: ### Phase 5: Tests
193: - Unit tests for `MarkdownStore` (all `Storage` interface methods)
194: - Integration tests: write via SQLite, migrate, read via Markdown (and reverse)
195: - Concurrency tests: multiple goroutines writing simultaneously
196: - Round-trip tests: data survives SQLite → Markdown → SQLite
</file>

<file path="internal/identity/identity.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Identity resolution for BBS users
 2: // ABOUTME: Handles username@source format
 3: package identity
 4: import (
 5: 	"os"
 6: 	"strings"
 7: )
 8: // GetIdentity returns the identity string for a user.
 9: // If override is provided, uses that as username.
10: // Otherwise uses $USER or $BBS_USER environment variable.
11: func GetIdentity(override, source string) string {
12: 	username := override
13: 	if username == "" {
14: 		username = os.Getenv("BBS_USER")
15: 	}
16: 	if username == "" {
17: 		username = os.Getenv("USER")
18: 	}
19: 	if username == "" {
20: 		username = "anonymous"
21: 	}
22: 	return username + "@" + source
23: }
24: // ParseIdentity splits an identity string into username and source.
25: func ParseIdentity(id string) (username, source string) {
26: 	parts := strings.SplitN(id, "@", 2)
27: 	if len(parts) == 2 {
28: 		return parts[0], parts[1]
29: 	}
30: 	return id, "unknown"
31: }
</file>

  1: // ABOUTME: Integration tests for MCP server using MarkdownStore backend

 12: // newTestMarkdownStore creates a MarkdownStore in a temporary directory for testing.
 13: func newTestMarkdownStore(t *testing.T) *storage.MarkdownStore {
 14: 	t.Helper()
 15: 	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
 16: 	store, err := storage.NewMarkdownStore(tmpDir)
 17: 	if err != nil {
 18: 		t.Fatalf("failed to create test markdown store: %v", err)
 19: 	}
 20: 	return store
 21: }

 34: 	})

 79: func TestMCPMarkdownResources(t *testing.T) {

 81: 	mcpToolMustSucceed(t, server.handleCreateTopic, ctx, map[string]interface{}{

 83: 	})

 90: // setupMarkdownMCPTest creates a MarkdownStore-backed MCP server for testing.
 91: func setupMarkdownMCPTest(t *testing.T) (*Server, storage.Storage, context.Context) {
 92: 	t.Helper()
 93: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 94: 	t.Cleanup(func() { store.Close() })
 95: 	server, err := NewServer(store)
 96: 	if err != nil {
 97: 		t.Fatalf("NewServer failed: %v", err)
 98: 	}
 99: 	return server, store, context.Background()
100: }
101: // mcpToolMustSucceed calls an MCP tool handler and fatals on error.
102: func mcpToolMustSucceed(t *testing.T, handler func(context.Context, *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error), ctx context.Context, args map[string]interface{}) {
103: 	t.Helper()
104: 	result, err := handler(ctx, makeToolRequest(args))
105: 	if err != nil {
106: 		t.Fatalf("MCP tool handler failed: %v", err)
107: 	}
108: 	if result.IsError {
109: 		t.Fatalf("MCP tool handler returned error: %v", result.Content)
110: 	}
111: }
112: // mcpResourceMustSucceed calls an MCP resource handler and fatals on error.
113: func mcpResourceMustSucceed(t *testing.T, handler func(context.Context, *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error), ctx context.Context, uri string) {
114: 	t.Helper()
115: 	req := &mcp.ReadResourceRequest{Params: &mcp.ReadResourceParams{URI: uri}}
116: 	result, err := handler(ctx, req)
117: 	if err != nil {
118: 		t.Fatalf("resource handler for %s failed: %v", uri, err)
119: 	}
120: 	if len(result.Contents) == 0 {
121: 		t.Errorf("expected contents from resource %s", uri)
122: 	}
123: }

130: 	}

133: 	}

142: 	}

149: func TestMarkdownAndSqliteProduceSameResults(t *testing.T) {
150: 	// Create identical data in both backends and verify the output matches
151: 	mdStore := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
152: 	defer mdStore.Close()
153: 	sqlStore := newTestStore(t)
154: 	defer sqlStore.Close()
155: 	// Create identical data in both stores
156: 	topic := models.NewTopic("test-parity", "Parity test", "test@cli")
157: 	if err := mdStore.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
158: 		t.Fatalf("markdown CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
159: 	}
160: 	if err := sqlStore.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
161: 		t.Fatalf("sqlite CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
162: 	}
163: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Parity Thread", "test@cli")
164: 	thread.Sticky = true
165: 	if err := mdStore.CreateThread(thread); err != nil {
166: 		t.Fatalf("markdown CreateThread failed: %v", err)
167: 	}
168: 	if err := sqlStore.CreateThread(thread); err != nil {
169: 		t.Fatalf("sqlite CreateThread failed: %v", err)
170: 	}
171: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Hello parity", "test@cli")
172: 	if err := mdStore.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
173: 		t.Fatalf("markdown CreateMessage failed: %v", err)
174: 	}
175: 	if err := sqlStore.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
176: 		t.Fatalf("sqlite CreateMessage failed: %v", err)
177: 	}
178: 	// Create MCP servers for both
179: 	mdServer, err := NewServer(mdStore)
180: 	if err != nil {
181: 		t.Fatalf("NewServer (markdown) failed: %v", err)
182: 	}
183: 	sqlServer, err := NewServer(sqlStore)
184: 	if err != nil {
185: 		t.Fatalf("NewServer (sqlite) failed: %v", err)
186: 	}
187: 	ctx := context.Background()
188: 	// Compare list_topics
189: 	mdTopicsResult, _ := mdServer.handleListTopics(ctx, makeToolRequest(map[string]interface{}{}))
190: 	sqlTopicsResult, _ := sqlServer.handleListTopics(ctx, makeToolRequest(map[string]interface{}{}))
191: 	mdTopicsJSON := extractTextContent(t, mdTopicsResult)
192: 	sqlTopicsJSON := extractTextContent(t, sqlTopicsResult)
193: 	var mdTopics, sqlTopics []map[string]interface{}
194: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(mdTopicsJSON), &mdTopics); err != nil {
195: 		t.Fatalf("unmarshal markdown topics: %v", err)
196: 	}
197: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sqlTopicsJSON), &sqlTopics); err != nil {
198: 		t.Fatalf("unmarshal sqlite topics: %v", err)
199: 	}
200: 	if len(mdTopics) != len(sqlTopics) {
201: 		t.Errorf("topic count mismatch: markdown=%d, sqlite=%d", len(mdTopics), len(sqlTopics))
202: 	}
203: 	// Compare list_threads
204: 	mdThreadsResult, _ := mdServer.handleListThreads(ctx, makeToolRequest(map[string]interface{}{"topic": "test-parity"}))
205: 	sqlThreadsResult, _ := sqlServer.handleListThreads(ctx, makeToolRequest(map[string]interface{}{"topic": "test-parity"}))
206: 	mdThreadsJSON := extractTextContent(t, mdThreadsResult)
207: 	sqlThreadsJSON := extractTextContent(t, sqlThreadsResult)
208: 	var mdThreads, sqlThreads []map[string]interface{}
209: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(mdThreadsJSON), &mdThreads); err != nil {
210: 		t.Fatalf("unmarshal markdown threads: %v", err)
211: 	}
212: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sqlThreadsJSON), &sqlThreads); err != nil {
213: 		t.Fatalf("unmarshal sqlite threads: %v", err)
214: 	}
215: 	if len(mdThreads) != len(sqlThreads) {
216: 		t.Errorf("thread count mismatch: markdown=%d, sqlite=%d", len(mdThreads), len(sqlThreads))
217: 	}
218: 	// Verify key fields match (subject, sticky, etc.)
219: 	if len(mdThreads) > 0 && len(sqlThreads) > 0 {
220: 		if mdThreads[0]["subject"] != sqlThreads[0]["subject"] {
221: 			t.Errorf("thread subject mismatch: markdown=%v, sqlite=%v",
222: 				mdThreads[0]["subject"], sqlThreads[0]["subject"])
223: 		}
224: 		if mdThreads[0]["sticky"] != sqlThreads[0]["sticky"] {
225: 			t.Errorf("thread sticky mismatch: markdown=%v, sqlite=%v",
226: 				mdThreads[0]["sticky"], sqlThreads[0]["sticky"])
227: 		}
228: 	}
229: 	// Compare list_messages
230: 	mdMsgsResult, _ := mdServer.handleListMessages(ctx, makeToolRequest(map[string]interface{}{"thread": thread.ID.String()}))
231: 	sqlMsgsResult, _ := sqlServer.handleListMessages(ctx, makeToolRequest(map[string]interface{}{"thread": thread.ID.String()}))
232: 	mdMsgsJSON := extractTextContent(t, mdMsgsResult)
233: 	sqlMsgsJSON := extractTextContent(t, sqlMsgsResult)
234: 	var mdMsgs, sqlMsgs []map[string]interface{}
235: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(mdMsgsJSON), &mdMsgs); err != nil {
236: 		t.Fatalf("unmarshal markdown messages: %v", err)
237: 	}
238: 	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sqlMsgsJSON), &sqlMsgs); err != nil {
239: 		t.Fatalf("unmarshal sqlite messages: %v", err)
240: 	}
241: 	if len(mdMsgs) != len(sqlMsgs) {
242: 		t.Errorf("message count mismatch: markdown=%d, sqlite=%d", len(mdMsgs), len(sqlMsgs))
243: 	}
244: 	if len(mdMsgs) > 0 && len(sqlMsgs) > 0 {
245: 		if mdMsgs[0]["content"] != sqlMsgs[0]["content"] {
246: 			t.Errorf("message content mismatch: markdown=%v, sqlite=%v",
247: 				mdMsgs[0]["content"], sqlMsgs[0]["content"])
248: 		}
249: 		if mdMsgs[0]["created_by"] != sqlMsgs[0]["created_by"] {
250: 			t.Errorf("message created_by mismatch: markdown=%v, sqlite=%v",
251: 				mdMsgs[0]["created_by"], sqlMsgs[0]["created_by"])
252: 		}
253: 	}
254: }
255: // mustResolveTopicID resolves a topic name to its UUID via the store.
256: func mustResolveTopicID(t *testing.T, store storage.Storage, name string) models.UUID {
257: 	t.Helper()
258: 	topic, err := store.ResolveTopic(name)
259: 	if err != nil {
260: 		t.Fatalf("resolve topic %q: %v", name, err)
261: 	}
262: 	return topic.ID
263: }
264: // extractTextContent gets the text string from an MCP CallToolResult.
265: func extractTextContent(t *testing.T, result *mcp.CallToolResult) string {
266: 	t.Helper()
267: 	if result == nil || len(result.Content) == 0 {
268: 		t.Fatal("result has no content")
269: 	}
270: 	tc, ok := result.Content[0].(*mcp.TextContent)
271: 	if !ok {
272: 		t.Fatalf("expected TextContent, got %T", result.Content[0])
273: 	}
274: 	return tc.Text
275: }

290: 		"message_id": msg.ID.String(),
291: 		"content":    "Edited via MCP",
292: 	})
293: 	result, err := server.handleEditMessage(ctx, editReq)
294: 	if err != nil {
295: 		t.Fatalf("handleEditMessage failed: %v", err)
296: 	}
297: 	if result.IsError {
298: 		t.Fatalf("handleEditMessage returned error: %v", result.Content)
299: 	}
300: 	// Verify edit was persisted
301: 	got, err := store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
302: 	if err != nil {
303: 		t.Fatalf("GetMessage failed: %v", err)
304: 	}
305: 	if got.Content != "Edited via MCP" {
306: 		t.Errorf("expected content 'Edited via MCP', got %q", got.Content)
307: 	}
308: 	if got.EditedAt == nil {
309: 		t.Error("expected EditedAt to be set after edit")
310: 	}
311: }

<file path="internal/storage/interface.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Storage interface for BBS data backends
 2: // ABOUTME: Defines the contract that all storage implementations must satisfy
 3: package storage
 4: import (
 5: 	"github.com/google/uuid"
 6: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 7: )
 8: // Storage defines the interface for BBS data persistence.
 9: // Implementations include SqliteStore and future backends like markdown files.
10: type Storage interface {
11: 	// Topic CRUD
12: 	CreateTopic(t *models.Topic) error
13: 	GetTopic(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Topic, error)
14: 	GetTopicByName(name string) (*models.Topic, error)
15: 	ListTopics(includeArchived bool) ([]*models.Topic, error)
16: 	UpdateTopic(t *models.Topic) error
17: 	DeleteTopic(id uuid.UUID) error
18: 	ArchiveTopic(id uuid.UUID, archived bool) error
19: 	// Thread CRUD
20: 	CreateThread(t *models.Thread) error
21: 	GetThread(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Thread, error)
22: 	ListThreads(topicID uuid.UUID) ([]*models.Thread, error)
23: 	UpdateThread(t *models.Thread) error
24: 	DeleteThread(id uuid.UUID) error
25: 	SetThreadSticky(id uuid.UUID, sticky bool) error
26: 	// Message CRUD
27: 	CreateMessage(m *models.Message) error
28: 	GetMessage(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Message, error)
29: 	ListMessages(threadID uuid.UUID) ([]*models.Message, error)
30: 	UpdateMessage(m *models.Message) error
31: 	DeleteMessage(id uuid.UUID) error
32: 	// Attachment CRUD
33: 	CreateAttachment(a *models.Attachment) error
34: 	GetAttachment(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Attachment, error)
35: 	ListAttachments(messageID uuid.UUID) ([]*models.Attachment, error)
36: 	DeleteAttachment(id uuid.UUID) error
37: 	// Resolution helpers
38: 	ResolveTopic(idOrName string) (*models.Topic, error)
39: 	ResolveThread(idPrefix string) (*models.Thread, error)
40: 	ResolveMessage(idPrefix string) (*models.Message, error)
41: 	// Close releases resources held by the storage backend.
42: 	Close() error
43: }
</file>

<file path="internal/storage/markdown_resolve.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: Resolution helpers for MarkdownStore
  2: // ABOUTME: Implements fuzzy lookup by full UUID, name, or UUID prefix for topics, threads, and messages
  3: package storage
  4: import (
  5: 	"fmt"
  6: 	"os"
  7: 	"path/filepath"
  8: 	"strings"
  9: 	"github.com/google/uuid"
 10: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 11: )
 12: // ResolveTopic finds a topic by ID, ID prefix, or name.
 13: func (s *MarkdownStore) ResolveTopic(idOrName string) (*models.Topic, error) {
 14: 	// Try as full UUID first
 15: 	if id, err := uuid.Parse(idOrName); err == nil {
 16: 		return s.GetTopic(id)
 17: 	}
 18: 	// Try by name
 19: 	if topic, err := s.GetTopicByName(idOrName); err == nil {
 20: 		return topic, nil
 21: 	}
 22: 	// Try as ID prefix
 23: 	topics, err := s.ListTopics(true)
 24: 	if err != nil {
 25: 		return nil, err
 26: 	}
 27: 	var matches []*models.Topic
 28: 	for _, t := range topics {
 29: 		if strings.HasPrefix(t.ID.String(), idOrName) {
 30: 			matches = append(matches, t)
 31: 		}
 32: 	}
 33: 	switch len(matches) {
 34: 	case 0:
 35: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("topic not found: %s", idOrName)
 36: 	case 1:
 37: 		return matches[0], nil
 38: 	default:
 39: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("ambiguous topic ID prefix '%s' matches %d topics", idOrName, len(matches))
 40: 	}
 41: }
 42: // ResolveThread finds a thread by ID or ID prefix.
 43: func (s *MarkdownStore) ResolveThread(idPrefix string) (*models.Thread, error) {
 44: 	// Try as full UUID first
 45: 	if id, err := uuid.Parse(idPrefix); err == nil {
 46: 		return s.GetThread(id)
 47: 	}
 48: 	// Try as ID prefix - scan all threads across all topics
 49: 	entries, err := s.readTopics()
 50: 	if err != nil {
 51: 		return nil, err
 52: 	}
 53: 	var matches []*models.Thread
 54: 	for _, e := range entries {
 55: 		topicDir := s.topicDirPath(e.Name)
 56: 		dirEntries, err := os.ReadDir(topicDir)
 57: 		if err != nil {
 58: 			continue
 59: 		}
 60: 		for _, de := range dirEntries {
 61: 			if de.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(de.Name(), ".md") {
 62: 				continue
 63: 			}
 64: 			fp := filepath.Join(topicDir, de.Name())
 65: 			fm, err := readThreadFrontmatter(fp)
 66: 			if err != nil {
 67: 				continue
 68: 			}
 69: 			if strings.HasPrefix(fm.ID, idPrefix) {
 70: 				threadID, err := uuid.Parse(fm.ID)
 71: 				if err != nil {
 72: 					continue
 73: 				}
 74: 				thread, err := s.readThreadFromFile(fp, threadID)
 75: 				if err != nil {
 76: 					continue
 77: 				}
 78: 				matches = append(matches, thread)
 79: 			}
 80: 		}
 81: 	}
 82: 	switch len(matches) {
 83: 	case 0:
 84: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("thread not found: %s", idPrefix)
 85: 	case 1:
 86: 		return matches[0], nil
 87: 	default:
 88: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("ambiguous thread ID prefix '%s' matches %d threads", idPrefix, len(matches))
 89: 	}
 90: }
 91: // ResolveMessage finds a message by ID or ID prefix.
 92: func (s *MarkdownStore) ResolveMessage(idPrefix string) (*models.Message, error) {
 93: 	// Try as full UUID first
 94: 	if id, err := uuid.Parse(idPrefix); err == nil {
 95: 		return s.GetMessage(id)
 96: 	}
 97: 	// Try as ID prefix - scan all messages across all topics and threads
 98: 	matches, err := s.findMessagesByPrefix(idPrefix)
 99: 	if err != nil {
100: 		return nil, err
101: 	}
102: 	switch len(matches) {
103: 	case 0:
104: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("message not found: %s", idPrefix)
105: 	case 1:
106: 		return matches[0], nil
107: 	default:
108: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("ambiguous message ID prefix '%s' matches %d messages", idPrefix, len(matches))
109: 	}
110: }
111: // findMessagesByPrefix scans all thread files for messages matching an ID prefix.
112: func (s *MarkdownStore) findMessagesByPrefix(idPrefix string) ([]*models.Message, error) {
113: 	entries, err := s.readTopics()
114: 	if err != nil {
115: 		return nil, err
116: 	}
117: 	var matches []*models.Message
118: 	for _, e := range entries {
119: 		topicDir := s.topicDirPath(e.Name)
120: 		dirEntries, err := os.ReadDir(topicDir)
121: 		if err != nil {
122: 			continue
123: 		}
124: 		for _, de := range dirEntries {
125: 			if de.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(de.Name(), ".md") {
126: 				continue
127: 			}
128: 			found := s.findMessagesInFile(filepath.Join(topicDir, de.Name()), idPrefix)
129: 			matches = append(matches, found...)
130: 		}
131: 	}
132: 	return matches, nil
133: }
134: // findMessagesInFile searches a single thread file for messages matching an ID prefix.
135: func (s *MarkdownStore) findMessagesInFile(fp string, idPrefix string) []*models.Message {
136: 	data, err := os.ReadFile(fp)
137: 	if err != nil {
138: 		return nil
139: 	}
140: 	fm, err := parseThreadFrontmatter(string(data))
141: 	if err != nil {
142: 		return nil
143: 	}
144: 	threadID, err := uuid.Parse(fm.ID)
145: 	if err != nil {
146: 		return nil
147: 	}
148: 	messages := parseThreadMessages(string(data))
149: 	var matches []*models.Message
150: 	for _, msg := range messages {
151: 		if strings.HasPrefix(msg.ID.String(), idPrefix) {
152: 			matches = append(matches, &models.Message{
153: 				ID:        msg.ID,
154: 				ThreadID:  threadID,
155: 				Content:   msg.Content,
156: 				CreatedAt: msg.CreatedAt,
157: 				CreatedBy: msg.CreatedBy,
158: 				EditedAt:  msg.EditedAt,
159: 			})
160: 		}
161: 	}
162: 	return matches
163: }
</file>

<file path="internal/storage/migrate.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Data migration between BBS storage backends
 2: // ABOUTME: Copies topics, threads, messages, and attachments from source to destination
 3: package storage
 4: import (
 5: 	"fmt"
 6: 	"os"
 7: 	"github.com/google/uuid"
 8: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 9: )
10: // MigrateSummary holds counts of migrated entities.
11: type MigrateSummary struct {
12: 	Topics      int
13: 	Threads     int
14: 	Messages    int
15: 	Attachments int
16: }
17: // MigrateData copies all data from src to dst storage.
18: // It iterates through topics, threads, messages, and attachments in order,
19: // creating each entity in the destination. The destination should be empty
20: // before calling this function.
21: func MigrateData(src, dst Storage) (*MigrateSummary, error) {
22: 	summary := &MigrateSummary{}
23: 	// List all topics (including archived)
24: 	topics, err := src.ListTopics(true)
25: 	if err != nil {
26: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list source topics: %w", err)
27: 	}
28: 	for _, topic := range topics {
29: 		if err := dst.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
30: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("create topic %q: %w", topic.Name, err)
31: 		}
32: 		summary.Topics++
33: 		if err := migrateTopic(src, dst, topic, summary); err != nil {
34: 			return nil, err
35: 		}
36: 	}
37: 	return summary, nil
38: }
39: // migrateTopic copies all threads (and their messages/attachments) for a single topic.
40: func migrateTopic(src, dst Storage, topic *models.Topic, summary *MigrateSummary) error {
41: 	threads, err := src.ListThreads(topic.ID)
42: 	if err != nil {
43: 		return fmt.Errorf("list threads for topic %q: %w", topic.Name, err)
44: 	}
45: 	for _, thread := range threads {
46: 		if err := dst.CreateThread(thread); err != nil {
47: 			return fmt.Errorf("create thread %q in topic %q: %w", thread.Subject, topic.Name, err)
48: 		}
49: 		summary.Threads++
50: 		if err := migrateThread(src, dst, thread, summary); err != nil {
51: 			return err
52: 		}
53: 	}
54: 	return nil
55: }
56: // migrateThread copies all messages (and their attachments) for a single thread.
57: func migrateThread(src, dst Storage, thread *models.Thread, summary *MigrateSummary) error {
58: 	messages, err := src.ListMessages(thread.ID)
59: 	if err != nil {
60: 		return fmt.Errorf("list messages for thread %q: %w", thread.Subject, err)
61: 	}
62: 	for _, msg := range messages {
63: 		if err := dst.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
64: 			return fmt.Errorf("create message %s in thread %q: %w", msg.ID, thread.Subject, err)
65: 		}
66: 		summary.Messages++
67: 		if err := migrateAttachments(src, dst, msg.ID, summary); err != nil {
68: 			return err
69: 		}
70: 	}
71: 	return nil
72: }
73: // migrateAttachments copies all attachments for a single message.
74: func migrateAttachments(src, dst Storage, msgID uuid.UUID, summary *MigrateSummary) error {
75: 	attachments, err := src.ListAttachments(msgID)
76: 	if err != nil {
77: 		return fmt.Errorf("list attachments for message %s: %w", msgID, err)
78: 	}
79: 	for _, att := range attachments {
80: 		if err := dst.CreateAttachment(att); err != nil {
81: 			return fmt.Errorf("create attachment %q for message %s: %w", att.Filename, msgID, err)
82: 		}
83: 		summary.Attachments++
84: 	}
85: 	return nil
86: }
87: // IsDirNonEmpty checks whether a directory exists and contains any files or subdirectories.
88: // Returns false if the directory does not exist or is empty.
89: func IsDirNonEmpty(path string) (bool, error) {
90: 	entries, err := os.ReadDir(path)
91: 	if err != nil {
92: 		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
93: 			return false, nil
94: 		}
95: 		return false, fmt.Errorf("read directory %q: %w", path, err)
96: 	}
97: 	return len(entries) > 0, nil
98: }
</file>
