<file path="CHARM_REMOVAL_PLAN.md">
  1: # BBS - Charm Removal Plan
  2:
  3: ## Charmbracelet Dependencies
  4:
  5: **KEEP (TUI):**
  6: - `github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea v1.3.10` - TUI framework
  7: - `github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss v1.1.0` - TUI styling
  8:
  9: **REMOVE:**
 10: - `github.com/charmbracelet/charm` (2389-research fork) - KV sync, auth
 11:
 12: ## Files with Charm Imports
 13:
 14: | File | Packages | Purpose |
 15: |------|----------|---------|
 16: | `internal/charm/charm.go` | `charm/client`, `charm/kv` | Core KV operations |
 17: | `internal/charm/config.go` | `charm/kv` | Stale threshold config |
 18: | `cmd/bbs/sync.go` | `charm/client`, `charm/kv`, `charm/ui/link` | Sync commands |
 19:
 20: ## Files Using internal/charm (Need Import Updates)
 21:
 22: - `cmd/bbs/root.go`, `topic.go`, `thread.go`, `post.go`, `whoami.go`
 23: - `internal/tui/app.go`, `topics.go`, `threads.go`, `messages.go`
 24: - `internal/mcp/server.go`, `tools.go`
 25:
 26: ## Removal Strategy
 27:
 28: ### Phase 1: Create SQLite Store
 29:
 30: New package: `internal/storage/storage.go` (standardized across suite)
 31:
 32: **Schema:**
 33: ```sql
 34: CREATE TABLE topics (
 35:     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 36:     name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
 37:     description TEXT,
 38:     created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
 39:     created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
 40:     archived INTEGER DEFAULT 0
 41: );
 42:
 43: CREATE TABLE threads (
 44:     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 45:     topic_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES topics(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 46:     subject TEXT NOT NULL,
 47:     created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
 48:     created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
 49:     updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,  -- For sorting by activity
 50:     sticky INTEGER DEFAULT 0
 51: );
 52:
 53: CREATE TABLE messages (
 54:     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 55:     thread_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES threads(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 56:     content TEXT NOT NULL,
 57:     created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
 58:     created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
 59:     edited_at DATETIME
 60: );
 61:
 62: CREATE TABLE attachments (
 63:     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 64:     message_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES messages(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 65:     filename TEXT NOT NULL,
 66:     mime_type TEXT NOT NULL,
 67:     data BLOB NOT NULL,
 68:     created_at DATETIME NOT NULL
 69: );
 70: ```
 71:
 72: ### Phase 2: Add Export Commands
 73:
 74: ```bash
 75: bbs export markdown [path]  # Human-readable
 76: bbs export yaml [path]      # Structured backup
 77: bbs export json [path]      # Full backup
 78: bbs import yaml [path]      # Restore
 79: ```
 80:
 81: **Markdown Format:**
 82: ```markdown
 83: # Topic: General Discussion
 84: Welcome to the board!
 85:
 86: ## Thread: Hello World
 87: *by Pixel Blaster on 2026-01-31*
 88:
 89: ### The Harpoonator - 2026-01-31
 90: First post!
 91: ```
 92:
 93: **YAML Format:**
 94: ```yaml
 95: version: "1.0"
 96: exported_at: "2026-01-31T12:00:00Z"
 97: tool: "bbs"
 98:
 99: topics:
100:   - id: uuid
101:     name: "General Discussion"
102:     threads:
103:       - id: uuid
104:         subject: "Hello World"
105:         messages:
106:           - id: uuid
107:             content: "First post!"
108:             created_by: "The Harpoonator"
109: ```
110:
111: ### Phase 3: Migration Tool
112:
113: ```bash
114: bbs migrate  # One-time Charm KV -> SQLite migration
115: ```
116:
117: ## Files to Modify
118:
119: ### DELETE:
120: - `internal/charm/charm.go`
121: - `internal/charm/config.go`
122: - `internal/charm/resolve.go`
123: - `internal/charm/charm_test.go`
124: - `cmd/bbs/sync.go`
125:
126: ### CREATE:
127: - `internal/storage/storage.go` - SQLite implementation
128: - `internal/storage/storage_test.go`
129: - `cmd/bbs/export.go` - Export/import commands
130: - `cmd/bbs/migrate.go` - Migration utility
131:
132: ### MODIFY:
133: - `go.mod` - Remove charm, keep bubbletea/lipgloss
134: - `cmd/bbs/root.go` - Use store instead of charm
135: - `cmd/bbs/topic.go` - Update import
136: - `cmd/bbs/thread.go` - Update import
137: - `cmd/bbs/post.go` - Update import
138: - `cmd/bbs/whoami.go` - Remove Charm ID display
139: - `internal/tui/app.go` - Update import
140: - `internal/tui/topics.go` - Update import
141: - `internal/tui/threads.go` - Update import
142: - `internal/tui/messages.go` - Update import
143: - `internal/mcp/server.go` - Update import
144: - `internal/config/config.go` - Remove Charm settings
145:
146: ## go.mod Changes
147:
148: **Remove:**
149: ```go
150: replace github.com/charmbracelet/charm => github.com/2389-research/charm v0.20.0
151: require github.com/charmbracelet/charm v0.0.0
152: ```
153:
154: **Keep:**
155: ```go
156: require (
157:     github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea v1.3.10
158:     github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss v1.1.0
159: )
160: ```
161:
162: **Add:**
163: ```go
164: require modernc.org/sqlite v1.41.0
165: ```
166:
167: ## Data Path Changes
168:
169: | Current | New |
170: |---------|-----|
171: | `~/.local/share/charm/kv/bbs/` | `~/.local/share/bbs/bbs.db` |
172: | `~/.config/bbs/charm.json` | `~/.config/bbs/config.json` |
173: | `~/.config/bbs/sync.json` | (removed) |
174:
175: ## Implementation Order
176:
177: 1. Create `internal/storage/storage.go` with SQLite
178: 2. Create store tests
179: 3. Create export commands
180: 4. Create migration utility
181: 5. Update all imports from `internal/charm` to `internal/storage`
182: 6. Update root.go for new store init
183: 7. Remove Charm config settings
184: 8. Delete `internal/charm/`
185: 9. Delete `cmd/bbs/sync.go`
186: 10. Update go.mod
187: 11. Run `go mod tidy`
</file>

<file path="cmd/bbs/skill.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Install Claude Code skill for bbs
 2: // ABOUTME: Embeds and installs the skill definition to ~/.claude/skills/
 3: package main
 4: import (
 5: 	"bufio"
 6: 	"embed"
 7: 	"fmt"
 8: 	"os"
 9: 	"path/filepath"
10: 	"strings"
11: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
12: )
13: //go:embed skill/SKILL.md
14: var skillFS embed.FS
15: var skillSkipConfirm bool
16: var installSkillCmd = &cobra.Command{
17: 	Use:   "install-skill",
18: 	Short: "Install Claude Code skill",
19: 	Long: `Install the bbs skill for Claude Code.
20: This copies the skill definition to ~/.claude/skills/bbs/
21: so Claude Code can use bbs commands contextually.`,
22: 	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
23: 		return installSkill()
24: 	},
25: }
26: func init() {
27: 	installSkillCmd.Flags().BoolVarP(&skillSkipConfirm, "yes", "y", false, "Skip confirmation prompt")
28: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(installSkillCmd)
29: }
30: func installSkill() error {
31: 	// Determine destination
32: 	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
33: 	if err != nil {
34: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
35: 	}
36: 	skillDir := filepath.Join(home, ".claude", "skills", "bbs")
37: 	skillPath := filepath.Join(skillDir, "SKILL.md")
38: 	// Show explanation
39: 	fmt.Println("┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐")
40: 	fmt.Println("│               BBS Skill for Claude Code                     │")
41: 	fmt.Println("└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘")
42: 	fmt.Println()
43: 	fmt.Println("This will install the bbs skill, enabling Claude Code to:")
44: 	fmt.Println()
45: 	fmt.Println("  • Create and manage discussion topics")
46: 	fmt.Println("  • Post and reply to threaded messages")
47: 	fmt.Println("  • Collaborate with other agents and humans")
48: 	fmt.Println("  • Use the /bbs slash command")
49: 	fmt.Println()
50: 	fmt.Println("Destination:")
51: 	fmt.Printf("  %s\n", skillPath)
52: 	fmt.Println()
53: 	// Check if already installed
54: 	if _, err := os.Stat(skillPath); err == nil {
55: 		fmt.Println("Note: A skill file already exists and will be overwritten.")
56: 		fmt.Println()
57: 	}
58: 	// Ask for confirmation unless --yes flag is set
59: 	if !skillSkipConfirm {
60: 		fmt.Print("Install the bbs skill? [y/N] ")
61: 		reader := bufio.NewReader(os.Stdin)
62: 		response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
63: 		if err != nil {
64: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to read response: %w", err)
65: 		}
66: 		response = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(response))
67: 		if response != "y" && response != "yes" {
68: 			fmt.Println("Installation cancelled.")
69: 			return nil
70: 		}
71: 		fmt.Println()
72: 	}
73: 	// Read embedded skill file
74: 	content, err := skillFS.ReadFile("skill/SKILL.md")
75: 	if err != nil {
76: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read embedded skill: %w", err)
77: 	}
78: 	// Create directory
79: 	if err := os.MkdirAll(skillDir, 0755); err != nil {
80: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create skill directory: %w", err)
81: 	}
82: 	// Write skill file
83: 	if err := os.WriteFile(skillPath, content, 0644); err != nil {
84: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write skill file: %w", err)
85: 	}
86: 	fmt.Println("✓ Installed bbs skill successfully!")
87: 	fmt.Println()
88: 	fmt.Println("Claude Code will now recognize /bbs commands.")
89: 	fmt.Println("Try asking Claude: \"Start a discussion about the new feature\" or \"Check the board\"")
90: 	return nil
91: }
</file>

<file path="internal/identity/identity_test.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Tests for identity resolution
 2: // ABOUTME: Verifies username@source format handling
 3: package identity
 4: import (
 5: 	"os"
 6: 	"testing"
 7: )
 8: func TestGetIdentity(t *testing.T) {
 9: 	tests := []struct {
10: 		name     string
11: 		override string
12: 		source   string
13: 		want     string
14: 	}{
15: 		{"with override", "mybot", "cli", "mybot@cli"},
16: 		{"without override", "", "mcp", os.Getenv("USER") + "@mcp"},
17: 	}
18: 	for _, tt := range tests {
19: 		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
20: 			got := GetIdentity(tt.override, tt.source)
21: 			if got != tt.want {
22: 				t.Errorf("GetIdentity() = %v, want %v", got, tt.want)
23: 			}
24: 		})
25: 	}
26: }
27: func TestParseIdentity(t *testing.T) {
28: 	user, source := ParseIdentity("harper@cli")
29: 	if user != "harper" {
30: 		t.Errorf("expected user 'harper', got '%s'", user)
31: 	}
32: 	if source != "cli" {
33: 		t.Errorf("expected source 'cli', got '%s'", source)
34: 	}
35: }
36: func TestParseIdentityNoAt(t *testing.T) {
37: 	user, source := ParseIdentity("justusername")
38: 	if user != "justusername" {
39: 		t.Errorf("expected user 'justusername', got '%s'", user)
40: 	}
41: 	if source != "unknown" {
42: 		t.Errorf("expected source 'unknown', got '%s'", source)
43: 	}
44: }
45: func TestGetIdentityWithBBSUser(t *testing.T) {
46: 	t.Setenv("BBS_USER", "bbsuser")
47: 	// Clear USER to ensure BBS_USER is used
48: 	originalUser := os.Getenv("USER")
49: 	t.Setenv("USER", "")
50: 	defer t.Setenv("USER", originalUser)
51: 	got := GetIdentity("", "cli")
52: 	if got != "bbsuser@cli" {
53: 		t.Errorf("GetIdentity() = %v, want bbsuser@cli", got)
54: 	}
55: }
56: func TestGetIdentityAnonymous(t *testing.T) {
57: 	// Clear both USER and BBS_USER
58: 	t.Setenv("BBS_USER", "")
59: 	t.Setenv("USER", "")
60: 	got := GetIdentity("", "cli")
61: 	if got != "anonymous@cli" {
62: 		t.Errorf("GetIdentity() = %v, want anonymous@cli", got)
63: 	}
64: }
65: func TestParseIdentityMultipleAts(t *testing.T) {
66: 	// Test with multiple @ symbols - should only split on first
67: 	user, source := ParseIdentity("user@domain@source")
68: 	if user != "user" {
69: 		t.Errorf("expected user 'user', got '%s'", user)
70: 	}
71: 	if source != "domain@source" {
72: 		t.Errorf("expected source 'domain@source', got '%s'", source)
73: 	}
74: }

<file path="internal/mcp/prompts.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: MCP prompt templates
 2: // ABOUTME: Guided workflows for common tasks
 3: package mcp
 4: import (
 5: 	"context"
 6: 	"fmt"
 7: 	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
 8: )
 9: func (s *Server) registerPrompts() {
10: 	s.mcp.AddPrompt(&mcp.Prompt{
11: 		Name:        "post-update",
12: 		Description: "Post a status update to a topic",
13: 		Arguments: []*mcp.PromptArgument{
14: 			{Name: "topic", Description: "Topic to post to", Required: true},
15: 			{Name: "subject", Description: "Thread subject", Required: true},
16: 		},
17: 	}, s.handlePostUpdatePrompt)
18: 	s.mcp.AddPrompt(&mcp.Prompt{
19: 		Name:        "summarize-thread",
20: 		Description: "Summarize a thread discussion",
21: 		Arguments: []*mcp.PromptArgument{
22: 			{Name: "thread", Description: "Thread ID to summarize", Required: true},
23: 		},
24: 	}, s.handleSummarizePrompt)
25: }
26: func (s *Server) handlePostUpdatePrompt(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.GetPromptRequest) (*mcp.GetPromptResult, error) {
27: 	topic := req.Params.Arguments["topic"]
28: 	subject := req.Params.Arguments["subject"]
29: 	return &mcp.GetPromptResult{
30: 		Description: fmt.Sprintf("Post update to %s: %s", topic, subject),
31: 		Messages: []*mcp.PromptMessage{
32: 			{
33: 				Role: "user",
34: 				Content: &mcp.TextContent{
35: 					Text: fmt.Sprintf(`Post a status update to the BBS.
36: Topic: %s
37: Subject: %s
38: Please use the create_thread tool with your update message. Keep it concise and informative.`, topic, subject),
39: 				},
40: 			},
41: 		},
42: 	}, nil
43: }
44: func (s *Server) handleSummarizePrompt(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.GetPromptRequest) (*mcp.GetPromptResult, error) {
45: 	threadID := req.Params.Arguments["thread"]
46: 	return &mcp.GetPromptResult{
47: 		Description: fmt.Sprintf("Summarize thread %s", threadID),
48: 		Messages: []*mcp.PromptMessage{
49: 			{
50: 				Role: "user",
51: 				Content: &mcp.TextContent{
52: 					Text: fmt.Sprintf(`Please summarize the discussion in thread %s.
53: First, use the list_messages tool to read the thread, then provide a concise summary of:
54: 1. The main topic/question
55: 2. Key points discussed
56: 3. Any conclusions or action items`, threadID),
57: 				},
58: 			},
59: 		},
60: 	}, nil
61: }
</file>

<file path="internal/storage/migrate_test.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: Tests for storage migration between backends
  2: // ABOUTME: Covers sqlite-to-markdown, markdown-to-sqlite, data integrity, and error cases
  3: package storage
  4: import (
  5: 	"os"
  6: 	"path/filepath"
  7: 	"strings"
  8: 	"testing"
  9: 	"time"
 10: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 11: )
 12: // seedTestData populates a storage backend with a representative data set
 13: // and returns the counts and IDs for verification.
 14: func seedTestData(t *testing.T, src Storage) (topics []*models.Topic, threads []*models.Thread, messages []*models.Message, attachments []*models.Attachment) {
 15: 	t.Helper()
 16: 	// Create two topics, one archived
 17: 	topic1 := models.NewTopic("general", "General discussion", "admin@cli")
 18: 	topic2 := models.NewTopic("dev", "Development talk", "admin@cli")
 19: 	topic2.Archived = true
 20: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateTopic(topic1))
 21: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateTopic(topic2))
 22: 	topics = append(topics, topic1, topic2)
 23: 	// Create threads in topic1
 24: 	thread1 := models.NewThread(topic1.ID, "Welcome Thread", "admin@cli")
 25: 	thread1.Sticky = true
 26: 	thread2 := models.NewThread(topic1.ID, "Random Chat", "user@cli")
 27: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(thread1))
 28: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(thread2))
 29: 	// Create a thread in topic2
 30: 	thread3 := models.NewThread(topic2.ID, "Build Failures", "ci@mcp")
 31: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(thread3))
 32: 	threads = append(threads, thread1, thread2, thread3)
 33: 	// Create messages
 34: 	msg1 := models.NewMessage(thread1.ID, "Welcome to the BBS!", "admin@cli")
 35: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
 36: 	msg2 := models.NewMessage(thread1.ID, "Thanks for the welcome.", "user@cli")
 37: 	now := time.Now()
 38: 	msg2.EditedAt = &now
 39: 	msg3 := models.NewMessage(thread2.ID, "Anyone here?", "user@cli")
 40: 	msg4 := models.NewMessage(thread3.ID, "Build is red on main.", "ci@mcp")
 41: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg1))
 42: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg2))
 43: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg3))
 44: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg4))
 45: 	messages = append(messages, msg1, msg2, msg3, msg4)
 46: 	// Create attachments
 47: 	att1 := models.NewAttachment(msg4.ID, "build.log", "text/plain", []byte("ERROR: test failure on line 42"))
 48: 	att2 := models.NewAttachment(msg1.ID, "rules.txt", "text/plain", []byte("Be kind. Have fun."))
 49: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateAttachment(att1))
 50: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateAttachment(att2))
 51: 	attachments = append(attachments, att1, att2)
 52: 	return
 53: }
 54: func mustNoErr(t *testing.T, err error) {
 55: 	t.Helper()
 56: 	if err != nil {
 57: 		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
 58: 	}
 59: }
 60: // verifyMigratedData checks that the destination storage contains all expected data.
 61: func verifyMigratedData(t *testing.T, dst Storage, topics []*models.Topic, threads []*models.Thread, messages []*models.Message, attachments []*models.Attachment) {
 62: 	t.Helper()
 63: 	verifyMigratedTopics(t, dst, topics)
 64: 	verifyMigratedThreads(t, dst, threads)
 65: 	verifyMigratedMessages(t, dst, messages)
 66: 	verifyMigratedAttachments(t, dst, attachments)
 67: }
 68: func verifyMigratedTopics(t *testing.T, dst Storage, topics []*models.Topic) {
 69: 	t.Helper()
 70: 	for _, orig := range topics {
 71: 		got, err := dst.GetTopic(orig.ID)
 72: 		if err != nil {
 73: 			t.Errorf("topic %s (%s) not found in destination: %v", orig.Name, orig.ID, err)
 74: 			continue
 75: 		}
 76: 		if got.Name != orig.Name {
 77: 			t.Errorf("topic name mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.Name, got.Name)
 78: 		}
 79: 		if got.Description != orig.Description {
 80: 			t.Errorf("topic description mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.Description, got.Description)
 81: 		}
 82: 		if got.CreatedBy != orig.CreatedBy {
 83: 			t.Errorf("topic createdBy mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.CreatedBy, got.CreatedBy)
 84: 		}
 85: 		if got.Archived != orig.Archived {
 86: 			t.Errorf("topic archived mismatch: want %v, got %v", orig.Archived, got.Archived)
 87: 		}
 88: 	}
 89: }
 90: func verifyMigratedThreads(t *testing.T, dst Storage, threads []*models.Thread) {
 91: 	t.Helper()
 92: 	for _, orig := range threads {
 93: 		got, err := dst.GetThread(orig.ID)
 94: 		if err != nil {
 95: 			t.Errorf("thread %s (%s) not found in destination: %v", orig.Subject, orig.ID, err)
 96: 			continue
 97: 		}
 98: 		if got.Subject != orig.Subject {
 99: 			t.Errorf("thread subject mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.Subject, got.Subject)
100: 		}
101: 		if got.TopicID != orig.TopicID {
102: 			t.Errorf("thread topicID mismatch: want %s, got %s", orig.TopicID, got.TopicID)
103: 		}
104: 		if got.CreatedBy != orig.CreatedBy {
105: 			t.Errorf("thread createdBy mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.CreatedBy, got.CreatedBy)
106: 		}
107: 		if got.Sticky != orig.Sticky {
108: 			t.Errorf("thread sticky mismatch: want %v, got %v", orig.Sticky, got.Sticky)
109: 		}
110: 	}
111: }
112: func verifyMigratedMessages(t *testing.T, dst Storage, messages []*models.Message) {
113: 	t.Helper()
114: 	for _, orig := range messages {
115: 		got, err := dst.GetMessage(orig.ID)
116: 		if err != nil {
117: 			t.Errorf("message %s not found in destination: %v", orig.ID, err)
118: 			continue
119: 		}
120: 		if got.Content != orig.Content {
121: 			t.Errorf("message content mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.Content, got.Content)
122: 		}
123: 		if got.ThreadID != orig.ThreadID {
124: 			t.Errorf("message threadID mismatch: want %s, got %s", orig.ThreadID, got.ThreadID)
125: 		}
126: 		if got.CreatedBy != orig.CreatedBy {
127: 			t.Errorf("message createdBy mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.CreatedBy, got.CreatedBy)
128: 		}
129: 		if (orig.EditedAt == nil) != (got.EditedAt == nil) {
130: 			t.Errorf("message editedAt nil mismatch: orig=%v, got=%v", orig.EditedAt, got.EditedAt)
131: 		}
132: 	}
133: }
134: func verifyMigratedAttachments(t *testing.T, dst Storage, attachments []*models.Attachment) {
135: 	t.Helper()
136: 	for _, orig := range attachments {
137: 		got, err := dst.GetAttachment(orig.ID)
138: 		if err != nil {
139: 			t.Errorf("attachment %s (%s) not found in destination: %v", orig.Filename, orig.ID, err)
140: 			continue
141: 		}
142: 		if got.Filename != orig.Filename {
143: 			t.Errorf("attachment filename mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.Filename, got.Filename)
144: 		}
145: 		if got.MimeType != orig.MimeType {
146: 			t.Errorf("attachment mimetype mismatch: want %q, got %q", orig.MimeType, got.MimeType)
147: 		}
148: 		if string(got.Data) != string(orig.Data) {
149: 			t.Errorf("attachment data mismatch: want %q, got %q", string(orig.Data), string(got.Data))
150: 		}
151: 		if got.MessageID != orig.MessageID {
152: 			t.Errorf("attachment messageID mismatch: want %s, got %s", orig.MessageID, got.MessageID)
153: 		}
154: 	}
155: }
156: func TestMigrateData_SqliteToMarkdown(t *testing.T) {
157: 	// Set up source (sqlite)
158: 	srcDir := t.TempDir()
159: 	src, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(srcDir, "bbs.db"))
160: 	if err != nil {
161: 		t.Fatalf("create source store: %v", err)
162: 	}
163: 	defer src.Close()
164: 	topics, threads, messages, attachments := seedTestData(t, src)
165: 	// Set up destination (markdown)
166: 	dstDir := t.TempDir()
167: 	dst, err := NewMarkdownStore(dstDir)
168: 	if err != nil {
169: 		t.Fatalf("create destination store: %v", err)
170: 	}
171: 	defer dst.Close()
172: 	// Run migration
173: 	summary, err := MigrateData(src, dst)
174: 	if err != nil {
175: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData failed: %v", err)
176: 	}
177: 	// Verify summary counts
178: 	if summary.Topics != len(topics) {
179: 		t.Errorf("summary topics: want %d, got %d", len(topics), summary.Topics)
180: 	}
181: 	if summary.Threads != len(threads) {
182: 		t.Errorf("summary threads: want %d, got %d", len(threads), summary.Threads)
183: 	}
184: 	if summary.Messages != len(messages) {
185: 		t.Errorf("summary messages: want %d, got %d", len(messages), summary.Messages)
186: 	}
187: 	if summary.Attachments != len(attachments) {
188: 		t.Errorf("summary attachments: want %d, got %d", len(attachments), summary.Attachments)
189: 	}
190: 	// Verify all data was migrated correctly
191: 	verifyMigratedData(t, dst, topics, threads, messages, attachments)
192: }

230: func TestMigrateData_EmptySource(t *testing.T) {
231: 	// Set up empty source (sqlite)
232: 	srcDir := t.TempDir()
233: 	src, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(srcDir, "bbs.db"))
234: 	if err != nil {
235: 		t.Fatalf("create source store: %v", err)
236: 	}
237: 	defer src.Close()
238: 	// Set up destination (markdown)
239: 	dstDir := t.TempDir()
240: 	dst, err := NewMarkdownStore(dstDir)
241: 	if err != nil {
242: 		t.Fatalf("create destination store: %v", err)
243: 	}
244: 	defer dst.Close()
245: 	summary, err := MigrateData(src, dst)
246: 	if err != nil {
247: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData failed: %v", err)
248: 	}
249: 	if summary.Topics != 0 || summary.Threads != 0 || summary.Messages != 0 || summary.Attachments != 0 {
250: 		t.Errorf("expected all zero counts for empty source, got topics=%d threads=%d messages=%d attachments=%d",
251: 			summary.Topics, summary.Threads, summary.Messages, summary.Attachments)
252: 	}
253: }
254: func TestIsDirNonEmpty(t *testing.T) {
255: 	// Empty directory
256: 	emptyDir := t.TempDir()
257: 	nonEmpty, err := IsDirNonEmpty(emptyDir)
258: 	if err != nil {
259: 		t.Fatalf("IsDirNonEmpty on empty dir: %v", err)
260: 	}
261: 	if nonEmpty {
262: 		t.Error("expected empty dir to be reported as empty")
263: 	}
264: 	// Non-empty directory
265: 	nonEmptyDir := t.TempDir()
266: 	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(nonEmptyDir, "file.txt"), []byte("data"), 0644); err != nil {
267: 		t.Fatalf("create file: %v", err)
268: 	}
269: 	nonEmpty, err = IsDirNonEmpty(nonEmptyDir)
270: 	if err != nil {
271: 		t.Fatalf("IsDirNonEmpty on non-empty dir: %v", err)
272: 	}
273: 	if !nonEmpty {
274: 		t.Error("expected non-empty dir to be reported as non-empty")
275: 	}
276: 	// Non-existent directory
277: 	nonEmpty, err = IsDirNonEmpty(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "does-not-exist"))
278: 	if err != nil {
279: 		t.Fatalf("IsDirNonEmpty on non-existent dir: %v", err)
280: 	}
281: 	if nonEmpty {
282: 		t.Error("expected non-existent dir to be reported as empty")
283: 	}
284: }
285: func TestMigrateData_SqliteToSqlite(t *testing.T) {
286: 	// Test migrating between two sqlite instances (roundtrip sanity check)
287: 	srcDir := t.TempDir()
288: 	src, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(srcDir, "bbs.db"))
289: 	if err != nil {
290: 		t.Fatalf("create source store: %v", err)
291: 	}
292: 	defer src.Close()
293: 	topics, threads, messages, attachments := seedTestData(t, src)
294: 	dstDir := t.TempDir()
295: 	dst, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(dstDir, "bbs.db"))
296: 	if err != nil {
297: 		t.Fatalf("create destination store: %v", err)
298: 	}
299: 	defer dst.Close()
300: 	summary, err := MigrateData(src, dst)
301: 	if err != nil {
302: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData failed: %v", err)
303: 	}
304: 	if summary.Topics != len(topics) {
305: 		t.Errorf("summary topics: want %d, got %d", len(topics), summary.Topics)
306: 	}
307: 	verifyMigratedData(t, dst, topics, threads, messages, attachments)
308: }
309: // --- Round-trip Tests ---
310: func TestMigrateRoundTrip_SqliteToMarkdownToSqlite(t *testing.T) {
311: 	// Phase 1: Create rich data in SQLite
312: 	srcDir := t.TempDir()
313: 	original, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(srcDir, "original.db"))
314: 	if err != nil {
315: 		t.Fatalf("create original store: %v", err)
316: 	}
317: 	defer original.Close()
318: 	topics, threads, messages, attachments := seedRichTestData(t, original)
319: 	// Phase 2: Migrate SQLite -> Markdown
320: 	mdDir := t.TempDir()
321: 	mdStore, err := NewMarkdownStore(mdDir)
322: 	if err != nil {
323: 		t.Fatalf("create markdown store: %v", err)
324: 	}
325: 	defer mdStore.Close()
326: 	summary1, err := MigrateData(original, mdStore)
327: 	if err != nil {
328: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData (sqlite->markdown) failed: %v", err)
329: 	}
330: 	if summary1.Topics != len(topics) || summary1.Threads != len(threads) ||
331: 		summary1.Messages != len(messages) || summary1.Attachments != len(attachments) {
332: 		t.Errorf("phase 1 summary mismatch: topics=%d/%d threads=%d/%d messages=%d/%d attachments=%d/%d",
333: 			summary1.Topics, len(topics), summary1.Threads, len(threads),
334: 			summary1.Messages, len(messages), summary1.Attachments, len(attachments))
335: 	}
336: 	// Phase 3: Migrate Markdown -> new SQLite
337: 	dstDir := t.TempDir()
338: 	final, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(dstDir, "final.db"))
339: 	if err != nil {
340: 		t.Fatalf("create final store: %v", err)
341: 	}
342: 	defer final.Close()
343: 	summary2, err := MigrateData(mdStore, final)
344: 	if err != nil {
345: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData (markdown->sqlite) failed: %v", err)
346: 	}
347: 	if summary2.Topics != len(topics) || summary2.Threads != len(threads) ||
348: 		summary2.Messages != len(messages) || summary2.Attachments != len(attachments) {
349: 		t.Errorf("phase 2 summary mismatch: topics=%d/%d threads=%d/%d messages=%d/%d attachments=%d/%d",
350: 			summary2.Topics, len(topics), summary2.Threads, len(threads),
351: 			summary2.Messages, len(messages), summary2.Attachments, len(attachments))
352: 	}
353: 	// Phase 4: Field-by-field verification against original data
354: 	verifyMigratedData(t, final, topics, threads, messages, attachments)
355: }
356: func TestMigrateRoundTrip_MarkdownToSqliteToMarkdown(t *testing.T) {
357: 	// Phase 1: Create data in Markdown
358: 	srcDir := t.TempDir()
359: 	original, err := NewMarkdownStore(srcDir)
360: 	if err != nil {
361: 		t.Fatalf("create original store: %v", err)
362: 	}
363: 	defer original.Close()
364: 	topics, threads, messages, attachments := seedRichTestData(t, original)
365: 	// Phase 2: Migrate Markdown -> SQLite
366: 	sqlDir := t.TempDir()
367: 	sqlStore, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(sqlDir, "mid.db"))
368: 	if err != nil {
369: 		t.Fatalf("create sqlite store: %v", err)
370: 	}
371: 	defer sqlStore.Close()
372: 	_, err = MigrateData(original, sqlStore)
373: 	if err != nil {
374: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData (markdown->sqlite) failed: %v", err)
375: 	}
376: 	// Phase 3: Migrate SQLite -> new Markdown
377: 	dstDir := t.TempDir()
378: 	final, err := NewMarkdownStore(dstDir)
379: 	if err != nil {
380: 		t.Fatalf("create final store: %v", err)
381: 	}
382: 	defer final.Close()
383: 	_, err = MigrateData(sqlStore, final)
384: 	if err != nil {
385: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData (sqlite->markdown) failed: %v", err)
386: 	}
387: 	// Phase 4: Verify all data
388: 	verifyMigratedData(t, final, topics, threads, messages, attachments)
389: }
390: // seedRichTestData creates a richer data set than seedTestData, including
391: // special characters, multiple messages with edits, and binary attachments.
392: func seedRichTestData(t *testing.T, src Storage) (topics []*models.Topic, threads []*models.Thread, messages []*models.Message, attachments []*models.Attachment) {
393: 	t.Helper()
394: 	// Topics with special characters in descriptions
395: 	topic1 := models.NewTopic("general", "General discussion & Q/A", "admin@cli")
396: 	topic2 := models.NewTopic("dev-ops", "CI/CD, deployments, & infrastructure", "admin@cli")
397: 	topic2.Archived = true
398: 	topic3 := models.NewTopic("random", "Off-topic chatter", "admin@cli")
399: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateTopic(topic1))
400: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateTopic(topic2))
401: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateTopic(topic3))
402: 	topics = append(topics, topic1, topic2, topic3)
403: 	// Threads with various configurations
404: 	thread1 := models.NewThread(topic1.ID, "Welcome Thread", "admin@cli")
405: 	thread1.Sticky = true
406: 	thread2 := models.NewThread(topic1.ID, "Random Chat", "user@cli")
407: 	thread3 := models.NewThread(topic2.ID, "Build Failures", "ci@mcp")
408: 	thread4 := models.NewThread(topic3.ID, "Favorite Foods", "user@cli")
409: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(thread1))
410: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(thread2))
411: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(thread3))
412: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(thread4))
413: 	threads = append(threads, thread1, thread2, thread3, thread4)
414: 	// Messages with special content
415: 	msg1 := models.NewMessage(thread1.ID, "Welcome to the BBS! Rules:\n1. Be kind\n2. Have fun", "admin@cli")
416: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
417: 	msg2 := models.NewMessage(thread1.ID, "Thanks for the welcome!", "user@cli")
418: 	now := time.Now()
419: 	msg2.EditedAt = &now
420: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
421: 	msg3 := models.NewMessage(thread2.ID, "Content with `backticks` and **bold** and _italic_", "user@cli")
422: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
423: 	msg4 := models.NewMessage(thread3.ID, "Build is red:\n```\nERROR: test failure on line 42\n```", "ci@mcp")
424: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
425: 	msg5 := models.NewMessage(thread4.ID, "Pizza > everything", "user@cli")
426: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
427: 	msg6 := models.NewMessage(thread1.ID, "Message with special chars: <>&\"'", "user2@cli")
428: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg1))
429: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg2))
430: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg3))
431: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg4))
432: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg5))
433: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg6))
434: 	messages = append(messages, msg1, msg2, msg3, msg4, msg5, msg6)
435: 	// Attachments including binary-like data
436: 	att1 := models.NewAttachment(msg4.ID, "build.log", "text/plain", []byte("ERROR: test failure on line 42\nFAILED: 3 tests"))
437: 	att2 := models.NewAttachment(msg1.ID, "rules.txt", "text/plain", []byte("Be kind. Have fun."))
438: 	att3 := models.NewAttachment(msg4.ID, "screenshot.bin", "application/octet-stream", []byte{0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47, 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A})
439: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateAttachment(att1))
440: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateAttachment(att2))
441: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateAttachment(att3))
442: 	attachments = append(attachments, att1, att2, att3)
443: 	return
444: }
445: func TestMigrateData_PreservesThreadOrdering(t *testing.T) {
446: 	// Create data in SQLite with specific sticky/non-sticky threads
447: 	srcDir := t.TempDir()
448: 	src, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(srcDir, "bbs.db"))
449: 	if err != nil {
450: 		t.Fatalf("create source store: %v", err)
451: 	}
452: 	defer src.Close()
453: 	topic := models.NewTopic("test-ordering", "Test ordering", "test@cli")
454: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateTopic(topic))
455: 	// Create threads: first normal, then sticky
456: 	normalThread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Normal Thread", "test@cli")
457: 	stickyThread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Sticky Thread", "test@cli")
458: 	stickyThread.Sticky = true
459: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(normalThread))
460: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(stickyThread))
461: 	// Migrate to markdown
462: 	dstDir := t.TempDir()
463: 	dst, err := NewMarkdownStore(dstDir)
464: 	if err != nil {
465: 		t.Fatalf("create destination store: %v", err)
466: 	}
467: 	defer dst.Close()
468: 	_, err = MigrateData(src, dst)
469: 	if err != nil {
470: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData failed: %v", err)
471: 	}
472: 	// Verify ordering: sticky should come first
473: 	threads, err := dst.ListThreads(topic.ID)
474: 	if err != nil {
475: 		t.Fatalf("ListThreads failed: %v", err)
476: 	}
477: 	if len(threads) != 2 {
478: 		t.Fatalf("expected 2 threads, got %d", len(threads))
479: 	}
480: 	if !threads[0].Sticky {
481: 		t.Error("sticky thread should be first in listing")
482: 	}
483: 	if threads[1].Sticky {
484: 		t.Error("normal thread should not be sticky")
485: 	}
486: }
487: func TestMigrateData_PreservesMessageOrdering(t *testing.T) {
488: 	// Verify messages arrive in created_at order after migration
489: 	srcDir := t.TempDir()
490: 	src, err := NewSqliteStore(filepath.Join(srcDir, "bbs.db"))
491: 	if err != nil {
492: 		t.Fatalf("create source store: %v", err)
493: 	}
494: 	defer src.Close()
495: 	topic := models.NewTopic("msg-order", "Message order test", "test@cli")
496: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateTopic(topic))
497: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Ordered Thread", "test@cli")
498: 	mustNoErr(t, src.CreateThread(thread))
499: 	// Create messages with defined order
500: 	for i := 0; i < 5; i++ {
501: 		time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
502: 		msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, strings.Repeat("x", i+1), "test@cli")
503: 		mustNoErr(t, src.CreateMessage(msg))
504: 	}
505: 	// Migrate to markdown
506: 	dstDir := t.TempDir()
507: 	dst, err := NewMarkdownStore(dstDir)
508: 	if err != nil {
509: 		t.Fatalf("create destination store: %v", err)
510: 	}
511: 	defer dst.Close()
512: 	_, err = MigrateData(src, dst)
513: 	if err != nil {
514: 		t.Fatalf("MigrateData failed: %v", err)
515: 	}
516: 	// Verify ordering
517: 	messages, err := dst.ListMessages(thread.ID)
518: 	if err != nil {
519: 		t.Fatalf("ListMessages failed: %v", err)
520: 	}
521: 	if len(messages) != 5 {
522: 		t.Fatalf("expected 5 messages, got %d", len(messages))
523: 	}
524: 	for i := 0; i < len(messages)-1; i++ {
525: 		if !messages[i].CreatedAt.Before(messages[i+1].CreatedAt) {
526: 			t.Errorf("messages not in order: msg[%d].CreatedAt=%v >= msg[%d].CreatedAt=%v",
527: 				i, messages[i].CreatedAt, i+1, messages[i+1].CreatedAt)
528: 		}
529: 	}
530: }
</file>

<file path="internal/storage/storage.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: SQLite storage layer for BBS data
  2: // ABOUTME: Implements CRUD operations for topics, threads, messages, attachments
  3: package storage
  4: import (
  5: 	"database/sql"
  6: 	"fmt"
  7: 	"os"
  8: 	"path/filepath"
  9: 	"strings"
 10: 	"time"
 11: 	"github.com/google/uuid"
 12: 	_ "modernc.org/sqlite"
 13: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 14: )
 15: // SqliteStore provides SQLite-backed storage for BBS data.
 16: type SqliteStore struct {
 17: 	db *sql.DB
 18: }
 19: // Compile-time check that SqliteStore implements Storage.
 20: var _ Storage = (*SqliteStore)(nil)
 21: // schema defines the database schema
 22: const schema = `
 23: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS topics (
 24:     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 25:     name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
 26:     description TEXT,
 27:     created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
 28:     created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
 29:     archived INTEGER DEFAULT 0
 30: );
 31: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS threads (
 32:     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 33:     topic_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES topics(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 34:     subject TEXT NOT NULL,
 35:     created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
 36:     created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
 37:     updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
 38:     sticky INTEGER DEFAULT 0
 39: );
 40: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
 41:     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 42:     thread_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES threads(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 43:     content TEXT NOT NULL,
 44:     created_at DATETIME NOT NULL,
 45:     created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
 46:     edited_at DATETIME
 47: );
 48: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attachments (
 49:     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 50:     message_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES messages(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 51:     filename TEXT NOT NULL,
 52:     mime_type TEXT NOT NULL,
 53:     data BLOB NOT NULL,
 54:     created_at DATETIME NOT NULL
 55: );
 56: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_threads_topic_id ON threads(topic_id);
 57: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_threads_updated_at ON threads(updated_at);
 58: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_thread_id ON messages(thread_id);
 59: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attachments_message_id ON attachments(message_id);
 60: `
 61: // NewSqliteStore creates a new SQLite-backed store.
 62: func NewSqliteStore(dbPath string) (*SqliteStore, error) {
 63: 	// Ensure directory exists
 64: 	dir := filepath.Dir(dbPath)
 65: 	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0750); err != nil {
 66: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create database directory: %w", err)
 67: 	}
 68: 	db, err := sql.Open("sqlite", dbPath)
 69: 	if err != nil {
 70: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
 71: 	}
 72: 	// Enable foreign keys and WAL mode
 73: 	if _, err := db.Exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON"); err != nil {
 74: 		db.Close()
 75: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("enable foreign keys: %w", err)
 76: 	}
 77: 	if _, err := db.Exec("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL"); err != nil {
 78: 		db.Close()
 79: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("enable WAL mode: %w", err)
 80: 	}
 81: 	// Create schema
 82: 	if _, err := db.Exec(schema); err != nil {
 83: 		db.Close()
 84: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create schema: %w", err)
 85: 	}
 86: 	return &SqliteStore{db: db}, nil
 87: }
 88: // Close closes the database connection.
 89: func (s *SqliteStore) Close() error {
 90: 	return s.db.Close()
 91: }
 92: // Topic CRUD
 93: // CreateTopic stores a new topic.
 94: func (s *SqliteStore) CreateTopic(t *models.Topic) error {
 95: 	_, err := s.db.Exec(
 96: 		`INSERT INTO topics (id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived)
 97: 		 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
 98: 		t.ID.String(), t.Name, t.Description, t.CreatedAt.UTC(), t.CreatedBy, boolToInt(t.Archived),
 99: 	)
100: 	if err != nil {
101: 		return fmt.Errorf("insert topic: %w", err)
102: 	}
103: 	return nil
104: }
105: // GetTopic retrieves a topic by ID.
106: func (s *SqliteStore) GetTopic(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Topic, error) {
107: 	var t models.Topic
108: 	var idStr string
109: 	var archived int
110: 	var createdAt string
111: 	err := s.db.QueryRow(
112: 		`SELECT id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived FROM topics WHERE id = ?`,
113: 		id.String(),
114: 	).Scan(&idStr, &t.Name, &t.Description, &createdAt, &t.CreatedBy, &archived)
115: 	if err == sql.ErrNoRows {
116: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("topic not found: %s", id)
117: 	}
118: 	if err != nil {
119: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query topic: %w", err)
120: 	}
121: 	t.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
122: 	t.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
123: 	t.Archived = archived != 0
124: 	return &t, nil
125: }
126: // GetTopicByName finds a topic by its name.
127: func (s *SqliteStore) GetTopicByName(name string) (*models.Topic, error) {
128: 	var t models.Topic
129: 	var idStr string
130: 	var archived int
131: 	var createdAt string
132: 	err := s.db.QueryRow(
133: 		`SELECT id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived FROM topics WHERE name = ?`,
134: 		name,
135: 	).Scan(&idStr, &t.Name, &t.Description, &createdAt, &t.CreatedBy, &archived)
136: 	if err == sql.ErrNoRows {
137: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("topic not found: %s", name)
138: 	}
139: 	if err != nil {
140: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query topic: %w", err)
141: 	}
142: 	t.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
143: 	t.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
144: 	t.Archived = archived != 0
145: 	return &t, nil
146: }
147: // UpdateTopic updates an existing topic.
148: func (s *SqliteStore) UpdateTopic(t *models.Topic) error {
149: 	_, err := s.db.Exec(
150: 		`UPDATE topics SET name = ?, description = ?, archived = ? WHERE id = ?`,
151: 		t.Name, t.Description, boolToInt(t.Archived), t.ID.String(),
152: 	)
153: 	if err != nil {
154: 		return fmt.Errorf("update topic: %w", err)
155: 	}
156: 	return nil
157: }
158: // DeleteTopic deletes a topic (cascades to threads).
159: func (s *SqliteStore) DeleteTopic(id uuid.UUID) error {
160: 	_, err := s.db.Exec(`DELETE FROM topics WHERE id = ?`, id.String())
161: 	if err != nil {
162: 		return fmt.Errorf("delete topic: %w", err)
163: 	}
164: 	return nil
165: }
166: // ListTopics returns all topics, optionally including archived ones.
167: func (s *SqliteStore) ListTopics(includeArchived bool) ([]*models.Topic, error) {
168: 	query := `SELECT id, name, description, created_at, created_by, archived FROM topics`
169: 	if !includeArchived {
170: 		query += ` WHERE archived = 0`
171: 	}
172: 	query += ` ORDER BY name`
173: 	rows, err := s.db.Query(query)
174: 	if err != nil {
175: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query topics: %w", err)
176: 	}
177: 	defer rows.Close()
178: 	var topics []*models.Topic
179: 	for rows.Next() {
180: 		var t models.Topic
181: 		var idStr string
182: 		var archived int
183: 		var createdAt string
184: 		if err := rows.Scan(&idStr, &t.Name, &t.Description, &createdAt, &t.CreatedBy, &archived); err != nil {
185: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan topic: %w", err)
186: 		}
187: 		t.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
188: 		t.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
189: 		t.Archived = archived != 0
190: 		topics = append(topics, &t)
191: 	}
192: 	return topics, rows.Err()
193: }
194: // ArchiveTopic sets the archived status of a topic.

196: 	_, err := s.db.Exec(`UPDATE topics SET archived = ? WHERE id = ?`, boolToInt(archived), id.String())
197: 	if err != nil {
198: 		return fmt.Errorf("archive topic: %w", err)
199: 	}
200: 	return nil
201: }

257: // ListThreads returns all threads for a topic, sorted by sticky then updated_at.
258: func (s *SqliteStore) ListThreads(topicID uuid.UUID) ([]*models.Thread, error) {
259: 	rows, err := s.db.Query(
260: 		`SELECT id, topic_id, subject, created_at, created_by, updated_at, sticky
261: 		 FROM threads WHERE topic_id = ?
262: 		 ORDER BY sticky DESC, updated_at DESC`,
263: 		topicID.String(),
264: 	)
265: 	if err != nil {
266: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query threads: %w", err)
267: 	}
268: 	defer rows.Close()
269: 	var threads []*models.Thread
270: 	for rows.Next() {
271: 		var t models.Thread
272: 		var idStr, topicIDStr string
273: 		var sticky int
274: 		var createdAt, updatedAt string
275: 		if err := rows.Scan(&idStr, &topicIDStr, &t.Subject, &createdAt, &t.CreatedBy, &updatedAt, &sticky); err != nil {
276: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan thread: %w", err)
277: 		}
278: 		t.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
279: 		t.TopicID, _ = uuid.Parse(topicIDStr)
280: 		t.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
281: 		t.UpdatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, updatedAt)
282: 		t.Sticky = sticky != 0
283: 		threads = append(threads, &t)
284: 	}
285: 	return threads, rows.Err()
286: }
287: // SetThreadSticky sets the sticky status of a thread.
288: func (s *SqliteStore) SetThreadSticky(id uuid.UUID, sticky bool) error {
289: 	_, err := s.db.Exec(`UPDATE threads SET sticky = ? WHERE id = ?`, boolToInt(sticky), id.String())
290: 	if err != nil {
291: 		return fmt.Errorf("set thread sticky: %w", err)
292: 	}
293: 	return nil
294: }
295: // Message CRUD
296: // CreateMessage stores a new message.
297: func (s *SqliteStore) CreateMessage(m *models.Message) error {
298: 	var editedAt interface{}
299: 	if m.EditedAt != nil {
300: 		editedAt = m.EditedAt.UTC()
301: 	}
302: 	_, err := s.db.Exec(
303: 		`INSERT INTO messages (id, thread_id, content, created_at, created_by, edited_at)
304: 		 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
305: 		m.ID.String(), m.ThreadID.String(), m.Content, m.CreatedAt.UTC(), m.CreatedBy, editedAt,
306: 	)
307: 	if err != nil {
308: 		return fmt.Errorf("insert message: %w", err)
309: 	}
310: 	// Update thread's updated_at
311: 	_, err = s.db.Exec(`UPDATE threads SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`, time.Now().UTC(), m.ThreadID.String())
312: 	if err != nil {
313: 		return fmt.Errorf("update thread timestamp: %w", err)
314: 	}
315: 	return nil
316: }
317: // GetMessage retrieves a message by ID.
318: func (s *SqliteStore) GetMessage(id uuid.UUID) (*models.Message, error) {
319: 	var m models.Message
320: 	var idStr, threadIDStr string
321: 	var createdAt string
322: 	var editedAt sql.NullString
323: 	err := s.db.QueryRow(
324: 		`SELECT id, thread_id, content, created_at, created_by, edited_at FROM messages WHERE id = ?`,
325: 		id.String(),
326: 	).Scan(&idStr, &threadIDStr, &m.Content, &createdAt, &m.CreatedBy, &editedAt)
327: 	if err == sql.ErrNoRows {
328: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("message not found: %s", id)
329: 	}
330: 	if err != nil {
331: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query message: %w", err)
332: 	}
333: 	m.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
334: 	m.ThreadID, _ = uuid.Parse(threadIDStr)
335: 	m.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
336: 	if editedAt.Valid {
337: 		t, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, editedAt.String)
338: 		m.EditedAt = &t
339: 	}
340: 	return &m, nil
341: }
342: // UpdateMessage updates an existing message.
343: func (s *SqliteStore) UpdateMessage(m *models.Message) error {
344: 	var editedAt interface{}
345: 	if m.EditedAt != nil {
346: 		editedAt = m.EditedAt.UTC()
347: 	}
348: 	_, err := s.db.Exec(
349: 		`UPDATE messages SET content = ?, edited_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
350: 		m.Content, editedAt, m.ID.String(),
351: 	)
352: 	if err != nil {
353: 		return fmt.Errorf("update message: %w", err)
354: 	}
355: 	return nil
356: }
357: // DeleteMessage deletes a message (cascades to attachments).

359: 	_, err := s.db.Exec(`DELETE FROM messages WHERE id = ?`, id.String())
360: 	if err != nil {
361: 		return fmt.Errorf("delete message: %w", err)
362: 	}
363: 	return nil
364: }
365: // ListMessages returns all messages for a thread, sorted by created_at.
366: func (s *SqliteStore) ListMessages(threadID uuid.UUID) ([]*models.Message, error) {
367: 	rows, err := s.db.Query(
368: 		`SELECT id, thread_id, content, created_at, created_by, edited_at
369: 		 FROM messages WHERE thread_id = ?
370: 		 ORDER BY created_at ASC`,
371: 		threadID.String(),
372: 	)
373: 	if err != nil {
374: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query messages: %w", err)
375: 	}
376: 	defer rows.Close()
377: 	var messages []*models.Message
378: 	for rows.Next() {
379: 		var m models.Message
380: 		var idStr, threadIDStr string
381: 		var createdAt string
382: 		var editedAt sql.NullString
383: 		if err := rows.Scan(&idStr, &threadIDStr, &m.Content, &createdAt, &m.CreatedBy, &editedAt); err != nil {
384: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan message: %w", err)
385: 		}
386: 		m.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
387: 		m.ThreadID, _ = uuid.Parse(threadIDStr)
388: 		m.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
389: 		if editedAt.Valid {
390: 			t, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, editedAt.String)
391: 			m.EditedAt = &t
392: 		}
393: 		messages = append(messages, &m)
394: 	}
395: 	return messages, rows.Err()
396: }

438: // ListAttachments returns all attachments for a message.

443: 		 ORDER BY created_at ASC`,

458: 		a.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
459: 		a.MessageID, _ = uuid.Parse(messageIDStr)
460: 		a.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
461: 		attachments = append(attachments, &a)
462: 	}
463: 	return attachments, rows.Err()
464: }
465: // Resolution helpers
466: // ResolveTopic finds a topic by ID, ID prefix, or name.
467: func (s *SqliteStore) ResolveTopic(idOrName string) (*models.Topic, error) {
468: 	// Try as full UUID first
469: 	if id, err := uuid.Parse(idOrName); err == nil {
470: 		return s.GetTopic(id)
471: 	}
472: 	// Try by name
473: 	if topic, err := s.GetTopicByName(idOrName); err == nil {
474: 		return topic, nil
475: 	}
476: 	// Try as ID prefix
477: 	topics, err := s.ListTopics(true)
478: 	if err != nil {
479: 		return nil, err
480: 	}
481: 	var matches []*models.Topic
482: 	for _, t := range topics {
483: 		if strings.HasPrefix(t.ID.String(), idOrName) {
484: 			matches = append(matches, t)
485: 		}
486: 	}
487: 	switch len(matches) {
488: 	case 0:
489: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("topic not found: %s", idOrName)
490: 	case 1:
491: 		return matches[0], nil
492: 	default:
493: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("ambiguous topic ID prefix '%s' matches %d topics", idOrName, len(matches))
494: 	}
495: }
496: // ResolveThread finds a thread by ID or ID prefix.
497: func (s *SqliteStore) ResolveThread(idPrefix string) (*models.Thread, error) {
498: 	// Try as full UUID first
499: 	if id, err := uuid.Parse(idPrefix); err == nil {
500: 		return s.GetThread(id)
501: 	}
502: 	// Try as ID prefix - scan all threads
503: 	rows, err := s.db.Query(
504: 		`SELECT id, topic_id, subject, created_at, created_by, updated_at, sticky FROM threads WHERE id LIKE ?`,
505: 		idPrefix+"%",
506: 	)
507: 	if err != nil {
508: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query threads: %w", err)
509: 	}
510: 	defer rows.Close()
511: 	var matches []*models.Thread
512: 	for rows.Next() {
513: 		var t models.Thread
514: 		var idStr, topicIDStr string
515: 		var sticky int
516: 		var createdAt, updatedAt string
517: 		if err := rows.Scan(&idStr, &topicIDStr, &t.Subject, &createdAt, &t.CreatedBy, &updatedAt, &sticky); err != nil {
518: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan thread: %w", err)
519: 		}
520: 		t.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
521: 		t.TopicID, _ = uuid.Parse(topicIDStr)
522: 		t.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
523: 		t.UpdatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, updatedAt)
524: 		t.Sticky = sticky != 0
525: 		matches = append(matches, &t)
526: 	}
527: 	switch len(matches) {
528: 	case 0:
529: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("thread not found: %s", idPrefix)
530: 	case 1:
531: 		return matches[0], nil
532: 	default:
533: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("ambiguous thread ID prefix '%s' matches %d threads", idPrefix, len(matches))
534: 	}
535: }
536: // ResolveMessage finds a message by ID or ID prefix.
537: func (s *SqliteStore) ResolveMessage(idPrefix string) (*models.Message, error) {
538: 	// Try as full UUID first
539: 	if id, err := uuid.Parse(idPrefix); err == nil {
540: 		return s.GetMessage(id)
541: 	}
542: 	// Try as ID prefix - scan all messages
543: 	rows, err := s.db.Query(
544: 		`SELECT id, thread_id, content, created_at, created_by, edited_at FROM messages WHERE id LIKE ?`,
545: 		idPrefix+"%",
546: 	)
547: 	if err != nil {
548: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query messages: %w", err)
549: 	}
550: 	defer rows.Close()
551: 	var matches []*models.Message
552: 	for rows.Next() {
553: 		var m models.Message
554: 		var idStr, threadIDStr string
555: 		var createdAt string
556: 		var editedAt sql.NullString
557: 		if err := rows.Scan(&idStr, &threadIDStr, &m.Content, &createdAt, &m.CreatedBy, &editedAt); err != nil {
558: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan message: %w", err)
559: 		}
560: 		m.ID, _ = uuid.Parse(idStr)
561: 		m.ThreadID, _ = uuid.Parse(threadIDStr)
562: 		m.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, createdAt)
563: 		if editedAt.Valid {
564: 			t, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, editedAt.String)
565: 			m.EditedAt = &t
566: 		}
567: 		matches = append(matches, &m)
568: 	}
569: 	switch len(matches) {
570: 	case 0:
571: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("message not found: %s", idPrefix)
572: 	case 1:
573: 		return matches[0], nil
574: 	default:
575: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("ambiguous message ID prefix '%s' matches %d messages", idPrefix, len(matches))
576: 	}
577: }
578: // DataDir returns the default data directory path.
579: func DataDir() string {
580: 	dataHome := os.Getenv("XDG_DATA_HOME")
581: 	if dataHome == "" {
582: 		home, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
583: 		dataHome = filepath.Join(home, ".local", "share")
584: 	}
585: 	return filepath.Join(dataHome, "bbs")
586: }
587: // DefaultDBPath returns the default database path.
588: func DefaultDBPath() string {
589: 	return filepath.Join(DataDir(), "bbs.db")
590: }
591: // Helper function
592: func boolToInt(b bool) int {
593: 	if b {
594: 		return 1
595: 	}
596: 	return 0
597: }
</file>

<file path="CLAUDE.md">
 1: # BBS - Bulletin Board System
 2:
 3: A lightweight message board for humans and agents.
 4:
 5: ## Names
 6:
 7: - **Project Codename**: THUNDERBOARD 3000
 8: - **AI Assistant**: Pixel Blaster
 9: - **Human Lead**: The Harpoonator
10:
11: ## Architecture
12:
13: See `docs/plans/2025-12-15-bbs-design.md` for full design.
14:
15: Quick summary:
16: - Go CLI with Cobra
17: - SQLite storage (modernc.org/sqlite)
18: - Bubble Tea TUI
19: - MCP server for agent access
20: - Vault sync via suitesync/vault
21:
22: ## Development
23:
24: ```bash
25: # Run
26: go run ./cmd/bbs
27:
28: # Build
29: go build -o bin/bbs ./cmd/bbs
30:
31: # Test
32: go test ./...
33: ```
34:
35: ## Data Model
36:
37: Topics → Threads → Messages (+ Attachments)
38:
39: Identity format: `username@source` (cli, tui, mcp)
40:
41: ## Key Paths
42:
43: - App DB: `~/.local/share/bbs/bbs.db`
44: - Config: `~/.config/bbs/config.json`
45:
46: ## Export/Import
47:
48: ```bash
49: # Export data
50: bbs export markdown output.md
51: bbs export yaml output.yaml
52: bbs export json output.json
53:
54: # Import data
55: bbs import yaml backup.yaml
56: ```
</file>

12:

15: 	go tool cover -html=coverage.out -o coverage.html
16:
17: install:
18: 	go install ./cmd/bbs
19:
20: clean:
21: 	rm -f bbs coverage.out coverage.html
22: 	go clean
23:
24: .DEFAULT_GOAL := build

<file path="cmd/bbs/migrate.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: Migration command for converting BBS data between storage backends
  2: // ABOUTME: Supports sqlite-to-markdown and markdown-to-sqlite with safety checks
  3: package main
  4: import (
  5: 	"fmt"
  6: 	"path/filepath"
  7: 	"github.com/fatih/color"
  8: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
  9: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/config"
 10: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/storage"
 11: )
 12: var migrateCmd = &cobra.Command{
 13: 	Use:   "migrate",
 14: 	Short: "Migrate data between storage backends",
 15: 	Long: `Migrate all BBS data from the currently configured backend to a different backend.
 16: Reads topics, threads, messages, and attachments from the current backend
 17: and writes them to the target backend. Does NOT update the config file;
 18: verify the migration was successful then update config.json manually.
 19: Examples:
 20:   bbs migrate --to markdown
 21:   bbs migrate --to sqlite --data-dir ~/bbs-sqlite
 22:   bbs migrate --to markdown --force`,
 23: 	RunE: runMigrate,
 24: }
 25: var (
 26: 	migrateTo      string
 27: 	migrateDataDir string
 28: 	migrateForce   bool
 29: )
 30: func init() {
 31: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(migrateCmd)
 32: 	migrateCmd.Flags().StringVar(&migrateTo, "to", "", "target backend (sqlite or markdown)")
 33: 	migrateCmd.Flags().StringVar(&migrateDataDir, "data-dir", "", "target data directory (defaults to current config data_dir)")
 34: 	migrateCmd.Flags().BoolVar(&migrateForce, "force", false, "allow writing into a non-empty target directory")
 35: 	_ = migrateCmd.MarkFlagRequired("to")
 36: }
 37: func runMigrate(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
 38: 	// Load config and determine source backend
 39: 	cfg, err := config.Load()
 40: 	if err != nil {
 41: 		return fmt.Errorf("load config: %w", err)
 42: 	}
 43: 	sourceBackend := cfg.GetBackend()
 44: 	targetBackend := migrateTo
 45: 	// Validate target backend
 46: 	if targetBackend != "sqlite" && targetBackend != "markdown" {
 47: 		return fmt.Errorf("invalid target backend %q: must be \"sqlite\" or \"markdown\"", targetBackend)
 48: 	}
 49: 	if targetBackend == sourceBackend {
 50: 		return fmt.Errorf("target backend %q is the same as the current backend", targetBackend)
 51: 	}
 52: 	// Determine target data directory
 53: 	targetDataDir := cfg.GetDataDir()
 54: 	if migrateDataDir != "" {
 55: 		targetDataDir = config.ExpandPath(migrateDataDir)
 56: 	}
 57: 	// Check if target directory is non-empty
 58: 	nonEmpty, err := storage.IsDirNonEmpty(targetDataDir)
 59: 	if err != nil {
 60: 		return fmt.Errorf("check target directory: %w", err)
 61: 	}
 62: 	if nonEmpty && !migrateForce {
 63: 		return fmt.Errorf("target directory %q is not empty; use --force to overwrite", targetDataDir)
 64: 	}
 65: 	// Open source storage
 66: 	src, err := cfg.OpenStorage()
 67: 	if err != nil {
 68: 		return fmt.Errorf("open source storage (%s): %w", sourceBackend, err)
 69: 	}
 70: 	defer src.Close()
 71: 	// Open target storage
 72: 	dst, err := openStorage(targetBackend, targetDataDir)
 73: 	if err != nil {
 74: 		return fmt.Errorf("open target storage (%s): %w", targetBackend, err)
 75: 	}
 76: 	defer dst.Close()
 77: 	// Print plan
 78: 	color.Yellow("Migrating BBS data:")
 79: 	fmt.Printf("  Source:  %s (%s)\n", sourceBackend, cfg.GetDataDir())
 80: 	fmt.Printf("  Target:  %s (%s)\n", targetBackend, targetDataDir)
 81: 	fmt.Println()
 82: 	// Run migration
 83: 	summary, err := storage.MigrateData(src, dst)
 84: 	if err != nil {
 85: 		return fmt.Errorf("migration failed: %w", err)
 86: 	}
 87: 	// Print summary
 88: 	color.Green("Migration complete!")
 89: 	fmt.Printf("  Topics:      %d\n", summary.Topics)
 90: 	fmt.Printf("  Threads:     %d\n", summary.Threads)
 91: 	fmt.Printf("  Messages:    %d\n", summary.Messages)
 92: 	fmt.Printf("  Attachments: %d\n", summary.Attachments)
 93: 	fmt.Println()
 94: 	color.Yellow("Note: config.json was NOT updated. To switch to the new backend, edit:")
 95: 	fmt.Printf("  %s\n", config.GetConfigPath())
 96: 	fmt.Printf("  Set \"backend\": %q", targetBackend)
 97: 	if migrateDataDir != "" {
 98: 		fmt.Printf(" and \"data_dir\": %q", migrateDataDir)
 99: 	}
100: 	fmt.Println()
101: 	return nil
102: }
103: // openStorage creates a Storage implementation for the given backend and data directory.
104: func openStorage(backend, dataDir string) (storage.Storage, error) {
105: 	switch backend {
106: 	case "sqlite":
107: 		dbPath := filepath.Join(dataDir, "bbs.db")
108: 		return storage.NewSqliteStore(dbPath)
109: 	case "markdown":
110: 		return storage.NewMarkdownStore(dataDir)
111: 	default:
112: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown backend: %q", backend)
113: 	}
114: }
</file>

<file path="internal/models/models_test.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: Tests for BBS data models
  2: // ABOUTME: Verifies model creation, validation, and UUID parsing
  3: package models
  4: import (
  5: 	"testing"
  6: 	"time"
  7: 	"github.com/google/uuid"
  8: )
  9: func TestNewTopic(t *testing.T) {
 10: 	tests := []struct {
 11: 		name        string
 12: 		topicName   string
 13: 		description string
 14: 		createdBy   string
 15: 	}{
 16: 		{
 17: 			name:        "basic topic",
 18: 			topicName:   "general",
 19: 			description: "General discussion",
 20: 			createdBy:   "harper@cli",
 21: 		},
 22: 		{
 23: 			name:        "empty description",
 24: 			topicName:   "support",
 25: 			description: "",
 26: 			createdBy:   "agent@mcp",
 27: 		},
 28: 		{
 29: 			name:        "special characters",
 30: 			topicName:   "test-topic_123",
 31: 			description: "Topic with special chars: !@#$%",
 32: 			createdBy:   "user@tui",
 33: 		},
 34: 	}
 35: 	for _, tt := range tests {
 36: 		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
 37: 			topic := NewTopic(tt.topicName, tt.description, tt.createdBy)
 38: 			if topic.ID == uuid.Nil {
 39: 				t.Error("expected non-nil UUID")
 40: 			}
 41: 			if topic.Name != tt.topicName {
 42: 				t.Errorf("expected name %q, got %q", tt.topicName, topic.Name)
 43: 			}
 44: 			if topic.Description != tt.description {
 45: 				t.Errorf("expected description %q, got %q", tt.description, topic.Description)
 46: 			}
 47: 			if topic.CreatedBy != tt.createdBy {
 48: 				t.Errorf("expected createdBy %q, got %q", tt.createdBy, topic.CreatedBy)
 49: 			}
 50: 			if topic.Archived {
 51: 				t.Error("expected archived to be false")
 52: 			}
 53: 			if topic.CreatedAt.IsZero() {
 54: 				t.Error("expected CreatedAt to be set")
 55: 			}
 56: 		})
 57: 	}
 58: }
 59: func TestNewThread(t *testing.T) {
 60: 	tests := []struct {
 61: 		name      string
 62: 		subject   string
 63: 		createdBy string
 64: 	}{
 65: 		{
 66: 			name:      "basic thread",
 67: 			subject:   "Test subject",
 68: 			createdBy: "harper@cli",
 69: 		},
 70: 		{
 71: 			name:      "long subject",
 72: 			subject:   "This is a really long subject that might be used for detailed discussions about complex topics",
 73: 			createdBy: "claude@mcp",
 74: 		},
 75: 		{
 76: 			name:      "unicode subject",
 77: 			subject:   "Thread with emoji: 🎉 and unicode: 你好",
 78: 			createdBy: "user@tui",
 79: 		},
 80: 	}
 81: 	for _, tt := range tests {
 82: 		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
 83: 			topicID := uuid.New()
 84: 			thread := NewThread(topicID, tt.subject, tt.createdBy)
 85: 			if thread.ID == uuid.Nil {
 86: 				t.Error("expected non-nil UUID")
 87: 			}
 88: 			if thread.TopicID != topicID {
 89: 				t.Error("expected topicID to match")
 90: 			}
 91: 			if thread.Subject != tt.subject {
 92: 				t.Errorf("expected subject %q, got %q", tt.subject, thread.Subject)
 93: 			}
 94: 			if thread.CreatedBy != tt.createdBy {
 95: 				t.Errorf("expected createdBy %q, got %q", tt.createdBy, thread.CreatedBy)
 96: 			}
 97: 			if thread.UpdatedAt.IsZero() {
 98: 				t.Error("expected UpdatedAt to be set")
 99: 			}
100: 			if thread.UpdatedAt != thread.CreatedAt {
101: 				t.Error("expected UpdatedAt to equal CreatedAt on new thread")
102: 			}
103: 			if thread.Sticky {
104: 				t.Error("expected Sticky to be false")
105: 			}
106: 		})
107: 	}
108: }
109: func TestNewMessage(t *testing.T) {

113: 		createdBy string

144: 				t.Error("expected threadID to match")

153: 				t.Error("expected CreatedAt to be set")
154: 			}

156: 				t.Error("expected EditedAt to be nil for new message")
157: 			}

161: func TestNewAttachment(t *testing.T) {
162: 	tests := []struct {
163: 		name     string
164: 		filename string
165: 		mimeType string
166: 		data     []byte
167: 	}{
168: 		{
169: 			name:     "text file",
170: 			filename: "test.txt",
171: 			mimeType: "text/plain",
172: 			data:     []byte("test content"),
173: 		},
174: 		{
175: 			name:     "json file",
176: 			filename: "data.json",
177: 			mimeType: "application/json",
178: 			data:     []byte(`{"key": "value"}`),
179: 		},
180: 		{
181: 			name:     "binary data",
182: 			filename: "image.png",
183: 			mimeType: "image/png",
184: 			data:     []byte{0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47, 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A},
185: 		},
186: 		{
187: 			name:     "empty file",
188: 			filename: "empty.txt",
189: 			mimeType: "text/plain",
190: 			data:     []byte{},
191: 		},
192: 	}
193: 	for _, tt := range tests {
194: 		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
195: 			messageID := uuid.New()
196: 			att := NewAttachment(messageID, tt.filename, tt.mimeType, tt.data)
197: 			if att.ID == uuid.Nil {
198: 				t.Error("expected non-nil UUID")
199: 			}
200: 			if att.MessageID != messageID {
201: 				t.Error("expected messageID to match")
202: 			}
203: 			if att.Filename != tt.filename {
204: 				t.Errorf("expected filename %q, got %q", tt.filename, att.Filename)
205: 			}
206: 			if att.MimeType != tt.mimeType {
207: 				t.Errorf("expected mimeType %q, got %q", tt.mimeType, att.MimeType)
208: 			}
209: 			if string(att.Data) != string(tt.data) {
210: 				t.Errorf("expected data %v, got %v", tt.data, att.Data)
211: 			}
212: 			if att.CreatedAt.IsZero() {
213: 				t.Error("expected CreatedAt to be set")
214: 			}
215: 		})
216: 	}
217: }
218: func TestParseUUID(t *testing.T) {
219: 	tests := []struct {
220: 		name    string
221: 		input   string
222: 		wantErr bool
223: 	}{
224: 		{
225: 			name:    "valid UUID",
226: 			input:   "123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000",
227: 			wantErr: false,
228: 		},
229: 		{
230: 			name:    "generated UUID",
231: 			input:   uuid.New().String(),
232: 			wantErr: false,
233: 		},
234: 		{
235: 			name:    "uppercase UUID",
236: 			input:   "123E4567-E89B-12D3-A456-426614174000",
237: 			wantErr: false,
238: 		},
239: 		{
240: 			name:    "invalid UUID - too short",
241: 			input:   "123e4567-e89b-12d3",
242: 			wantErr: true,
243: 		},
244: 		{
245: 			name:    "invalid UUID - wrong format",
246: 			input:   "not-a-uuid",
247: 			wantErr: true,
248: 		},
249: 		{
250: 			name:    "empty string",
251: 			input:   "",
252: 			wantErr: true,
253: 		},
254: 	}
255: 	for _, tt := range tests {
256: 		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
257: 			result, err := ParseUUID(tt.input)
258: 			if tt.wantErr {
259: 				if err == nil {
260: 					t.Error("expected error")
261: 				}
262: 			} else {
263: 				if err != nil {
264: 					t.Errorf("unexpected error: %v", err)
265: 				}
266: 				if result == uuid.Nil {
267: 					t.Error("expected non-nil UUID")
268: 				}
269: 			}
270: 		})
271: 	}
272: }

277: 		t.Error("ID should not be nil")
278: 	}

280: 		t.Error("Name should not be empty")

283: 		t.Error("Description should not be empty")
284: 	}

286: 		t.Error("CreatedAt should be set")
287: 	}

289: 		t.Error("CreatedBy should not be empty")
290: 	}

294: 		t.Error("Archived should be modifiable")

300: 	// Test that fields are accessible
301: 	if thread.ID == uuid.Nil {
302: 		t.Error("ID should not be nil")
303: 	}

305: 		t.Error("TopicID should not be nil")
306: 	}

308: 		t.Error("Subject should not be empty")
309: 	}
310: 	if thread.CreatedAt.IsZero() {
311: 		t.Error("CreatedAt should be set")
312: 	}
313: 	if thread.UpdatedAt.IsZero() {
314: 		t.Error("UpdatedAt should be set")
315: 	}
316: 	if thread.CreatedBy == "" {
317: 		t.Error("CreatedBy should not be empty")
318: 	}

402: func TestTimestampPrecision(t *testing.T) {
403: 	before := time.Now()
404: 	topic := NewTopic("test", "desc", "user@cli")
405: 	after := time.Now()
406: 	if topic.CreatedAt.Before(before) {
407: 		t.Error("CreatedAt should not be before creation")
408: 	}
409: 	if topic.CreatedAt.After(after) {
410: 		t.Error("CreatedAt should not be after creation completed")
411: 	}
412: }

<file path="internal/models/models.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Core data models for topics, threads, messages, attachments
 2: // ABOUTME: Provides constructor functions for each model type
 3: package models
 4: import (
 5: 	"time"
 6: 	"github.com/google/uuid"
 7: )
 8: type UUID = uuid.UUID
 9: // ParseUUID parses a string into a UUID.
10: func ParseUUID(s string) (uuid.UUID, error) {
11: 	return uuid.Parse(s)
12: }
13: // Topic represents a message board category.
14: type Topic struct {
15: 	ID          uuid.UUID
16: 	Name        string
17: 	Description string
18: 	CreatedAt   time.Time
19: 	CreatedBy   string
20: 	Archived    bool
21: }
22: // Thread represents a discussion within a topic.
23: type Thread struct {
24: 	ID        uuid.UUID
25: 	TopicID   uuid.UUID
26: 	Subject   string
27: 	CreatedAt time.Time
28: 	CreatedBy string
29: 	UpdatedAt time.Time
30: 	Sticky    bool
31: }
32: // Message represents a post within a thread.
33: type Message struct {
34: 	ID        uuid.UUID
35: 	ThreadID  uuid.UUID
36: 	Content   string
37: 	CreatedAt time.Time
38: 	CreatedBy string
39: 	EditedAt  *time.Time
40: }
41: // Attachment represents a file attached to a message.
42: type Attachment struct {
43: 	ID        uuid.UUID
44: 	MessageID uuid.UUID
45: 	Filename  string
46: 	MimeType  string
47: 	Data      []byte
48: 	CreatedAt time.Time
49: }
50: // NewTopic creates a new topic with generated UUID and timestamp.
51: func NewTopic(name, description, createdBy string) *Topic {
52: 	return &Topic{
53: 		ID:          uuid.New(),
54: 		Name:        name,
55: 		Description: description,
56: 		CreatedAt:   time.Now(),
57: 		CreatedBy:   createdBy,
58: 		Archived:    false,
59: 	}
60: }
61: // NewThread creates a new thread with generated UUID and timestamp.
62: func NewThread(topicID uuid.UUID, subject, createdBy string) *Thread {
63: 	now := time.Now()
64: 	return &Thread{
65: 		ID:        uuid.New(),
66: 		TopicID:   topicID,
67: 		Subject:   subject,
68: 		CreatedAt: now,
69: 		CreatedBy: createdBy,
70: 		UpdatedAt: now,
71: 		Sticky:    false,
72: 	}
73: }
74: // NewMessage creates a new message with generated UUID and timestamp.
75: func NewMessage(threadID uuid.UUID, content, createdBy string) *Message {
76: 	return &Message{
77: 		ID:        uuid.New(),
78: 		ThreadID:  threadID,
79: 		Content:   content,
80: 		CreatedAt: time.Now(),
81: 		CreatedBy: createdBy,
82: 	}
83: }
84: // NewAttachment creates a new attachment with generated UUID and timestamp.
85: func NewAttachment(messageID uuid.UUID, filename, mimeType string, data []byte) *Attachment {
86: 	return &Attachment{
87: 		ID:        uuid.New(),
88: 		MessageID: messageID,
89: 		Filename:  filename,
90: 		MimeType:  mimeType,
91: 		Data:      data,
92: 		CreatedAt: time.Now(),
93: 	}
94: }
</file>

  1: // ABOUTME: Tests for SQLite storage layer

 13: func TestNewSqliteStore(t *testing.T) {
 14: 	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
 15: 	dbPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "test.db")
 16: 	store, err := NewSqliteStore(dbPath)
 17: 	if err != nil {
 18: 		t.Fatalf("NewSqliteStore failed: %v", err)
 19: 	}
 20: 	defer store.Close()
 21: 	if store == nil {
 22: 		t.Fatal("NewSqliteStore returned nil")
 23: 	}
 24: 	// Verify database file exists
 25: 	if _, err := os.Stat(dbPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {
 26: 		t.Fatal("Database file was not created")
 27: 	}

 29: func TestTopicCRUD(t *testing.T) {
 30: 	store := newTestStore(t)
 31: 	defer store.Close()
 32: 	// Create
 33: 	topic := models.NewTopic("General", "General discussion", "test@cli")
 34: 	err := store.CreateTopic(topic)
 35: 	if err != nil {
 36: 		t.Fatalf("CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
 37: 	}
 38: 	// Read
 39: 	got, err := store.GetTopic(topic.ID)
 40: 	if err != nil {
 41: 		t.Fatalf("GetTopic failed: %v", err)
 42: 	}
 43: 	if got.Name != topic.Name {
 44: 		t.Errorf("expected name %q, got %q", topic.Name, got.Name)
 45: 	}
 46: 	if got.Description != topic.Description {
 47: 		t.Errorf("expected description %q, got %q", topic.Description, got.Description)
 48: 	}
 49: 	if got.CreatedBy != topic.CreatedBy {
 50: 		t.Errorf("expected createdBy %q, got %q", topic.CreatedBy, got.CreatedBy)
 51: 	}
 52: 	// Update
 53: 	topic.Description = "Updated description"
 54: 	topic.Archived = true
 55: 	err = store.UpdateTopic(topic)
 56: 	if err != nil {
 57: 		t.Fatalf("UpdateTopic failed: %v", err)
 58: 	}
 59: 	got, _ = store.GetTopic(topic.ID)
 60: 	if got.Description != "Updated description" {
 61: 		t.Errorf("expected updated description, got %q", got.Description)
 62: 	}
 63: 	if !got.Archived {
 64: 		t.Error("expected topic to be archived")
 65: 	}
 66: 	// List
 67: 	topics, err := store.ListTopics(false)
 68: 	if err != nil {
 69: 		t.Fatalf("ListTopics failed: %v", err)
 70: 	}
 71: 	if len(topics) != 0 {
 72: 		t.Errorf("expected 0 non-archived topics, got %d", len(topics))
 73: 	}
 74: 	topics, err = store.ListTopics(true)
 75: 	if err != nil {
 76: 		t.Fatalf("ListTopics (with archived) failed: %v", err)
 77: 	}
 78: 	if len(topics) != 1 {
 79: 		t.Errorf("expected 1 topic, got %d", len(topics))
 80: 	}
 81: 	// Delete
 82: 	err = store.DeleteTopic(topic.ID)
 83: 	if err != nil {
 84: 		t.Fatalf("DeleteTopic failed: %v", err)
 85: 	}
 86: 	_, err = store.GetTopic(topic.ID)
 87: 	if err == nil {
 88: 		t.Error("expected error getting deleted topic")
 89: 	}
 90: }

162: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test", "test@cli")
163: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)

230: 	}
231: 	_, err = store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
232: 	if err == nil {
233: 		t.Error("expected error getting deleted message")
234: 	}

281: func TestCascadeDelete(t *testing.T) {
282: 	store := newTestStore(t)
283: 	defer store.Close()
284: 	// Create full hierarchy
285: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test topic", "test@cli")
286: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
287: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
288: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
289: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Hello", "test@cli")
290: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg)
291: 	att := models.NewAttachment(msg.ID, "test.txt", "text/plain", []byte("test"))
292: 	_ = store.CreateAttachment(att)
293: 	// Delete topic should cascade
294: 	err := store.DeleteTopic(topic.ID)
295: 	if err != nil {
296: 		t.Fatalf("DeleteTopic failed: %v", err)
297: 	}
298: 	// All children should be gone
299: 	_, err = store.GetThread(thread.ID)
300: 	if err == nil {
301: 		t.Error("thread should be deleted by cascade")
302: 	}
303: 	_, err = store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
304: 	if err == nil {
305: 		t.Error("message should be deleted by cascade")
306: 	}
307: 	_, err = store.GetAttachment(att.ID)
308: 	if err == nil {
309: 		t.Error("attachment should be deleted by cascade")
310: 	}
311: }

685: 	t.Helper()
686: 	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
687: 	dbPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "test.db")
688: 	store, err := NewSqliteStore(dbPath)
689: 	if err != nil {

691: 	}
