<file path="cmd/bbs/mcp.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: MCP server command implementation
 2: // ABOUTME: Starts BBS MCP server in stdio mode
 3: package main
 4: import (
 5: 	"context"
 6: 	"fmt"
 7: 	"os"
 8: 	"os/signal"
 9: 	"syscall"
10: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
11: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/config"
12: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/mcp"
13: )
14: var mcpCmd = &cobra.Command{
15: 	Use:   "mcp",
16: 	Short: "Start MCP server (stdio mode)",
17: 	Long: `Start the Model Context Protocol server for AI agent integration.
18: The MCP server communicates via stdio, allowing AI agents like Claude
19: to interact with BBS through a standardized protocol.`,
20: 	RunE: runMCP,
21: }
22: func init() {
23: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(mcpCmd)
24: }
25: func runMCP(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
26: 	ctx, cancel := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
27: 	defer cancel()
28: 	cfg, err := config.Load()
29: 	if err != nil {
30: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load config: %w", err)
31: 	}
32: 	store, err := cfg.OpenStorage()
33: 	if err != nil {
34: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open storage: %w", err)
35: 	}
36: 	defer store.Close()
37: 	server, err := mcp.NewServer(store)
38: 	if err != nil {
39: 		return err
40: 	}
41: 	return server.Serve(ctx)
42: }
</file>

<file path="cmd/bbs/whoami.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Whoami command
 2: // ABOUTME: Shows current identity and backend configuration
 3: package main
 4: import (
 5: 	"fmt"
 6: 	"path/filepath"
 7: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/config"
 8: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/identity"
 9: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
10: )
11: var whoamiCmd = &cobra.Command{
12: 	Use:   "whoami",
13: 	Short: "Show current identity",
14: 	RunE:  runWhoami,
15: }
16: func init() {
17: 	rootCmd.AddCommand(whoamiCmd)
18: }
19: func runWhoami(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
20: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(identityFlag, "cli")
21: 	cfg, err := config.Load()
22: 	if err != nil {
23: 		return fmt.Errorf("load config: %w", err)
24: 	}
25: 	fmt.Printf("Identity: %s\n", id)
26: 	fmt.Printf("Backend:  %s\n", cfg.GetBackend())
27: 	fmt.Printf("Data dir: %s\n", cfg.GetDataDir())
28: 	if cfg.GetBackend() == "sqlite" {
29: 		fmt.Printf("Database: %s\n", filepath.Join(cfg.GetDataDir(), "bbs.db"))
30: 	}
31: 	return nil
32: }
</file>

<file path="internal/mcp/resources.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: MCP resource implementations
  2: // ABOUTME: Read-only data access via MCP resources
  3: package mcp
  4: import (
  5: 	"context"
  6: 	"encoding/json"
  7: 	"fmt"
  8: 	"strings"
  9: 	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
 10: )
 11: func (s *Server) registerResources() {
 12: 	s.mcp.AddResource(&mcp.Resource{
 13: 		URI:         "bbs://topics",
 14: 		Name:        "All Topics",
 15: 		Description: "List of all active topics",
 16: 		MIMEType:    "application/json",
 17: 	}, s.handleTopicsResource)
 18: 	s.mcp.AddResource(&mcp.Resource{
 19: 		URI:         "bbs://recent",
 20: 		Name:        "Recent Activity",
 21: 		Description: "Recent threads and messages across all topics",
 22: 		MIMEType:    "text/markdown",
 23: 	}, s.handleRecentResource)
 24: 	// Dynamic resources for threads and messages
 25: 	s.mcp.AddResourceTemplate(&mcp.ResourceTemplate{
 26: 		URITemplate: "bbs://topics/{topic}/threads",
 27: 		Name:        "Topic Threads",
 28: 		Description: "Threads in a specific topic",
 29: 		MIMEType:    "application/json",
 30: 	}, s.handleTopicThreadsResource)
 31: 	s.mcp.AddResourceTemplate(&mcp.ResourceTemplate{
 32: 		URITemplate: "bbs://threads/{thread}/messages",
 33: 		Name:        "Thread Messages",
 34: 		Description: "Messages in a specific thread",
 35: 		MIMEType:    "text/markdown",
 36: 	}, s.handleThreadMessagesResource)
 37: }
 38: func (s *Server) handleTopicsResource(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error) {
 39: 	topics, err := s.store.ListTopics(false)
 40: 	if err != nil {
 41: 		return nil, err
 42: 	}
 43: 	data, _ := json.MarshalIndent(topics, "", "  ")
 44: 	return &mcp.ReadResourceResult{
 45: 		Contents: []*mcp.ResourceContents{{
 46: 			URI:      "bbs://topics",
 47: 			MIMEType: "application/json",
 48: 			Text:     string(data),
 49: 		}},
 50: 	}, nil
 51: }
 52: func (s *Server) handleRecentResource(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error) {
 53: 	topics, _ := s.store.ListTopics(false)
 54: 	var sb strings.Builder
 55: 	sb.WriteString("# Recent Activity\n\n")
 56: 	for _, topic := range topics {
 57: 		threads, _ := s.store.ListThreads(topic.ID)
 58: 		if len(threads) == 0 {
 59: 			continue
 60: 		}
 61: 		sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("## %s\n\n", topic.Name))
 62: 		for i, thread := range threads {
 63: 			if i >= 3 {
 64: 				break
 65: 			}
 66: 			prefix := ""
 67: 			if thread.Sticky {
 68: 				prefix = "📌 "
 69: 			}
 70: 			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("- %s**%s** by %s\n", prefix, thread.Subject, thread.CreatedBy))
 71: 		}
 72: 		sb.WriteString("\n")
 73: 	}
 74: 	return &mcp.ReadResourceResult{
 75: 		Contents: []*mcp.ResourceContents{{
 76: 			URI:      "bbs://recent",
 77: 			MIMEType: "text/markdown",
 78: 			Text:     sb.String(),
 79: 		}},
 80: 	}, nil
 81: }
 82: func (s *Server) handleTopicThreadsResource(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error) {
 83: 	// Extract topic from URI
 84: 	parts := strings.Split(req.Params.URI, "/")
 85: 	if len(parts) < 4 {
 86: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid URI")
 87: 	}
 88: 	topicName := parts[3]
 89: 	topic, err := s.store.ResolveTopic(topicName)
 90: 	if err != nil {
 91: 		return nil, err
 92: 	}
 93: 	threads, err := s.store.ListThreads(topic.ID)
 94: 	if err != nil {
 95: 		return nil, err
 96: 	}
 97: 	data, _ := json.MarshalIndent(threads, "", "  ")
 98: 	return &mcp.ReadResourceResult{
 99: 		Contents: []*mcp.ResourceContents{{
100: 			URI:      req.Params.URI,
101: 			MIMEType: "application/json",
102: 			Text:     string(data),
103: 		}},
104: 	}, nil
105: }
106: func (s *Server) handleThreadMessagesResource(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.ReadResourceRequest) (*mcp.ReadResourceResult, error) {
107: 	// Extract thread from URI
108: 	parts := strings.Split(req.Params.URI, "/")
109: 	if len(parts) < 4 {
110: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid URI")
111: 	}
112: 	threadID := parts[3]
113: 	thread, err := s.store.ResolveThread(threadID)
114: 	if err != nil {
115: 		return nil, err
116: 	}
117: 	messages, err := s.store.ListMessages(thread.ID)
118: 	if err != nil {
119: 		return nil, err
120: 	}
121: 	var sb strings.Builder
122: 	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("# %s\n\n", thread.Subject))
123: 	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("*Started by %s on %s*\n\n", thread.CreatedBy, thread.CreatedAt.Format("2006-01-02")))
124: 	sb.WriteString("---\n\n")
125: 	for _, msg := range messages {
126: 		sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("**%s** · %s\n\n", msg.CreatedBy, msg.CreatedAt.Format("Jan 02 15:04")))
127: 		sb.WriteString(msg.Content)
128: 		sb.WriteString("\n\n---\n\n")
129: 	}
130: 	return &mcp.ReadResourceResult{
131: 		Contents: []*mcp.ResourceContents{{
132: 			URI:      req.Params.URI,
133: 			MIMEType: "text/markdown",
134: 			Text:     sb.String(),
135: 		}},
136: 	}, nil
137: }
</file>

<file path="internal/mcp/tools.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: MCP tool implementations
  2: // ABOUTME: CRUD operations exposed as MCP tools
  3: package mcp
  4: import (
  5: 	"context"
  6: 	"encoding/json"
  7: 	"fmt"
  8: 	"time"
  9: 	"github.com/modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk/mcp"
 10: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/identity"
 11: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/models"
 12: )
 13: func (s *Server) registerTools() {
 14: 	// Topic tools
 15: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 16: 		Name:        "list_topics",
 17: 		Description: "List all topics on the board",
 18: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"include_archived":{"type":"boolean","description":"Include archived topics"}}}`),
 19: 	}, s.handleListTopics)
 20: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 21: 		Name:        "create_topic",
 22: 		Description: "Create a new topic",
 23: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"name":{"type":"string"},"description":{"type":"string"}},"required":["name"]}`),
 24: 	}, s.handleCreateTopic)
 25: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 26: 		Name:        "archive_topic",
 27: 		Description: "Archive or unarchive a topic",
 28: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"topic":{"type":"string"},"archived":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["topic","archived"]}`),
 29: 	}, s.handleArchiveTopic)
 30: 	// Thread tools
 31: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 32: 		Name:        "list_threads",
 33: 		Description: "List threads in a topic",
 34: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"topic":{"type":"string"}},"required":["topic"]}`),
 35: 	}, s.handleListThreads)
 36: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 37: 		Name:        "create_thread",
 38: 		Description: "Create a new thread with initial message",
 39: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"topic":{"type":"string"},"subject":{"type":"string"},"message":{"type":"string"},"agent_name":{"type":"string"}},"required":["topic","subject"]}`),
 40: 	}, s.handleCreateThread)
 41: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 42: 		Name:        "sticky_thread",
 43: 		Description: "Pin or unpin a thread",
 44: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"thread":{"type":"string"},"sticky":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["thread","sticky"]}`),
 45: 	}, s.handleStickyThread)
 46: 	// Message tools
 47: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 48: 		Name:        "list_messages",
 49: 		Description: "List messages in a thread",
 50: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"thread":{"type":"string"}},"required":["thread"]}`),
 51: 	}, s.handleListMessages)
 52: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 53: 		Name:        "post_message",
 54: 		Description: "Post a message to a thread",
 55: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"thread":{"type":"string"},"content":{"type":"string"},"agent_name":{"type":"string"}},"required":["thread","content"]}`),
 56: 	}, s.handlePostMessage)
 57: 	s.mcp.AddTool(&mcp.Tool{
 58: 		Name:        "edit_message",
 59: 		Description: "Edit an existing message",
 60: 		InputSchema: json.RawMessage(`{"type":"object","properties":{"message_id":{"type":"string"},"content":{"type":"string"}},"required":["message_id","content"]}`),
 61: 	}, s.handleEditMessage)
 62: }
 63: func (s *Server) handleListTopics(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
 64: 	var args struct {
 65: 		IncludeArchived bool `json:"include_archived"`
 66: 	}
 67: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args); err != nil {
 68: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
 69: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("invalid arguments: %v", err)}},
 70: 			IsError: true,
 71: 		}, nil
 72: 	}
 73: 	topics, err := s.store.ListTopics(args.IncludeArchived)
 74: 	if err != nil {
 75: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
 76: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
 77: 			IsError: true,
 78: 		}, nil
 79: 	}
 80: 	result, err := json.Marshal(topics)
 81: 	if err != nil {
 82: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
 83: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("failed to marshal response: %v", err)}},
 84: 			IsError: true,
 85: 		}, nil
 86: 	}
 87: 	return &mcp.CallToolResult{
 88: 		Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: string(result)}},
 89: 	}, nil
 90: }
 91: func (s *Server) handleCreateTopic(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
 92: 	var args struct {
 93: 		Name        string `json:"name"`
 94: 		Description string `json:"description"`
 95: 		AgentName   string `json:"agent_name"`
 96: 	}
 97: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args); err != nil {
 98: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
 99: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("invalid arguments: %v", err)}},
100: 			IsError: true,
101: 		}, nil
102: 	}
103: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(args.AgentName, "mcp")
104: 	topic := models.NewTopic(args.Name, args.Description, id)
105: 	if err := s.store.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
106: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
107: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
108: 			IsError: true,
109: 		}, nil
110: 	}
111: 	return &mcp.CallToolResult{
112: 		Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("Created topic: %s (ID: %s)", args.Name, topic.ID.String()[:8])}},
113: 	}, nil
114: }
115: func (s *Server) handleArchiveTopic(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
116: 	var args struct {
117: 		Topic    string `json:"topic"`
118: 		Archived bool   `json:"archived"`
119: 	}
120: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args); err != nil {
121: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
122: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("invalid arguments: %v", err)}},
123: 			IsError: true,
124: 		}, nil
125: 	}
126: 	topic, err := s.store.ResolveTopic(args.Topic)
127: 	if err != nil {
128: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
129: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
130: 			IsError: true,
131: 		}, nil
132: 	}
133: 	if err := s.store.ArchiveTopic(topic.ID, args.Archived); err != nil {
134: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
135: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
136: 			IsError: true,
137: 		}, nil
138: 	}
139: 	status := "archived"
140: 	if !args.Archived {
141: 		status = "unarchived"
142: 	}
143: 	return &mcp.CallToolResult{
144: 		Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("Topic %s", status)}},
145: 	}, nil
146: }
147: func (s *Server) handleListThreads(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
148: 	var args struct {
149: 		Topic string `json:"topic"`
150: 	}
151: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args); err != nil {
152: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
153: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("invalid arguments: %v", err)}},
154: 			IsError: true,
155: 		}, nil
156: 	}
157: 	topic, err := s.store.ResolveTopic(args.Topic)
158: 	if err != nil {
159: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
160: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
161: 			IsError: true,
162: 		}, nil
163: 	}
164: 	threads, err := s.store.ListThreads(topic.ID)
165: 	if err != nil {
166: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
167: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
168: 			IsError: true,
169: 		}, nil
170: 	}
171: 	result, err := json.Marshal(threads)
172: 	if err != nil {
173: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
174: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("failed to marshal response: %v", err)}},
175: 			IsError: true,
176: 		}, nil
177: 	}
178: 	return &mcp.CallToolResult{
179: 		Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: string(result)}},
180: 	}, nil
181: }
182: func (s *Server) handleCreateThread(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
183: 	var args struct {
184: 		Topic     string `json:"topic"`
185: 		Subject   string `json:"subject"`
186: 		Message   string `json:"message"`
187: 		AgentName string `json:"agent_name"`
188: 	}
189: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args); err != nil {
190: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
191: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("invalid arguments: %v", err)}},
192: 			IsError: true,
193: 		}, nil
194: 	}
195: 	topic, err := s.store.ResolveTopic(args.Topic)
196: 	if err != nil {
197: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
198: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
199: 			IsError: true,
200: 		}, nil
201: 	}
202: 	id := identity.GetIdentity(args.AgentName, "mcp")
203: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, args.Subject, id)
204: 	if err := s.store.CreateThread(thread); err != nil {
205: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
206: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
207: 			IsError: true,
208: 		}, nil
209: 	}
210: 	// Post initial message if provided
211: 	if args.Message != "" {
212: 		msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, args.Message, id)
213: 		if err := s.store.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
214: 			return &mcp.CallToolResult{
215: 				Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("thread created but failed to post message: %v", err)}},
216: 				IsError: true,
217: 			}, nil
218: 		}
219: 	}
220: 	return &mcp.CallToolResult{
221: 		Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("Created thread: %s (ID: %s)", args.Subject, thread.ID.String()[:8])}},
222: 	}, nil
223: }
224: func (s *Server) handleStickyThread(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
225: 	var args struct {
226: 		Thread string `json:"thread"`
227: 		Sticky bool   `json:"sticky"`
228: 	}
229: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args); err != nil {
230: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
231: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("invalid arguments: %v", err)}},
232: 			IsError: true,
233: 		}, nil
234: 	}
235: 	thread, err := s.store.ResolveThread(args.Thread)
236: 	if err != nil {
237: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
238: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
239: 			IsError: true,
240: 		}, nil
241: 	}
242: 	if err := s.store.SetThreadSticky(thread.ID, args.Sticky); err != nil {
243: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
244: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
245: 			IsError: true,
246: 		}, nil
247: 	}
248: 	status := "pinned"
249: 	if !args.Sticky {
250: 		status = "unpinned"
251: 	}
252: 	return &mcp.CallToolResult{
253: 		Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("Thread %s", status)}},
254: 	}, nil
255: }
256: func (s *Server) handleListMessages(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {

322: func (s *Server) handleEditMessage(ctx context.Context, req *mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
323: 	var args struct {
324: 		MessageID string `json:"message_id"`
325: 		Content   string `json:"content"`
326: 	}
327: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(req.Params.Arguments, &args); err != nil {
328: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
329: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: fmt.Sprintf("invalid arguments: %v", err)}},
330: 			IsError: true,
331: 		}, nil
332: 	}
333: 	msg, err := s.store.ResolveMessage(args.MessageID)
334: 	if err != nil {
335: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
336: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
337: 			IsError: true,
338: 		}, nil
339: 	}
340: 	msg.Content = args.Content
341: 	now := time.Now()
342: 	msg.EditedAt = &now
343: 	if err := s.store.UpdateMessage(msg); err != nil {
344: 		return &mcp.CallToolResult{
345: 			Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: err.Error()}},
346: 			IsError: true,
347: 		}, nil
348: 	}
349: 	return &mcp.CallToolResult{
350: 		Content: []mcp.Content{&mcp.TextContent{Text: "Message updated"}},
351: 	}, nil
352: }
</file>

   2: // ABOUTME: Covers CRUD for topics, threads, messages, attachments, resolution, and edge cases

   5: 	"fmt"

  16: // newTestMarkdownStore creates a MarkdownStore in a temporary directory for testing.
  17: func newTestMarkdownStore(t *testing.T) *MarkdownStore {
  18: 	t.Helper()
  19: 	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
  20: 	store, err := NewMarkdownStore(tmpDir)
  21: 	if err != nil {
  22: 		t.Fatalf("failed to create test markdown store: %v", err)
  23: 	}
  24: 	return store
  25: }
  26: func TestNewMarkdownStore(t *testing.T) {
  27: 	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
  28: 	dataDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "bbs-data")
  29: 	store, err := NewMarkdownStore(dataDir)
  30: 	if err != nil {
  31: 		t.Fatalf("NewMarkdownStore failed: %v", err)
  32: 	}
  33: 	defer store.Close()
  34: 	if store == nil {
  35: 		t.Fatal("NewMarkdownStore returned nil")
  36: 	}
  37: 	// Verify data directory exists
  38: 	if _, err := os.Stat(dataDir); os.IsNotExist(err) {
  39: 		t.Fatal("Data directory was not created")
  40: 	}
  41: }
  42: func TestMarkdownTopicCRUD(t *testing.T) {
  43: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
  44: 	defer store.Close()
  45: 	// Create
  46: 	topic := models.NewTopic("General", "General discussion", "test@cli")
  47: 	err := store.CreateTopic(topic)
  48: 	if err != nil {
  49: 		t.Fatalf("CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
  50: 	}
  51: 	// Read
  52: 	got, err := store.GetTopic(topic.ID)
  53: 	if err != nil {
  54: 		t.Fatalf("GetTopic failed: %v", err)
  55: 	}
  56: 	if got.Name != topic.Name {
  57: 		t.Errorf("expected name %q, got %q", topic.Name, got.Name)
  58: 	}
  59: 	if got.Description != topic.Description {
  60: 		t.Errorf("expected description %q, got %q", topic.Description, got.Description)
  61: 	}
  62: 	if got.CreatedBy != topic.CreatedBy {
  63: 		t.Errorf("expected createdBy %q, got %q", topic.CreatedBy, got.CreatedBy)
  64: 	}
  65: 	// Update
  66: 	topic.Description = "Updated description"
  67: 	topic.Archived = true
  68: 	err = store.UpdateTopic(topic)
  69: 	if err != nil {
  70: 		t.Fatalf("UpdateTopic failed: %v", err)
  71: 	}
  72: 	got, _ = store.GetTopic(topic.ID)
  73: 	if got.Description != "Updated description" {
  74: 		t.Errorf("expected updated description, got %q", got.Description)
  75: 	}
  76: 	if !got.Archived {
  77: 		t.Error("expected topic to be archived")
  78: 	}
  79: 	// List
  80: 	topics, err := store.ListTopics(false)
  81: 	if err != nil {
  82: 		t.Fatalf("ListTopics failed: %v", err)
  83: 	}
  84: 	if len(topics) != 0 {
  85: 		t.Errorf("expected 0 non-archived topics, got %d", len(topics))
  86: 	}
  87: 	topics, err = store.ListTopics(true)
  88: 	if err != nil {
  89: 		t.Fatalf("ListTopics (with archived) failed: %v", err)
  90: 	}
  91: 	if len(topics) != 1 {
  92: 		t.Errorf("expected 1 topic, got %d", len(topics))
  93: 	}
  94: 	// Delete
  95: 	err = store.DeleteTopic(topic.ID)
  96: 	if err != nil {
  97: 		t.Fatalf("DeleteTopic failed: %v", err)
  98: 	}
  99: 	_, err = store.GetTopic(topic.ID)
 100: 	if err == nil {
 101: 		t.Error("expected error getting deleted topic")
 102: 	}
 103: }

 121: func TestMarkdownThreadCRUD(t *testing.T) {
 122: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 123: 	defer store.Close()
 124: 	// Create topic first
 125: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test topic", "test@cli")
 126: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 127: 	// Create thread
 128: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Hello World", "test@cli")
 129: 	err := store.CreateThread(thread)
 130: 	if err != nil {
 131: 		t.Fatalf("CreateThread failed: %v", err)
 132: 	}
 133: 	// Read
 134: 	got, err := store.GetThread(thread.ID)
 135: 	if err != nil {
 136: 		t.Fatalf("GetThread failed: %v", err)
 137: 	}
 138: 	if got.Subject != thread.Subject {
 139: 		t.Errorf("expected subject %q, got %q", thread.Subject, got.Subject)
 140: 	}
 141: 	if got.TopicID != topic.ID {
 142: 		t.Errorf("expected topicID %s, got %s", topic.ID, got.TopicID)
 143: 	}
 144: 	// Update
 145: 	thread.Sticky = true
 146: 	err = store.UpdateThread(thread)
 147: 	if err != nil {
 148: 		t.Fatalf("UpdateThread failed: %v", err)
 149: 	}
 150: 	got, _ = store.GetThread(thread.ID)
 151: 	if !got.Sticky {
 152: 		t.Error("expected thread to be sticky")
 153: 	}
 154: 	// List
 155: 	threads, err := store.ListThreads(topic.ID)
 156: 	if err != nil {
 157: 		t.Fatalf("ListThreads failed: %v", err)
 158: 	}
 159: 	if len(threads) != 1 {
 160: 		t.Errorf("expected 1 thread, got %d", len(threads))
 161: 	}
 162: 	// Delete
 163: 	err = store.DeleteThread(thread.ID)
 164: 	if err != nil {
 165: 		t.Fatalf("DeleteThread failed: %v", err)
 166: 	}
 167: 	_, err = store.GetThread(thread.ID)
 168: 	if err == nil {
 169: 		t.Error("expected error getting deleted thread")
 170: 	}
 171: }
 172: func TestMarkdownThreadUpdatedAt(t *testing.T) {
 173: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 174: 	defer store.Close()
 175: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test", "test@cli")
 176: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 177: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
 178: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
 179: 	// Get initial updated_at (should equal created_at since no messages yet)
 180: 	got, _ := store.GetThread(thread.ID)
 181: 	initialUpdatedAt := got.UpdatedAt
 182: 	// Wait a bit and add a message - this should advance updated_at
 183: 	time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
 184: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Bump", "test@cli")
 185: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg)
 186: 	got, _ = store.GetThread(thread.ID)
 187: 	if !got.UpdatedAt.After(initialUpdatedAt) {
 188: 		t.Error("expected updated_at to advance when a message is added")
 189: 	}
 190: }
 191: func TestMarkdownMessageCRUD(t *testing.T) {
 192: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 193: 	defer store.Close()
 194: 	// Create topic and thread first
 195: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test topic", "test@cli")
 196: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 197: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
 198: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
 199: 	// Create message
 200: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Hello, world!", "test@cli")
 201: 	err := store.CreateMessage(msg)
 202: 	if err != nil {
 203: 		t.Fatalf("CreateMessage failed: %v", err)
 204: 	}
 205: 	// Read
 206: 	got, err := store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
 207: 	if err != nil {
 208: 		t.Fatalf("GetMessage failed: %v", err)
 209: 	}
 210: 	if got.Content != msg.Content {
 211: 		t.Errorf("expected content %q, got %q", msg.Content, got.Content)
 212: 	}
 213: 	if got.ThreadID != thread.ID {
 214: 		t.Errorf("expected threadID %s, got %s", thread.ID, got.ThreadID)
 215: 	}
 216: 	// Update
 217: 	now := time.Now()
 218: 	msg.Content = "Edited message"
 219: 	msg.EditedAt = &now
 220: 	err = store.UpdateMessage(msg)
 221: 	if err != nil {
 222: 		t.Fatalf("UpdateMessage failed: %v", err)
 223: 	}
 224: 	got, _ = store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
 225: 	if got.Content != "Edited message" {
 226: 		t.Errorf("expected edited content, got %q", got.Content)
 227: 	}
 228: 	if got.EditedAt == nil {
 229: 		t.Error("expected editedAt to be set")
 230: 	}
 231: 	// List
 232: 	messages, err := store.ListMessages(thread.ID)
 233: 	if err != nil {
 234: 		t.Fatalf("ListMessages failed: %v", err)
 235: 	}
 236: 	if len(messages) != 1 {
 237: 		t.Errorf("expected 1 message, got %d", len(messages))
 238: 	}
 239: 	// Delete
 240: 	err = store.DeleteMessage(msg.ID)
 241: 	if err != nil {
 242: 		t.Fatalf("DeleteMessage failed: %v", err)
 243: 	}
 244: 	_, err = store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
 245: 	if err == nil {
 246: 		t.Error("expected error getting deleted message")
 247: 	}
 248: }
 249: func TestMarkdownAttachmentCRUD(t *testing.T) {
 250: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 251: 	defer store.Close()
 252: 	// Create topic, thread, message first
 253: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test topic", "test@cli")
 254: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 255: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
 256: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
 257: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Hello", "test@cli")
 258: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg)
 259: 	// Create attachment
 260: 	att := models.NewAttachment(msg.ID, "test.txt", "text/plain", []byte("test content"))
 261: 	err := store.CreateAttachment(att)
 262: 	if err != nil {
 263: 		t.Fatalf("CreateAttachment failed: %v", err)
 264: 	}
 265: 	// Read
 266: 	got, err := store.GetAttachment(att.ID)
 267: 	if err != nil {
 268: 		t.Fatalf("GetAttachment failed: %v", err)
 269: 	}
 270: 	if got.Filename != att.Filename {
 271: 		t.Errorf("expected filename %q, got %q", att.Filename, got.Filename)
 272: 	}
 273: 	if string(got.Data) != "test content" {
 274: 		t.Errorf("expected data %q, got %q", "test content", string(got.Data))
 275: 	}
 276: 	// List
 277: 	attachments, err := store.ListAttachments(msg.ID)
 278: 	if err != nil {
 279: 		t.Fatalf("ListAttachments failed: %v", err)
 280: 	}
 281: 	if len(attachments) != 1 {
 282: 		t.Errorf("expected 1 attachment, got %d", len(attachments))
 283: 	}
 284: 	// Delete
 285: 	err = store.DeleteAttachment(att.ID)
 286: 	if err != nil {
 287: 		t.Fatalf("DeleteAttachment failed: %v", err)
 288: 	}
 289: 	_, err = store.GetAttachment(att.ID)
 290: 	if err == nil {
 291: 		t.Error("expected error getting deleted attachment")
 292: 	}
 293: }
 294: func TestMarkdownCascadeDelete(t *testing.T) {
 295: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 296: 	defer store.Close()
 297: 	// Create full hierarchy
 298: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test topic", "test@cli")
 299: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 300: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
 301: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
 302: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Hello", "test@cli")
 303: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg)
 304: 	att := models.NewAttachment(msg.ID, "test.txt", "text/plain", []byte("test"))
 305: 	_ = store.CreateAttachment(att)
 306: 	// Delete topic should cascade
 307: 	err := store.DeleteTopic(topic.ID)
 308: 	if err != nil {
 309: 		t.Fatalf("DeleteTopic failed: %v", err)
 310: 	}
 311: 	// All children should be gone
 312: 	_, err = store.GetThread(thread.ID)
 313: 	if err == nil {
 314: 		t.Error("thread should be deleted by cascade")
 315: 	}
 316: 	_, err = store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
 317: 	if err == nil {
 318: 		t.Error("message should be deleted by cascade")
 319: 	}
 320: 	_, err = store.GetAttachment(att.ID)
 321: 	if err == nil {
 322: 		t.Error("attachment should be deleted by cascade")
 323: 	}
 324: }
 325: func TestMarkdownResolveTopic(t *testing.T) {
 326: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 327: 	defer store.Close()
 328: 	topic := models.NewTopic("General", "General discussion", "test@cli")
 329: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 330: 	// By full ID
 331: 	got, err := store.ResolveTopic(topic.ID.String())
 332: 	if err != nil {
 333: 		t.Fatalf("ResolveTopic by ID failed: %v", err)
 334: 	}
 335: 	if got.ID != topic.ID {
 336: 		t.Error("wrong topic returned")
 337: 	}
 338: 	// By name
 339: 	got, err = store.ResolveTopic("General")
 340: 	if err != nil {
 341: 		t.Fatalf("ResolveTopic by name failed: %v", err)
 342: 	}
 343: 	if got.ID != topic.ID {
 344: 		t.Error("wrong topic returned")
 345: 	}
 346: 	// By ID prefix (first 8 chars)
 347: 	prefix := topic.ID.String()[:8]
 348: 	got, err = store.ResolveTopic(prefix)
 349: 	if err != nil {
 350: 		t.Fatalf("ResolveTopic by prefix failed: %v", err)
 351: 	}
 352: 	if got.ID != topic.ID {
 353: 		t.Error("wrong topic returned")
 354: 	}
 355: 	// Not found
 356: 	_, err = store.ResolveTopic("nonexistent")
 357: 	if err == nil {
 358: 		t.Error("expected error for non-existent topic")
 359: 	}
 360: }

 518: func TestMarkdownListMessagesWithEditedAt(t *testing.T) {
 519: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 520: 	defer store.Close()
 521: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test", "test@cli")
 522: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 523: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
 524: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
 525: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Hello", "test@cli")
 526: 	now := msg.CreatedAt
 527: 	msg.EditedAt = &now
 528: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg)
 529: 	messages, err := store.ListMessages(thread.ID)
 530: 	if err != nil {
 531: 		t.Fatalf("ListMessages failed: %v", err)
 532: 	}
 533: 	if len(messages) != 1 {
 534: 		t.Fatalf("expected 1 message, got %d", len(messages))
 535: 	}
 536: 	if messages[0].EditedAt == nil {
 537: 		t.Error("expected EditedAt to be set")
 538: 	}
 539: }

 653: func TestMarkdownSlugify(t *testing.T) {
 654: 	tests := []struct {
 655: 		input    string
 656: 		expected string
 657: 	}{
 658: 		{"Hello World", "hello-world"},
 659: 		{"BBS Health Check", "bbs-health-check"},
 660: 		{"  multiple   spaces  ", "multiple-spaces"},
 661: 		{"Special! @chars# $here", "special-chars-here"},
 662: 		{"", "untitled"},
 663: 		{"ALLCAPS", "allcaps"},
 664: 		{"with-existing-hyphens", "with-existing-hyphens"},
 665: 		{"123-numbers", "123-numbers"},
 666: 	}
 667: 	for _, tt := range tests {
 668: 		got := mdstore.Slugify(tt.input)
 669: 		if got != tt.expected {
 670: 			t.Errorf("Slugify(%q) = %q, want %q", tt.input, got, tt.expected)
 671: 		}
 672: 	}
 673: }
 674: func TestMarkdownFrontmatterParsing(t *testing.T) {
 675: 	content := `---
 676: id: 5681e681-3603-4dbf-b289-08ae47819163
 677: topic: general
 678: subject: BBS Health Check
 679: created_at: "2026-02-02T02:39:08Z"
 680: created_by: Claude@mcp
 681: sticky: false
 682: ---
 683: ## Claude@mcp — 2026-02-02T02:39:08Z
 684: <!-- msg:49528f10-0ec9-49fa-903e-e078a45c08fc -->
 685: Testing that the BBS is operational.
 686: `
 687: 	fm, err := parseThreadFrontmatter(content)
 688: 	if err != nil {
 689: 		t.Fatalf("parseThreadFrontmatter failed: %v", err)
 690: 	}
 691: 	if fm.ID != "5681e681-3603-4dbf-b289-08ae47819163" {
 692: 		t.Errorf("expected ID 5681e681-3603-4dbf-b289-08ae47819163, got %s", fm.ID)
 693: 	}
 694: 	if fm.Topic != "general" {
 695: 		t.Errorf("expected topic general, got %s", fm.Topic)
 696: 	}
 697: 	if fm.Subject != "BBS Health Check" {
 698: 		t.Errorf("expected subject 'BBS Health Check', got %s", fm.Subject)
 699: 	}
 700: }
 701: func TestMarkdownMessageParsing(t *testing.T) {
 702: 	threadID := uuid.New()
 703: 	content := `---
 704: id: ` + threadID.String() + `
 705: topic: general
 706: subject: Test Thread
 707: created_at: "2026-02-02T02:39:08Z"
 708: created_by: test@cli
 709: sticky: false
 710: ---
 711: ## Claude@mcp — 2026-02-02T02:39:08Z
 712: <!-- msg:49528f10-0ec9-49fa-903e-e078a45c08fc -->
 713: Testing that the BBS is operational. All systems nominal.
 714: <!-- message-separator -->
 715: ## claude_opus@mcp — 2026-02-02T02:42:30Z
 716: <!-- msg:762ee7bf-1234-5678-abcd-ef0123456789 -->
 717: Reply test - confirming message posting works correctly.
 718: `
 719: 	messages := parseThreadMessages(content)
 720: 	if len(messages) != 2 {
 721: 		t.Fatalf("expected 2 messages, got %d", len(messages))
 722: 	}
 723: 	if messages[0].ID.String() != "49528f10-0ec9-49fa-903e-e078a45c08fc" {
 724: 		t.Errorf("expected first message ID 49528f10-0ec9-49fa-903e-e078a45c08fc, got %s", messages[0].ID)
 725: 	}
 726: 	if messages[0].CreatedBy != "Claude@mcp" {
 727: 		t.Errorf("expected first message author Claude@mcp, got %s", messages[0].CreatedBy)
 728: 	}
 729: 	if !strings.Contains(messages[0].Content, "Testing that the BBS is operational") {
 730: 		t.Errorf("unexpected first message content: %s", messages[0].Content)
 731: 	}
 732: 	if messages[1].ID.String() != "762ee7bf-1234-5678-abcd-ef0123456789" {
 733: 		t.Errorf("expected second message ID 762ee7bf-1234-5678-abcd-ef0123456789, got %s", messages[1].ID)
 734: 	}
 735: }
 736: func TestMarkdownMessageWithEditedMarker(t *testing.T) {
 737: 	threadID := uuid.New()
 738: 	content := `---
 739: id: ` + threadID.String() + `
 740: topic: general
 741: subject: Test
 742: created_at: "2026-02-02T02:39:08Z"
 743: created_by: test@cli
 744: sticky: false
 745: ---
 746: ## test@cli — 2026-02-02T02:39:08Z
 747: <!-- msg:49528f10-0ec9-49fa-903e-e078a45c08fc -->
 748: <!-- edited:2026-02-02T03:00:00Z -->
 749: This was edited.
 750: `
 751: 	messages := parseThreadMessages(content)
 752: 	if len(messages) != 1 {
 753: 		t.Fatalf("expected 1 message, got %d", len(messages))
 754: 	}
 755: 	if messages[0].EditedAt == nil {
 756: 		t.Fatal("expected EditedAt to be set")
 757: 	}
 758: 	expected, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, "2026-02-02T03:00:00Z")
 759: 	if !messages[0].EditedAt.Equal(expected) {
 760: 		t.Errorf("expected EditedAt %v, got %v", expected, *messages[0].EditedAt)
 761: 	}
 762: }

 803: func TestMarkdownMultipleMessages(t *testing.T) {
 804: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 805: 	defer store.Close()
 806: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test", "test@cli")
 807: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 808: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
 809: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
 810: 	// Create multiple messages
 811: 	msg1 := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "First message", "user1@cli")
 812: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
 813: 	msg2 := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Second message", "user2@cli")
 814: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
 815: 	msg3 := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Third message", "user3@cli")
 816: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg1)
 817: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg2)
 818: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg3)
 819: 	messages, err := store.ListMessages(thread.ID)
 820: 	if err != nil {
 821: 		t.Fatalf("ListMessages failed: %v", err)
 822: 	}
 823: 	if len(messages) != 3 {
 824: 		t.Fatalf("expected 3 messages, got %d", len(messages))
 825: 	}
 826: 	// Verify order is by created_at ASC
 827: 	if messages[0].Content != "First message" {
 828: 		t.Errorf("expected first message content 'First message', got %q", messages[0].Content)
 829: 	}
 830: 	if messages[1].Content != "Second message" {
 831: 		t.Errorf("expected second message content 'Second message', got %q", messages[1].Content)
 832: 	}
 833: 	if messages[2].Content != "Third message" {
 834: 		t.Errorf("expected third message content 'Third message', got %q", messages[2].Content)
 835: 	}
 836: }

 882: func TestMarkdownUpdateTopicRenameUpdatesThreadFrontmatter(t *testing.T) {
 883: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 884: 	defer store.Close()
 885: 	topic := models.NewTopic("old-name", "A topic", "test@cli")
 886: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 887: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
 888: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
 889: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Hello from old topic", "test@cli")
 890: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg)
 891: 	// Rename the topic
 892: 	topic.Name = "new-name"
 893: 	err := store.UpdateTopic(topic)
 894: 	if err != nil {
 895: 		t.Fatalf("UpdateTopic (rename) failed: %v", err)
 896: 	}
 897: 	// Verify old directory is gone and new directory exists
 898: 	oldDir := filepath.Join(store.dataDir, "old-name")
 899: 	newDir := filepath.Join(store.dataDir, "new-name")
 900: 	if _, err := os.Stat(oldDir); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
 901: 		t.Error("old topic directory should not exist after rename")
 902: 	}
 903: 	if _, err := os.Stat(newDir); os.IsNotExist(err) {
 904: 		t.Error("new topic directory should exist after rename")
 905: 	}
 906: 	// Verify the thread is still retrievable
 907: 	got, err := store.GetThread(thread.ID)
 908: 	if err != nil {
 909: 		t.Fatalf("GetThread after rename failed: %v", err)
 910: 	}
 911: 	if got.Subject != "Test Thread" {
 912: 		t.Errorf("expected subject 'Test Thread', got %q", got.Subject)
 913: 	}
 914: 	// Verify thread frontmatter was updated by reading the file directly
 915: 	entries, _ := os.ReadDir(newDir)
 916: 	foundThread := false
 917: 	for _, de := range entries {
 918: 		if de.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(de.Name(), ".md") {
 919: 			continue
 920: 		}
 921: 		fp := filepath.Join(newDir, de.Name())
 922: 		fm, fmErr := readThreadFrontmatter(fp)
 923: 		if fmErr != nil {
 924: 			continue
 925: 		}
 926: 		if fm.ID == thread.ID.String() {
 927: 			foundThread = true
 928: 			if fm.Topic != "new-name" {
 929: 				t.Errorf("expected thread frontmatter topic to be 'new-name', got %q", fm.Topic)
 930: 			}
 931: 		}
 932: 	}
 933: 	if !foundThread {
 934: 		t.Error("thread file not found in renamed topic directory")
 935: 	}
 936: 	// Verify messages survived the rename
 937: 	messages, err := store.ListMessages(thread.ID)
 938: 	if err != nil {
 939: 		t.Fatalf("ListMessages after rename failed: %v", err)
 940: 	}
 941: 	if len(messages) != 1 {
 942: 		t.Errorf("expected 1 message after rename, got %d", len(messages))
 943: 	}
 944: }
 945: func TestMarkdownAttachmentFilenameCollision(t *testing.T) {
 946: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
 947: 	defer store.Close()
 948: 	topic := models.NewTopic("Test", "Test topic", "test@cli")
 949: 	_ = store.CreateTopic(topic)
 950: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Test Thread", "test@cli")
 951: 	_ = store.CreateThread(thread)
 952: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Hello", "test@cli")
 953: 	_ = store.CreateMessage(msg)
 954: 	// Create first attachment with a filename
 955: 	att1 := models.NewAttachment(msg.ID, "report.txt", "text/plain", []byte("first report"))
 956: 	err := store.CreateAttachment(att1)
 957: 	if err != nil {
 958: 		t.Fatalf("CreateAttachment (first) failed: %v", err)
 959: 	}
 960: 	// Create second attachment with the same filename
 961: 	att2 := models.NewAttachment(msg.ID, "report.txt", "text/plain", []byte("second report"))
 962: 	err = store.CreateAttachment(att2)
 963: 	if err != nil {
 964: 		t.Fatalf("CreateAttachment (collision) failed: %v", err)
 965: 	}
 966: 	// Both attachments should be retrievable with correct data
 967: 	got1, err := store.GetAttachment(att1.ID)
 968: 	if err != nil {
 969: 		t.Fatalf("GetAttachment (first) failed: %v", err)
 970: 	}
 971: 	if string(got1.Data) != "first report" {
 972: 		t.Errorf("expected first attachment data 'first report', got %q", string(got1.Data))
 973: 	}
 974: 	got2, err := store.GetAttachment(att2.ID)
 975: 	if err != nil {
 976: 		t.Fatalf("GetAttachment (second) failed: %v", err)
 977: 	}
 978: 	if string(got2.Data) != "second report" {
 979: 		t.Errorf("expected second attachment data 'second report', got %q", string(got2.Data))
 980: 	}
 981: 	// The second attachment had a collision, so its filename should still be the original
 982: 	if got2.Filename != "report.txt" {
 983: 		t.Errorf("expected second attachment filename 'report.txt' (original preserved), got %q", got2.Filename)
 984: 	}
 985: 	// Both should appear in list
 986: 	attachments, err := store.ListAttachments(msg.ID)
 987: 	if err != nil {
 988: 		t.Fatalf("ListAttachments failed: %v", err)
 989: 	}
 990: 	if len(attachments) != 2 {
 991: 		t.Errorf("expected 2 attachments, got %d", len(attachments))
 992: 	}
 993: }
 994: func TestMarkdownToModelErrorPropagation(t *testing.T) {
 995: 	// Test that toModel properly returns an error for invalid data
 996: 	entry := topicEntry{
 997: 		ID:        "not-a-uuid",
 998: 		Name:      "test",
 999: 		CreatedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
1000: 	}
1001: 	_, err := entry.toModel()
1002: 	if err == nil {
1003: 		t.Error("expected error for invalid UUID in toModel")
1004: 	}
1005: 	entry2 := topicEntry{
1006: 		ID:        "5681e681-3603-4dbf-b289-08ae47819163",
1007: 		Name:      "test",
1008: 		CreatedAt: "not-a-timestamp",
1009: 	}
1010: 	_, err = entry2.toModel()
1011: 	if err == nil {
1012: 		t.Error("expected error for invalid timestamp in toModel")
1013: 	}
1014: 	// Valid entry should succeed
1015: 	entry3 := topicEntry{
1016: 		ID:        "5681e681-3603-4dbf-b289-08ae47819163",
1017: 		Name:      "test",
1018: 		CreatedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
1019: 		CreatedBy: "test@cli",
1020: 	}
1021: 	topic, err := entry3.toModel()
1022: 	if err != nil {
1023: 		t.Fatalf("expected no error for valid entry, got: %v", err)
1024: 	}
1025: 	if topic.Name != "test" {
1026: 		t.Errorf("expected name 'test', got %q", topic.Name)
1027: 	}
1028: }

1063: // --- Concurrency Tests ---
1064: func TestMarkdownConcurrentTopicCreation(t *testing.T) {
1065: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
1066: 	defer store.Close()
1067: 	const numGoroutines = 20
1068: 	var wg sync.WaitGroup
1069: 	errs := make(chan error, numGoroutines)
1070: 	wg.Add(numGoroutines)
1071: 	for i := 0; i < numGoroutines; i++ {
1072: 		go func(idx int) {
1073: 			defer wg.Done()
1074: 			topic := models.NewTopic(fmt.Sprintf("topic-%d", idx), fmt.Sprintf("Description %d", idx), "test@cli")
1075: 			if err := store.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
1076: 				errs <- fmt.Errorf("goroutine %d: %w", idx, err)
1077: 			}
1078: 		}(i)
1079: 	}
1080: 	wg.Wait()
1081: 	close(errs)
1082: 	for err := range errs {
1083: 		t.Error(err)
1084: 	}
1085: 	// Verify all topics are readable
1086: 	topics, err := store.ListTopics(true)
1087: 	if err != nil {
1088: 		t.Fatalf("ListTopics failed: %v", err)
1089: 	}
1090: 	if len(topics) != numGoroutines {
1091: 		t.Errorf("expected %d topics, got %d", numGoroutines, len(topics))
1092: 	}
1093: 	// Verify each topic is individually retrievable
1094: 	for _, topic := range topics {
1095: 		got, err := store.GetTopic(topic.ID)
1096: 		if err != nil {
1097: 			t.Errorf("GetTopic(%s) failed: %v", topic.ID, err)
1098: 			continue
1099: 		}
1100: 		if got.Name != topic.Name {
1101: 			t.Errorf("topic name mismatch: want %q, got %q", topic.Name, got.Name)
1102: 		}
1103: 	}
1104: }
1105: func TestMarkdownConcurrentMessagePosting(t *testing.T) {
1106: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
1107: 	defer store.Close()
1108: 	topic := models.NewTopic("concurrent-topic", "Concurrency test", "test@cli")
1109: 	if err := store.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
1110: 		t.Fatalf("CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
1111: 	}
1112: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Concurrent Thread", "test@cli")
1113: 	if err := store.CreateThread(thread); err != nil {
1114: 		t.Fatalf("CreateThread failed: %v", err)
1115: 	}
1116: 	const numGoroutines = 15
1117: 	var wg sync.WaitGroup
1118: 	errs := make(chan error, numGoroutines)
1119: 	wg.Add(numGoroutines)
1120: 	for i := 0; i < numGoroutines; i++ {
1121: 		go func(idx int) {
1122: 			defer wg.Done()
1123: 			// Small stagger so messages get slightly different timestamps
1124: 			time.Sleep(time.Duration(idx) * time.Millisecond)
1125: 			msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, fmt.Sprintf("Concurrent message %d", idx), fmt.Sprintf("user%d@cli", idx))
1126: 			if err := store.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
1127: 				errs <- fmt.Errorf("goroutine %d: %w", idx, err)
1128: 			}
1129: 		}(i)
1130: 	}
1131: 	wg.Wait()
1132: 	close(errs)
1133: 	for err := range errs {
1134: 		t.Error(err)
1135: 	}
1136: 	// Verify all messages are readable
1137: 	messages, err := store.ListMessages(thread.ID)
1138: 	if err != nil {
1139: 		t.Fatalf("ListMessages failed: %v", err)
1140: 	}
1141: 	if len(messages) != numGoroutines {
1142: 		t.Errorf("expected %d messages, got %d", numGoroutines, len(messages))
1143: 	}
1144: 	// Verify each message is individually retrievable
1145: 	for _, msg := range messages {
1146: 		got, err := store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
1147: 		if err != nil {
1148: 			t.Errorf("GetMessage(%s) failed: %v", msg.ID, err)
1149: 			continue
1150: 		}
1151: 		if got.Content != msg.Content {
1152: 			t.Errorf("message content mismatch: want %q, got %q", msg.Content, got.Content)
1153: 		}
1154: 	}
1155: }
1156: func TestMarkdownConcurrentMixedOperations(t *testing.T) {
1157: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
1158: 	defer store.Close()
1159: 	// Seed some topics and threads
1160: 	topic := models.NewTopic("mixed-ops", "Mixed operations", "test@cli")
1161: 	if err := store.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
1162: 		t.Fatalf("CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
1163: 	}
1164: 	const numThreads = 10
1165: 	threadIDs := make([]uuid.UUID, numThreads)
1166: 	for i := 0; i < numThreads; i++ {
1167: 		thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, fmt.Sprintf("Thread %d", i), "test@cli")
1168: 		if err := store.CreateThread(thread); err != nil {
1169: 			t.Fatalf("CreateThread %d failed: %v", i, err)
1170: 		}
1171: 		threadIDs[i] = thread.ID
1172: 	}
1173: 	// Concurrently: post messages to different threads, read threads, list threads
1174: 	const numGoroutines = 20
1175: 	var wg sync.WaitGroup
1176: 	errs := make(chan error, numGoroutines*3)
1177: 	// Writers: post a message to each thread
1178: 	wg.Add(numGoroutines)
1179: 	for i := 0; i < numGoroutines; i++ {
1180: 		go func(idx int) {
1181: 			defer wg.Done()
1182: 			threadIdx := idx % numThreads
1183: 			msg := models.NewMessage(threadIDs[threadIdx], fmt.Sprintf("msg %d", idx), "test@cli")
1184: 			if err := store.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
1185: 				errs <- fmt.Errorf("write goroutine %d: %w", idx, err)
1186: 			}
1187: 		}(i)
1188: 	}
1189: 	// Readers: list threads and messages concurrently with writes
1190: 	wg.Add(numGoroutines)
1191: 	for i := 0; i < numGoroutines; i++ {
1192: 		go func(idx int) {
1193: 			defer wg.Done()
1194: 			_, err := store.ListThreads(topic.ID)
1195: 			if err != nil {
1196: 				errs <- fmt.Errorf("list threads goroutine %d: %w", idx, err)
1197: 			}
1198: 		}(i)
1199: 	}
1200: 	wg.Wait()
1201: 	close(errs)
1202: 	for err := range errs {
1203: 		t.Error(err)
1204: 	}
1205: 	// Verify all threads exist and all messages are accounted for
1206: 	threads, err := store.ListThreads(topic.ID)
1207: 	if err != nil {
1208: 		t.Fatalf("ListThreads failed: %v", err)
1209: 	}
1210: 	if len(threads) != numThreads {
1211: 		t.Errorf("expected %d threads, got %d", numThreads, len(threads))
1212: 	}
1213: 	totalMessages := 0
1214: 	for _, thread := range threads {
1215: 		msgs, err := store.ListMessages(thread.ID)
1216: 		if err != nil {
1217: 			t.Errorf("ListMessages(%s) failed: %v", thread.ID, err)
1218: 			continue
1219: 		}
1220: 		totalMessages += len(msgs)
1221: 	}
1222: 	if totalMessages != numGoroutines {
1223: 		t.Errorf("expected %d total messages, got %d", numGoroutines, totalMessages)
1224: 	}
1225: }

1227: func TestMarkdownThreadFilenameCollision(t *testing.T) {
1228: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
1229: 	defer store.Close()
1230: 	topic := models.NewTopic("collision-test", "Test filename collision", "test@cli")
1231: 	if err := store.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
1232: 		t.Fatalf("CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
1233: 	}
1234: 	// Create two threads whose subjects slugify identically
1235: 	thread1 := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Hello World!", "test@cli")
1236: 	if err := store.CreateThread(thread1); err != nil {
1237: 		t.Fatalf("CreateThread (1) failed: %v", err)
1238: 	}
1239: 	thread2 := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Hello World?", "test@cli")
1240: 	if err := store.CreateThread(thread2); err != nil {
1241: 		t.Fatalf("CreateThread (2) failed: %v", err)
1242: 	}
1243: 	// Both threads should be independently retrievable
1244: 	got1, err := store.GetThread(thread1.ID)
1245: 	if err != nil {
1246: 		t.Fatalf("GetThread (1) failed: %v", err)
1247: 	}
1248: 	if got1.Subject != "Hello World!" {
1249: 		t.Errorf("thread 1 subject mismatch: want %q, got %q", "Hello World!", got1.Subject)
1250: 	}
1251: 	got2, err := store.GetThread(thread2.ID)
1252: 	if err != nil {
1253: 		t.Fatalf("GetThread (2) failed: %v", err)
1254: 	}
1255: 	if got2.Subject != "Hello World?" {
1256: 		t.Errorf("thread 2 subject mismatch: want %q, got %q", "Hello World?", got2.Subject)
1257: 	}
1258: 	// ListThreads should return both
1259: 	threads, err := store.ListThreads(topic.ID)
1260: 	if err != nil {
1261: 		t.Fatalf("ListThreads failed: %v", err)
1262: 	}
1263: 	if len(threads) != 2 {
1264: 		t.Errorf("expected 2 threads, got %d", len(threads))
1265: 	}
1266: 	// Post messages to both and verify they don't interfere
1267: 	msg1 := models.NewMessage(thread1.ID, "Message in thread 1", "test@cli")
1268: 	msg2 := models.NewMessage(thread2.ID, "Message in thread 2", "test@cli")
1269: 	if err := store.CreateMessage(msg1); err != nil {
1270: 		t.Fatalf("CreateMessage (1) failed: %v", err)
1271: 	}
1272: 	if err := store.CreateMessage(msg2); err != nil {
1273: 		t.Fatalf("CreateMessage (2) failed: %v", err)
1274: 	}
1275: 	msgs1, _ := store.ListMessages(thread1.ID)
1276: 	msgs2, _ := store.ListMessages(thread2.ID)
1277: 	if len(msgs1) != 1 || msgs1[0].Content != "Message in thread 1" {
1278: 		t.Errorf("thread 1 messages wrong: got %v", msgs1)
1279: 	}
1280: 	if len(msgs2) != 1 || msgs2[0].Content != "Message in thread 2" {
1281: 		t.Errorf("thread 2 messages wrong: got %v", msgs2)
1282: 	}
1283: }
1284: func TestMarkdownMessageContainingMarkdownSeparator(t *testing.T) {
1285: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
1286: 	defer store.Close()
1287: 	topic := models.NewTopic("separator-test", "Test markdown separators", "test@cli")
1288: 	if err := store.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
1289: 		t.Fatalf("CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
1290: 	}
1291: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Separator Test", "test@cli")
1292: 	if err := store.CreateThread(thread); err != nil {
1293: 		t.Fatalf("CreateThread failed: %v", err)
1294: 	}
1295: 	// Post a message containing "---" (the separator used between messages)
1296: 	contentWithSeparator := "Here is some content\n---\nThis looks like a separator\n---\nBut it's all one message"
1297: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, contentWithSeparator, "test@cli")
1298: 	if err := store.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
1299: 		t.Fatalf("CreateMessage failed: %v", err)
1300: 	}
1301: 	// Post a second message after it
1302: 	msg2 := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, "Second message", "test@cli")
1303: 	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond) // ensure distinct timestamp
1304: 	if err := store.CreateMessage(msg2); err != nil {
1305: 		t.Fatalf("CreateMessage (2) failed: %v", err)
1306: 	}
1307: 	// Retrieve and verify both messages
1308: 	messages, err := store.ListMessages(thread.ID)
1309: 	if err != nil {
1310: 		t.Fatalf("ListMessages failed: %v", err)
1311: 	}
1312: 	// The HTML comment separator ensures "---" in content does not corrupt parsing.
1313: 	if len(messages) != 2 {
1314: 		t.Errorf("expected exactly 2 messages, got %d", len(messages))
1315: 	}
1316: 	// Verify the first message content is fully preserved including "---"
1317: 	if messages[0].Content != contentWithSeparator {
1318: 		t.Errorf("first message content was corrupted:\nwant: %q\ngot:  %q", contentWithSeparator, messages[0].Content)
1319: 	}
1320: 	// Verify the second message is intact
1321: 	if messages[1].Content != "Second message" {
1322: 		t.Errorf("expected second message content 'Second message', got %q", messages[1].Content)
1323: 	}
1324: }

1377: 			continue
1378: 		}

1383: 			continue

1386: 			t.Errorf("topic name mismatch: want %q, got %q", tt.name, got.Name)
1387: 		}

1389: 		gotByName, err := store.GetTopicByName(tt.name)
1390: 		if err != nil {

1396: 		}
1397: 		// Verify directory was created

1399: 		if _, err := os.Stat(topicDir); os.IsNotExist(err) {
1400: 			t.Errorf("topic directory not created for %q", tt.name)
1401: 		}
1402: 	}
1403: 	// Verify all topics are listed
1404: 	topics, err := store.ListTopics(true)
1405: 	if err != nil {
1406: 		t.Fatalf("ListTopics failed: %v", err)
1407: 	}
1408: 	if len(topics) != len(tests) {
1409: 		t.Errorf("expected %d topics, got %d", len(tests), len(topics))
1410: 	}
1411: }

1464: func TestMarkdownThreadWithLongSubject(t *testing.T) {
1465: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
1466: 	defer store.Close()
1467: 	topic := models.NewTopic("long-subject", "Test long subjects", "test@cli")
1468: 	if err := store.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
1469: 		t.Fatalf("CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
1470: 	}
1471: 	// A moderately long subject (under filesystem limits) should work fine
1472: 	moderateSubject := strings.Repeat("long ", 30)
1473: 	thread1 := models.NewThread(topic.ID, moderateSubject, "test@cli")
1474: 	if err := store.CreateThread(thread1); err != nil {
1475: 		t.Fatalf("CreateThread with moderate subject failed: %v", err)
1476: 	}
1477: 	got, err := store.GetThread(thread1.ID)
1478: 	if err != nil {

1480: 	}
1481: 	if got.Subject != moderateSubject {
1482: 		t.Errorf("subject was altered: got %d chars, want %d chars", len(got.Subject), len(moderateSubject))
1483: 	}
1484: 	// Verify messages can be posted and retrieved on the long-subject thread
1485: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread1.ID, "Message in long-subject thread", "test@cli")
1486: 	if err := store.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
1487: 		t.Fatalf("CreateMessage failed: %v", err)
1488: 	}
1489: 	messages, err := store.ListMessages(thread1.ID)
1490: 	if err != nil {
1491: 		t.Fatalf("ListMessages failed: %v", err)
1492: 	}
1493: 	if len(messages) != 1 {
1494: 		t.Errorf("expected 1 message, got %d", len(messages))
1495: 	}
1496: 	// An extremely long subject (300+ chars) that exceeds filesystem filename limits
1497: 	// should return an error rather than panic
1498: 	extremeSubject := strings.Repeat("This is a very long subject ", 15)
1499: 	thread2 := models.NewThread(topic.ID, extremeSubject, "test@cli")
1500: 	err = store.CreateThread(thread2)
1501: 	if err == nil {
1502: 		// If it succeeds (e.g. on a filesystem with large name limits), that's ok too.
1503: 		// Verify the data round-trips.
1504: 		got2, getErr := store.GetThread(thread2.ID)
1505: 		if getErr != nil {
1506: 			t.Fatalf("GetThread (extreme) failed: %v", getErr)
1507: 		}
1508: 		if got2.Subject != extremeSubject {
1509: 			t.Errorf("extreme subject was altered")
1510: 		}
1511: 	}
1512: 	// If err != nil, that's the expected graceful failure for long filenames
1513: }
1514: func TestMarkdownMessageWithMultilineContent(t *testing.T) {
1515: 	store := newTestMarkdownStore(t)
1516: 	defer store.Close()
1517: 	topic := models.NewTopic("multiline", "Test multiline content", "test@cli")
1518: 	if err := store.CreateTopic(topic); err != nil {
1519: 		t.Fatalf("CreateTopic failed: %v", err)
1520: 	}
1521: 	thread := models.NewThread(topic.ID, "Multiline Test", "test@cli")
1522: 	if err := store.CreateThread(thread); err != nil {
1523: 		t.Fatalf("CreateThread failed: %v", err)
1524: 	}
1525: 	// Message with various markdown formatting
1526: 	content := "# Heading\n\nSome paragraph.\n\n```go\nfunc main() {\n\tprintln(\"hello\")\n}\n```\n\n- list item 1\n- list item 2\n\n> Blockquote here"
1527: 	msg := models.NewMessage(thread.ID, content, "test@cli")
1528: 	if err := store.CreateMessage(msg); err != nil {
1529: 		t.Fatalf("CreateMessage failed: %v", err)
1530: 	}
1531: 	got, err := store.GetMessage(msg.ID)
1532: 	if err != nil {
1533: 		t.Fatalf("GetMessage failed: %v", err)
1534: 	}
1535: 	if got.Content != content {
1536: 		t.Errorf("multiline content mismatch:\nwant: %q\ngot:  %q", content, got.Content)
1537: 	}
1538: }

<file path="internal/config/config.go">
  1: // ABOUTME: BBS configuration management with backend selection
  2: // ABOUTME: Handles settings, preferences, and storage backend factory
  3: package config
  4: import (
  5: 	"encoding/json"
  6: 	"fmt"
  7: 	"os"
  8: 	"path/filepath"
  9: 	"strings"
 10: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/storage"
 11: )
 12: // Config stores BBS configuration.
 13: type Config struct {
 14: 	// Backend selects the storage backend: "sqlite" (default) or "markdown".
 15: 	Backend string `json:"backend,omitempty"`
 16: 	// DataDir is the root directory for data storage.
 17: 	// SQLite puts bbs.db here. Markdown puts _topics.yaml and topic folders here.
 18: 	// Supports ~ expansion for home directory. Defaults to ~/.local/share/bbs.
 19: 	DataDir string `json:"data_dir,omitempty"`
 20: }
 21: // GetBackend returns the configured backend, defaulting to "sqlite".
 22: func (c *Config) GetBackend() string {
 23: 	if c.Backend == "" {
 24: 		return "sqlite"
 25: 	}
 26: 	return c.Backend
 27: }
 28: // GetDataDir returns the configured data directory with ~ expanded,
 29: // defaulting to the standard XDG data directory.
 30: func (c *Config) GetDataDir() string {
 31: 	if c.DataDir == "" {
 32: 		return storage.DataDir()
 33: 	}
 34: 	return ExpandPath(c.DataDir)
 35: }
 36: // ExpandPath expands a leading ~ to the user's home directory.
 37: func ExpandPath(path string) string {
 38: 	if path == "" {
 39: 		return ""
 40: 	}
 41: 	if path == "~" {
 42: 		home, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
 43: 		return home
 44: 	}
 45: 	if strings.HasPrefix(path, "~/") {
 46: 		home, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
 47: 		return filepath.Join(home, path[2:])
 48: 	}
 49: 	return path
 50: }
 51: // OpenStorage creates a Storage implementation based on the configured backend.
 52: func (c *Config) OpenStorage() (storage.Storage, error) {
 53: 	backend := c.GetBackend()
 54: 	dataDir := c.GetDataDir()
 55: 	switch backend {
 56: 	case "sqlite":
 57: 		dbPath := filepath.Join(dataDir, "bbs.db")
 58: 		return storage.NewSqliteStore(dbPath)
 59: 	case "markdown":
 60: 		return storage.NewMarkdownStore(dataDir)
 61: 	default:
 62: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown backend: %q", backend)
 63: 	}
 64: }
 65: // GetConfigPath returns the config file path.
 66: func GetConfigPath() string {
 67: 	configDir := os.Getenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME")
 68: 	if configDir == "" {
 69: 		homeDir, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
 70: 		configDir = filepath.Join(homeDir, ".config")
 71: 	}
 72: 	return filepath.Join(configDir, "bbs", "config.json")
 73: }
 74: // Load reads config from disk.
 75: func Load() (*Config, error) {
 76: 	path := GetConfigPath()
 77: 	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
 78: 	if err != nil {
 79: 		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
 80: 			return &Config{}, nil
 81: 		}
 82: 		return nil, err
 83: 	}
 84: 	var cfg Config
 85: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &cfg); err != nil {
 86: 		return nil, err
 87: 	}
 88: 	return &cfg, nil
 89: }
 90: // Save writes config to disk.
 91: func (c *Config) Save() error {
 92: 	path := GetConfigPath()
 93: 	dir := filepath.Dir(path)
 94: 	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0750); err != nil {
 95: 		return err
 96: 	}
 97: 	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(c, "", "  ")
 98: 	if err != nil {
 99: 		return err
100: 	}
101: 	return os.WriteFile(path, data, 0600)
102: }
</file>

235: func TestDataDirTildeOnlyExpansion(t *testing.T) {

239: 	}

246: func TestExpandPath(t *testing.T) {
247: 	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
248: 	if err != nil {
249: 		t.Skipf("cannot get home dir: %v", err)
250: 	}
251: 	tests := []struct {
252: 		input    string
253: 		expected string
254: 	}{
255: 		{"~/foo", filepath.Join(home, "foo")},
256: 		{"~", home},
257: 		{"/absolute/path", "/absolute/path"},
258: 		{"relative/path", "relative/path"},
259: 		{"", ""},
260: 	}
261: 	for _, tt := range tests {
262: 		result := ExpandPath(tt.input)
263: 		if result != tt.expected {
264: 			t.Errorf("ExpandPath(%q) = %q, want %q", tt.input, result, tt.expected)
265: 		}
266: 	}
267: }
268: func TestSaveAndLoadWithBackendFields(t *testing.T) {
269: 	tmpDir := t.TempDir()

280: 		t.Fatalf("Load failed: %v", err)

284: 	}

287: 	}
288: }
289: func TestSaveAndLoadPreservesJSON(t *testing.T) {
290: 	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
291: 	t.Setenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", tmpDir)
292: 	cfg := &Config{
293: 		Backend: "sqlite",
294: 		DataDir: "~/my-data",
295: 	}
296: 	if err := cfg.Save(); err != nil {
297: 		t.Fatalf("Save failed: %v", err)
298: 	}
299: 	// Read raw JSON and verify field names
300: 	path := GetConfigPath()
301: 	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
302: 	if err != nil {
303: 		t.Fatalf("read config file: %v", err)
304: 	}
305: 	var raw map[string]interface{}
306: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
307: 		t.Fatalf("unmarshal raw JSON: %v", err)
308: 	}
309: 	if raw["backend"] != "sqlite" {
310: 		t.Errorf("expected JSON key 'backend' with value 'sqlite', got %v", raw["backend"])
311: 	}
312: 	if raw["data_dir"] != "~/my-data" {
313: 		t.Errorf("expected JSON key 'data_dir' with value '~/my-data', got %v", raw["data_dir"])
314: 	}
315: }

<file path="cmd/bbs/root.go">
 1: // ABOUTME: Root Cobra command and global flags
 2: // ABOUTME: Sets up CLI structure and config-driven storage connection
 3: package main
 4: import (
 5: 	"fmt"
 6: 	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
 7: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/config"
 8: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/identity"
 9: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/storage"
10: 	"github.com/harper/bbs/internal/tui"
11: )
12: var identityFlag string
13: // globalStore holds the database connection for CLI commands
14: var globalStore storage.Storage
15: var rootCmd = &cobra.Command{
16: 	Use:   "bbs",
17: 	Short: "A lightweight message board for humans and agents",
18: 	Long: `
19: ██████╗ ██████╗ ███████╗
20: ██╔══██╗██╔══██╗██╔════╝
21: ██████╔╝██████╔╝███████╗
22: ██╔══██╗██╔══██╗╚════██║
23: ██████╔╝██████╔╝███████║
24: ╚═════╝ ╚═════╝ ╚══════╝
25:    THUNDERBOARD 3000
26: A message board for humans and agents to communicate.
27: Topics → Threads → Messages
28: Data is stored locally.`,
29: 	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
30: 		// Launch TUI if no subcommand
31: 		cfg, err := config.Load()
32: 		if err != nil {
33: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to load config: %w", err)
34: 		}
35: 		store, err := cfg.OpenStorage()
36: 		if err != nil {
37: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to open storage: %w", err)
38: 		}
39: 		defer store.Close()
40: 		return tui.Run(store, identity.GetIdentity(identityFlag, "tui"))
41: 	},
42: 	PersistentPreRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
43: 		// Skip init for help commands
44: 		if cmd.Name() == "help" || cmd.Name() == "version" {
45: 			return nil
46: 		}
47: 		// Load config and initialize global store for subcommands
48: 		cfg, err := config.Load()
49: 		if err != nil {
50: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to load config: %w", err)
51: 		}
52: 		store, err := cfg.OpenStorage()
53: 		if err != nil {
54: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to open storage: %w", err)
55: 		}
56: 		globalStore = store
57: 		return nil
58: 	},
59: 	PersistentPostRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
60: 		if globalStore != nil {
61: 			globalStore.Close()
62: 			globalStore = nil
63: 		}
64: 		return nil
65: 	},
66: }
67: func init() {
68: 	rootCmd.PersistentFlags().StringVar(&identityFlag, "as", "", "identity override (username)")
69: }
</file>
